# Kirchhoff P3 — Web UI (WS3) Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** The complete Kirchhoff web app in `apps/web`: landing, projects dashboard, a workspace that renders every beat of the 2:40 storyboard (agent lanes, SLD, 3D hall, compliance matrix, finding drawer with PDF citations, options, gates, revisions and diff, what-if, voice co-pilot), review/sign-off, integrations, asset-pack screens and the demo-world onboarding — all rendered from one `ProjectBundle` format that loads from static files, live Firestore, or a timed replay.

**Architecture:** React 19 SPA (Vite 8) on Firebase Hosting. One zustand store holds a normalised `Bundle` per project view, fed by three adapters: `staticBundle(pid)` (`/demo/projects/{pid}.json`), `subscribeLiveBundle(uid, runId)` (read-only Firestore `onSnapshot` over the visitor workspace) and `createReplay(bundle)` (static events on a timer). A memoised `WorkspaceView` (selected revision, statuses, diff, what-if overlay) feeds every canvas through React context. All network access is injected through a `Services` context (`ApiClient`, JSON fetch, Firestore source, Gemini Live starter), so tests never touch Firebase.

**Tech Stack:** Vite 8.3.4, React 19.3, TypeScript 5.9.3, Tailwind 4.3.3 + shadcn/ui (registry `new-york-v4`, `radix-ui`, `cn`), Motion 14 (`motion/react`), zustand 5, react-router 8, @xyflow/react 12 + elkjs 0.12, three 0.186 + @react-three/fiber 9 + drei 10, react-pdf 11 (pdf.js 6.3), @tanstack/react-table 9, Firebase JS SDK 13 (Auth, Firestore, App Check, AI Logic), vitest 5 + Testing Library (jsdom).

**Spec:** `docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-13-kirchhoff-design.md` (v4.1) — read §3, §6.3–6.5, §11, §12, §13, §14 first. **Interfaces (binding):** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-13-interfaces.md`. **Foundation:** `docs/superpowers/plans/2026-10-13-p0-foundation.md` Tasks 2 and 4.

## Global Constraints

- GCP project `ordinal-mep`; Hosting site `kirchhoff` (https://kirchhoff.web.app). Never run `gcloud config set project`.
- Fresh code only, written during the hackathon; open-source libraries are fine. Do not copy code from other repositories.
- Git: use the machine's global identity (no `-c user.*`). End every commit message with `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- Do not spawn subagents from inside a task.
- WS3 owns `apps/web/**` only. Never edit files outside it.
- Contracts come only from `src/contracts/generated.ts` (regenerate with `make gen-types`; never hand-edit it). Import contract types through `src/lib/types.ts`.
- Clients never write Firestore. Every mutation goes through `/api` (`ApiClient`). Firestore reads are limited to `workspaces/{uid}/**` of the signed-in visitor.
- Every number displayed comes from bundle/API data (engine records). The UI only formats (rounding, grouping, units). Counting records is allowed (spec §3 "counted live", §6.3 "N/M quotes verified", §13.5 "{n} counted from the bundle"); arithmetic on engineering values is not.
- Required labels, verbatim: "Demo workspace — all companies, people and data are fictional" (banner on every `/app` page), "sandbox (simulated)" (ERP/CRM/notifications), "IEC-based, illustrative values, not for construction" (matrix, drawer, SLD/3D legend, options, review, docs).
- Browsing never depends on the backend: seed projects, replay, integrations fallback, tour and checklist work with Cloud Run down (spec §11, §13.4).
- Status is always icon + text, never colour alone; WCAG AA contrast on `#0b0d10`; every control keyboard-reachable; motion only for progress, and `prefers-reduced-motion` disables it (CSS media query + `<MotionConfig reducedMotion="user">` + `useReducedMotion` in canvases).
- Agent accents (spec §6.1): Intake slate, Orchestrator white, Layout cyan, Power amber, Cooling blue, Procurement green, Checker red, Docs violet, Co-pilot gradient, Asset teal.
- Every `/api` call is aborted client-side after 12 s (server budget < 10 s).
- Tests: vitest + Testing Library only (Playwright e2e belongs to WS4). Run from `apps/web`: `npx vitest run <path>`; full suite `npm test`; type-check `npx tsc -b`.

## Rulings on spec ambiguities (binding for this plan)

| # | Question | Ruling |
|---|---|---|
| R1 | Spec §6.5 names `VertexAIBackend('us-central1')` | Firebase JS SDK 13.0.0 renamed it: `new AgentPlatformBackend('us-central1')` (verified in `node_modules/@firebase/ai/dist/ai-public.d.ts`; `VertexAIBackend` no longer exists). Same Vertex backend and region. |
| R2 | §14 lists camelCase Firestore fields; contracts are snake_case | Firestore docs are `model_dump()`s of the contracts (snake_case, as P0's hello-run writes). The lease heartbeat is read as `heartbeat_at ?? heartbeatAt`. All timestamps are unix seconds (float). |
| R3 | "Every number from the engine" vs counters/clocks/bars | Counting records and timestamp differences (elapsed clocks) are allowed; value-vs-limit bar geometry is visual only and its labels show the record's `value` and `limit`. No totals, deltas or percentages are computed in the UI. |
| R4 | Library majors newer than the brief | Pinned to current: TanStack Table **9.3.1** (v9 `useTable`/`tableFeatures`/`table.FlexRender`, verified), react-router **8.4.0** (declarative `BrowserRouter`/`Routes`; all from `react-router`), react-pdf **11.0.0** (`suspense={false}`), lucide-react **1.56.0** (v1 names: `CircleCheck`, `CircleX`, `TriangleAlert`, `LoaderCircle`). TypeScript stays **5.9.3** (P0's choice; TS 7's native compiler is not adopted mid-hackathon). shadcn 4.21.4's `new-york-v4` registry imports `cn` from the `cn` package — keep it. |
| R5 | Where static asset-pack results live (ProjectBundle has no slot) | The grid/solar static bundles carry the result dict as the last `tool_result` event with `payload.tool ∈ {"evaluate_grid","evaluate_solar"}` and `payload.summary = {options, recommended, findings, geometry, sld?}`. "Re-run" calls `POST /api/asset/{pack}`. |
| R6 | Tender PDF for citations with the backend down | Served from Hosting at `/demo/docs/{doc_id}.pdf`. `scripts/sync-demo-docs.mjs` (predev/prebuild) copies `scenarios/hall_a/out/*.pdf`; `Citation.doc_id` must equal the file stem (e.g. `tender-hall-a`). |
| R7 | What `artifact.payload.ref` points at | A Firestore path under `workspaces/{uid}/` (read once with `getDoc`) or a URL/storage path (fetched; storage paths via `GET /api/files/{path}`). Revision artifacts' ref tail is the revision id. Exports: public URL for seeds, storage path for live runs. |
| R8 | Which revision a `finding` event belongs to | Payload may carry optional `rev`; without it the finding belongs to the latest revision artifact played before it. |
| R9 | `/api/voice` request/response details | Multipart fields `audio` (webm/ogg) and `revision_ref`; response `tool_results: [{name, args, result}]` with `name ∈ {what_if, explain_finding, focus_element}`. |
| R10 | Visitor stamp text | The review-gate API writes `approver_name: "You (Demo Reviewer)"`, `role: "Reviewer"`; the UI renders whatever the Approval record says. |
| R11 | Ask/Auto switch semantics | Autonomy is chosen at `POST /api/runs`; during/after a run the switch shows `run.autonomy` read-only. |
| R12 | §17 puts "Integrations page" in WS4 | The page UI is WS3 (`apps/web/**` ownership is binding); WS4 provides `/api/enterprise/*` and `integrations.json`. |
| R13 | Shapes of `world.json` and `integrations.json` | Defined in `src/lib/types.ts` (`World`, `IntegrationsSnapshot`); WS4 generates exactly these. |
| R14 | "Drop tender PDF and drawing" (storyboard 0:10) with no upload API | The Hall B start panel's drop zone starts the run with the fixed Hall B package; nothing is uploaded and the panel says so. |
| R15 | SLD rack groups and 3D row busways absent from the model | Rack groups are synthesised per row from `racks[].row/feeds` when no `rack_group` equipment exists; row-busway tubes are synthesised from rack positions with the spec's ±0.3 m offsets. Geometry only. |
| R16 | "Ask the co-pilot (voice or text)" with no text endpoint | Typed input goes through a deterministic intent parser onto the same three tools (`what_if`, `explain_finding`, `focus_element`). |
| R17 | Revision refs | Live: `ws:{rev}`; static/replay: `seed:{pid}@{rev}`. What-if chips use the bundle's precomputed `what_ifs` first, then `/api/what-if`. |
| R18 | Visual reference archilabs.ai (light, spacious marketing site) | Spec §12 (dark, dense, Linear/Vercel-like) stays binding for the product. From ArchiLabs we take only landing clarity: product-first copy, a dual CTA, and a three-step "how it works" strip. |

```
    matrix/   matrixRows.ts, ComplianceMatrix.tsx
    finding/  bbox.ts, ValueLimitBar.tsx, FindingDrawer.tsx, PdfViewer.tsx (lazy)
```

### Task 1: Toolchain, design tokens and contract types (Tue)

Pins every library, wires Tailwind v4 + shadcn/ui, splits Vite and Vitest config with `mergeConfig`, removes P0 template leftovers, and lands the shared vocabulary every later task uses (contract re-exports, formatting, status/agent metadata, labels).

**Files:**
- Modify: `apps/web/package.json` (deps), `apps/web/vite.config.ts`, `apps/web/vitest.config.ts`, `apps/web/tsconfig.json`, `apps/web/tsconfig.app.json`, `apps/web/tsconfig.node.json`, `apps/web/src/test-setup.ts`, `apps/web/src/index.css`, `apps/web/.gitignore`
- Delete: `apps/web/src/App.css`, `apps/web/src/assets/react.svg`
- Create: `apps/web/components.json`, `apps/web/src/components/ui/*` (shadcn CLI), `apps/web/src/lib/types.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/format.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/status.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/agents.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/storage.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/StatusBadge.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/Labels.tsx`
- Test: `apps/web/src/lib/format.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/components/StatusBadge.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: P0 `src/contracts/generated.ts` (interfaces `ProjectBundle`, `Project`, `RunRecord`, `Revision`, `DesignModel`, `Finding`, `Citation`, …, typed tuples), P0 `src/firebase.ts`, `src/api.ts` (unchanged).
- Produces:
  - `@/lib/types`: re-exports every contract interface; aliases `ProjectState, AssetPack, FindingStatus, AgentName, EventKind, Phase, EquipmentKind, TopologyId, WhatIfParam, Autonomy`; `type Bundle = Required<ProjectBundle>`; `normalizeBundle(b: ProjectBundle): Bundle`; UI shapes `World, WorldPerson, WorldActivity, IntegrationsSnapshot, DocumentRecord, NotificationRecord, AssetResult`.
  - `@/lib/format`: `fmtNum, fmtQty(v, unit), fmtUsd(v, signed?), fmtTs(unixSeconds), fmtRelative(ts, nowMs?), fmtElapsed(seconds), shortHash, fillPlaceholders(text, {value, limit, unit}), describeKey(key), fmtKeyValue(key, value), DASH`.
  - `@/lib/status`: `STATUS_META: Record<FindingStatus, {label, icon, text, bg, border, hex}>`, `worstStatus(iterable)`, `statusRank(s)`, `RULES: {id, name}[]` (R01–R15 + R04b), `ruleName(id)`.
  - `@/lib/agents`: `AGENTS: Record<AgentName, {label, color, text}>`, `LANE_ORDER`, `CHAIN_COLORS = {electrical, cooling}`.
  - `@/lib/storage`: `PREFIX = 'kirchhoff.'`, `readJson(key, fallback, store?)`, `writeJson(key, value, store?)`, `clearKirchhoffStorage()`.
  - `@/components/StatusBadge`: `<StatusBadge status compact? className? />`.
  - `@/components/Labels`: `FICTIONAL_TEXT, SANDBOX_TEXT, ILLUSTRATIVE_TEXT`, `<DemoBanner/>`, `<SandboxLabel/>`, `<IllustrativeLabel/>`, `<AgentDot agent/>`, `<Num>`, `<EmptyState title action?>`.
  - shadcn components in `@/components/ui/`: `button badge popover sheet tabs tooltip switch toggle-group toggle scroll-area separator dialog dropdown-menu input textarea radio-group`.

- [ ] **Step 3: Vite and Vitest config (plugins and alias defined once)**

`apps/web/vite.config.ts`:
```ts
import path from 'node:path'
import tailwindcss from '@tailwindcss/vite'
import react from '@vitejs/plugin-react'
import { defineConfig } from 'vite'

export default defineConfig({
  plugins: [react(), tailwindcss()],
  resolve: { alias: { '@': path.resolve(import.meta.dirname, './src') } },
  server: { proxy: { '/api': 'http://localhost:8080' } },
})
```

`apps/web/vitest.config.ts`:
```ts
import { defineConfig, mergeConfig } from 'vitest/config'
import viteConfig from './vite.config.ts'

export default mergeConfig(
  viteConfig,
  defineConfig({
    test: {
      environment: 'jsdom',
      globals: true,
      setupFiles: ['./src/test-setup.ts'],
      css: false,
      restoreMocks: true,
    },
  }),
)
```

`apps/web/tsconfig.json`:
```json
{

      "path": "./tsconfig.node.json"
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Global CSS (dark tokens, presentation mode, reduced motion) and test setup**

`apps/web/src/index.css` (replace the one-liner):
```css
@import "tailwindcss";
@import "tw-animate-css";

@custom-variant dark (&:is(.dark *));

/* Dark-only, dense, Linear/Vercel-like (spec §12). Contrast of text tokens on --background ≥ 4.5:1. */
:root {
  --radius: 0.5rem;
  --background: #0b0d10;
  --foreground: #e4e4e7;
  --card: #111418;
  --card-foreground: #e4e4e7;
  --popover: #14181d;
  --popover-foreground: #e4e4e7;
  --primary: #e4e4e7;
  --primary-foreground: #0b0d10;
  --secondary: #1c2127;
  --secondary-foreground: #e4e4e7;
  --muted: #1c2127;
  --muted-foreground: #a1a1aa;
  --accent: #222831;
  --accent-foreground: #f4f4f5;
  --destructive: #e11d48;
  --border: #262c34;
  --input: #2a313a;
  --ring: #60a5fa;
}

@theme inline {
  --font-sans: "Inter Variable", ui-sans-serif, system-ui, sans-serif;
  --font-mono: "JetBrains Mono Variable", ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, monospace;
  --radius-sm: calc(var(--radius) - 4px);
  --radius-md: calc(var(--radius) - 2px);
  --radius-lg: var(--radius);
  --radius-xl: calc(var(--radius) + 4px);
  --color-background: var(--background);
  --color-foreground: var(--foreground);
  --color-card: var(--card);
  --color-card-foreground: var(--card-foreground);
  --color-popover: var(--popover);
  --color-popover-foreground: var(--popover-foreground);
  --color-primary: var(--primary);
  --color-primary-foreground: var(--primary-foreground);
  --color-secondary: var(--secondary);
  --color-secondary-foreground: var(--secondary-foreground);
  --color-muted: var(--muted);
  --color-muted-foreground: var(--muted-foreground);
  --color-accent: var(--accent);
  --color-accent-foreground: var(--accent-foreground);
  --color-destructive: var(--destructive);
  --color-border: var(--border);
  --color-input: var(--input);
  --color-ring: var(--ring);
}

@layer base {
  * {
    @apply border-border outline-ring/50;
  }
  html {
    font-size: 14px;
    color-scheme: dark;
  }
  body {
    @apply bg-background text-foreground font-sans antialiased;
    font-feature-settings: "cv11", "ss01";
  }
  :focus-visible {
    outline: 2px solid var(--ring);
    outline-offset: 2px;
  }
}

/* Presentation mode (?present=1): larger type, no dev affordances. */
html[data-present="1"] {
  font-size: 17px;
}
html[data-present="1"] [data-dev] {
  display: none !important;
}

/* Motion only for progress; honour the OS setting everywhere (Motion also uses MotionConfig reducedMotion="user"). */
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  *,
  *::before,
  *::after {
    animation-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    animation-iteration-count: 1 !important;
    transition-duration: 0.01ms !important;
    scroll-behavior: auto !important;
  }
}

/* Spotlight-tour cut-out. */
.tour-spotlight {
  position: fixed;
  z-index: 60;
  border-radius: 8px;
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 9999px rgb(0 0 0 / 0.62);
  outline: 2px solid #60a5fa;
  pointer-events: none;
  transition: all 200ms ease;
}

/* React Flow dark tweaks. */
```

```ts
// Single import point for contract types. Contracts come only from the generated file
// (never hand-edit src/contracts/generated.ts); this module re-exports them and adds the
// UI-only shapes of the static demo files.
import type {
  Equipment,
  Finding,
  Material,
  Opportunity,
  Project,
  ProjectBundle,
  PurchaseRequisition,
  RunEvent,
  RunRecord,
  TopologyOption,
  WhatIfResult,
} from '@/contracts/generated'

export type {
  Approval,
  Citation,
  ClauseRef,
  Connection,
  CoolingNode,
  CrmOverlay,
  DesignBrief,
  DesignModel,
  Equipment,
  Finding,
  Geometry,
  LedgerEntry,
  Material,
  OpenQuestion,
  Opportunity,
  Pipe,
  PlanStep,
  Point3,
  PRLine,
  Project,
  ProjectBundle,
  PurchaseRequisition,
  Rack,
  Requirement,
  Revision,
  RunEvent,
  RunRecord,
  StepRecord,
  TopologyOption,
  WhatIfResult,
} from '@/contracts/generated'

export type ProjectState = Project['state']
export type AssetPack = Project['asset_pack']
export type FindingStatus = Finding['status']
export type AgentName = RunEvent['agent']
export type EventKind = RunEvent['kind']
export type Phase = RunEvent['phase']
export type EquipmentKind = Equipment['kind']
export type TopologyId = TopologyOption['id']
export type WhatIfParam = WhatIfResult['param']
export type Autonomy = RunRecord['autonomy']

/** A ProjectBundle with every optional collection filled in (see normalizeBundle). */
export type Bundle = Required<ProjectBundle>

export function normalizeBundle(b: ProjectBundle): Bundle {
  return {
    project: b.project,
    brief: b.brief ?? null,
    runs: b.runs ?? [],
    steps: b.steps ?? [],
    events: [...(b.events ?? [])].sort((x, y) => x.seq - y.seq),
    revisions: [...(b.revisions ?? [])].sort((x, y) => x.created_at - y.created_at),
    findings: b.findings ?? {},
    approvals: b.approvals ?? [],
    prs: b.prs ?? [],
    crm: b.crm ?? null,
    what_ifs: b.what_ifs ?? [],
  }
}

}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/format.ts`:
```ts
// Formatting only. Nothing here computes an engineering value: every number shown in the UI
// comes from bundle/API data and passes through these functions unchanged except for
// rounding, grouping and units (Global Constraints).
import type { Finding } from './types'

const NUM = new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', { maximumFractionDigits: 2 })
const USD = new Intl.NumberFormat('en-US', { style: 'currency', currency: 'USD', maximumFractionDigits: 0 })
const DATE = new Intl.DateTimeFormat('en-GB', {
  day: 'numeric',
  month: 'short',
  year: 'numeric',
  hour: '2-digit',
  minute: '2-digit',
  timeZone: 'Asia/Kolkata',
})
const REL = new Intl.RelativeTimeFormat('en', { numeric: 'auto' })

export const DASH = '—'

export function fmtNum(v: number | null | undefined): string {
  return v === null || v === undefined || Number.isNaN(v) ? DASH : NUM.format(v)
}

export function fmtQty(v: number | null | undefined, unit?: string | null): string {
  if (v === null || v === undefined) return DASH
  if (!unit) return fmtNum(v)
  return unit === '%' ? `${fmtNum(v)}%` : `${fmtNum(v)} ${unit}`
}

export function fmtUsd(v: number | null | undefined, signed = false): string {
  if (v === null || v === undefined) return DASH
  const s = USD.format(v)
  return signed && v > 0 ? `+${s}` : s
}

/** `ts` is unix seconds (Python `time.time()`), rendered in IST. */
export function fmtTs(ts: number | null | undefined): string {
  return ts === null || ts === undefined ? DASH : DATE.format(new Date(ts * 1000))
}

export function fmtRelative(ts: number, nowMs: number = Date.now()): string {
  const diffS = ts - nowMs / 1000
  const abs = Math.abs(diffS)
  if (abs < 60) return REL.format(Math.round(diffS), 'second')
  if (abs < 3600) return REL.format(Math.round(diffS / 60), 'minute')
  if (abs < 86400) return REL.format(Math.round(diffS / 3600), 'hour')
  if (abs < 86400 * 14) return REL.format(Math.round(diffS / 86400), 'day')
  return REL.format(Math.round(diffS / (86400 * 7)), 'week')
}

/** Recorded elapsed time for replay clocks: 75 → "01:15", 3725 → "1:02:05". */
export function fmtElapsed(seconds: number): string {
  const s = Math.max(0, Math.floor(seconds))
  const h = Math.floor(s / 3600)
  const m = Math.floor((s % 3600) / 60)
  const ss = s % 60
  const pad = (n: number) => String(n).padStart(2, '0')
  return h > 0 ? `${h}:${pad(m)}:${pad(ss)}` : `${pad(m)}:${pad(ss)}`
}

export function shortHash(hash: string): string {
  return hash.length > 12 ? `${hash.slice(0, 12)}…` : hash
}

/** Fills `{value}` and `{limit}` in engine/LLM text from the finding record (spec §6.3). */
export function fillPlaceholders(text: string, f: Pick<Finding, 'value' | 'limit' | 'unit'>): string {
  return text
    .replaceAll('{value}', fmtQty(f.value ?? null, f.unit ?? null))
    .replaceAll('{limit}', fmtQty(f.limit ?? null, f.unit ?? null))
}

const KEY_LABELS: Record<string, { label: string; unit?: string; usd?: boolean }> = {
  dv_pct: { label: 'ΔV', unit: '%' },
  cost_delta: { label: 'Cost Δ', usd: true },
  cost_usd: { label: 'Cost', usd: true },
  cost: { label: 'Cost', usd: true },
  margin_pct: { label: 'Margin', unit: '%' },
  loading_pct: { label: 'Loading', unit: '%' },
  losses_kw: { label: 'Losses', unit: 'kW' },
  lead_time_weeks: { label: 'Lead time', unit: 'weeks' },
}

const SUFFIX_UNITS: [string, string][] = [
  ['_pct', '%'],
  ['_kva', 'kVA'],
  ['_mva', 'MVA'],
  ['_mwh', 'MWh'],
  ['_kw', 'kW'],
  ['_mw', 'MW'],
  ['_kv', 'kV'],
  ['_ka', 'kA'],
  ['_kpa', 'kPa'],
  ['_lps', 'L/s'],
  ['_ms', 'm/s'],
  ['_km', 'km'],
  ['_weeks', 'weeks'],
  ['_usd', 'USD'],
  ['_a', 'A'],
  ['_v', 'V'],
  ['_m', 'm'],
]

/** Turns an engine dict key into a label and unit: "ampacity_a" → { label: "Ampacity", unit: "A" }. */
export function describeKey(key: string): { label: string; unit?: string; usd?: boolean } {
  const known = KEY_LABELS[key]
  if (known) return known
  const lower = key.toLowerCase()
  for (const [suffix, unit] of SUFFIX_UNITS) {
    if (lower.endsWith(suffix) && lower.length > suffix.length) {
      return { label: humanize(key.slice(0, -suffix.length)), unit }
    }
  }
  return { label: humanize(key) }
}

function humanize(key: string): string {
  const words = key.replace(/_/g, ' ').trim()
  return words.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + words.slice(1)
}

/** Formats one engine key/value pair for display, e.g. ("dv_pct", 0.84) → "ΔV 0.84%". */
export function fmtKeyValue(key: string, value: unknown): string {
  const d = describeKey(key)
  if (typeof value === 'number') {
    if (d.usd) return `${d.label} ${fmtUsd(value, key.endsWith('delta'))}`
    return `${d.label} ${fmtQty(value, d.unit === 'USD' ? null : d.unit)}${d.unit === 'USD' ? ' USD' : ''}`
  }
  return `${d.label} ${String(value)}`
}
```

```ts
import { CircleCheck, CircleMinus, CircleX, TriangleAlert, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import type { FindingStatus } from './types'

export interface StatusMeta {
  label: string
  icon: LucideIcon
  text: string // tailwind text colour class
  bg: string // tailwind background class (subtle)
  border: string
  hex: string // for canvas/3D materials
}

// Status is always icon + text; colour is a third, redundant cue (spec §12, WCAG AA on #0b0d10).
export const STATUS_META: Record<FindingStatus, StatusMeta> = {
  pass: { label: 'Pass', icon: CircleCheck, text: 'text-emerald-400', bg: 'bg-emerald-500/10', border: 'border-emerald-500/40', hex: '#34d399' },
  fail: { label: 'Fail', icon: CircleX, text: 'text-rose-400', bg: 'bg-rose-500/15', border: 'border-rose-500/60', hex: '#fb7185' },
  warn: { label: 'Warn', icon: TriangleAlert, text: 'text-amber-300', bg: 'bg-amber-500/10', border: 'border-amber-400/50', hex: '#fcd34d' },
  'n/a': { label: 'N/A', icon: CircleMinus, text: 'text-zinc-400', bg: 'bg-zinc-500/10', border: 'border-zinc-600', hex: '#a1a1aa' },
}

const RANK: Record<FindingStatus, number> = { 'n/a': 0, pass: 1, warn: 2, fail: 3 }

export function worstStatus(statuses: Iterable<FindingStatus>): FindingStatus | null {
  let worst: FindingStatus | null = null
  for (const s of statuses) if (worst === null || RANK[s] > RANK[worst]) worst = s
  return worst
}

export function statusRank(s: FindingStatus): number {
  return RANK[s]
}

/** Rule pack v0.1 columns (spec §8.3). Names only; limits always come from finding records. */
export const RULES: { id: string; name: string }[] = [
  { id: 'R01', name: 'Transformer loading' },
  { id: 'R02', name: 'Busway / feeder rating' },
  { id: 'R03', name: 'Breaker coordination' },
  { id: 'R04', name: 'Breaker Icu' },
  { id: 'R04b', name: 'Busway withstand' },
  { id: 'R05', name: 'Voltage drop' },
  { id: 'R06', name: 'Single points of failure' },
  { id: 'R07', name: 'UPS capacity' },
  { id: 'R08', name: 'CDU capacity' },
  { id: 'R09', name: 'Rack flow' },
  { id: 'R10', name: 'Pipe velocity' },
  { id: 'R11', name: 'Liquid above electrical' },
  { id: 'R12', name: 'SLD = BOQ = PR' },
  { id: 'R13', name: 'CDU pump supply' },
  { id: 'R14', name: 'Index-circuit Δp' },
  { id: 'R15', name: 'Lead time' },
]

export function ruleName(ruleId: string): string {
  return RULES.find((r) => r.id === ruleId)?.name ?? ruleId
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/storage.ts`:
```ts
// Browser storage helpers. Every key is prefixed so "Reset demo workspace" can clear only ours.
export const PREFIX = 'kirchhoff.'

export function readJson<T>(key: string, fallback: T, store: Storage = localStorage): T {
  try {
    const raw = store.getItem(PREFIX + key)
    return raw === null ? fallback : (JSON.parse(raw) as T)
  } catch {
    return fallback
  }
}

export function writeJson(key: string, value: unknown, store: Storage = localStorage): void {
  try {
    store.setItem(PREFIX + key, JSON.stringify(value))
  } catch {
    // storage full or blocked (private mode): onboarding simply does not persist
  }
}

export function clearKirchhoffStorage(): void {
  for (const store of [localStorage, sessionStorage]) {
    const keys: string[] = []
    for (let i = 0; i < store.length; i++) {
      const k = store.key(i)
      if (k?.startsWith(PREFIX)) keys.push(k)
    }
    keys.forEach((k) => store.removeItem(k))
  }
}
```

```tsx
import { cn } from 'cn'
import { STATUS_META } from '@/lib/status'
import type { FindingStatus } from '@/lib/types'

export function StatusBadge({
  status,
  compact = false,
  className,
}: {
  status: FindingStatus
  compact?: boolean
  className?: string
}) {
  const m = STATUS_META[status]
  const Icon = m.icon
  return (
    <span
      className={cn(
        'inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-xs font-medium',
        m.text,
        m.bg,
        m.border,
        compact && 'border-transparent bg-transparent px-0',
        className,
      )}
    >
      <Icon aria-hidden className="size-3.5" />
      <span>{m.label}</span>
    </span>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { cn } from 'cn'
import { FlaskConical, Info } from 'lucide-react'

import { AGENTS } from '@/lib/agents'
import type { AgentName } from '@/lib/types'

export const FICTIONAL_TEXT = 'Demo workspace — all companies, people and data are fictional'
export const SANDBOX_TEXT = 'sandbox (simulated)'
export const ILLUSTRATIVE_TEXT = 'IEC-based, illustrative values, not for construction'

export function DemoBanner() {
  return (
    <div
      role="note"
      className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border-b border-amber-500/20 bg-amber-500/10 px-4 py-1 text-xs text-amber-200"
    >
      <Info aria-hidden className="size-3.5" />
      {FICTIONAL_TEXT}
    </div>
  )
}

export function SandboxLabel({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  return (
    <span className={cn('inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded bg-zinc-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-zinc-300', className)}>
      <FlaskConical aria-hidden className="size-3" />
      {SANDBOX_TEXT}
    </span>
  )
}

export function IllustrativeLabel({ className }: { className?: string }) {
  return <span className={cn('text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500', className)}>{ILLUSTRATIVE_TEXT}</span>
}

export function AgentDot({ agent, className }: { agent: AgentName; className?: string }) {
  return (
    <span
      aria-hidden
      className={cn('inline-block size-2 shrink-0 rounded-full', className)}
      style={{ background: AGENTS[agent].color }}
    />
  )
}

/** Monospace, tabular number with optional unit. Pass already-formatted text. */
export function Num({ children, className }: { children: ReactNode; className?: string }) {
  return <span className={cn('font-mono tabular-nums', className)}>{children}</span>
}

export function EmptyState({ title, children, action }: { title: string; children?: ReactNode; action?: ReactNode }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full min-h-40 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-2 p-6 text-center">
      <p className="text-sm font-medium text-zinc-200">{title}</p>
      {children && <div className="max-w-md text-sm text-zinc-400">{children}</div>}
      {action}
    </div>
  )
}
```

### Task 2: Fixture bundle and pure selectors (Tue)

A small but complete `ProjectBundle` fixture (two revisions, a fix loop, a stale attempt, a precomputed what-if) plus the pure functions every view uses: event payload readers, the §6.2 attempt filter, lanes, statuses, model diff and impact chains.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/test/fixtures/hall-mini.json`, `apps/web/src/test/fixtures/world.json`, `apps/web/src/test/fixtures/integrations.json`, `apps/web/src/test/fixtures/index.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/model.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/events.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/select.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/diff.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/impact.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/lib/select.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/lib/diff.test.ts`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 1 `@/lib/types`, `@/lib/status`, `@/lib/agents`.
- Produces:
  - Fixtures: `fixtureBundle(): Bundle`, `fixtureWorld(): World`, `fixtureIntegrations(): IntegrationsSnapshot` (fresh deep copies).
  - `@/lib/model`: `type Discipline = 'electrical' | 'cooling'`, `elementIndex(model): Map<id, {id, kind, discipline}>`.
  - `@/lib/events`: `findingPayload(ev)`, `artifactPayload(ev)` (`{type: 'brief'|'options'|'revision'|'export', ref}`), `gatePayload(ev)`, `ledgerPayload(ev)`, `toolPayload(ev)` (`{tool, args, summary}`), `planPayload(ev)`, `refTail(ref)`, `visibleEvents(events, steps)`.
  - `@/lib/select`: `type DataSource = 'static'|'live'|'replay'`, `latestRevision, revisionById, parentRevision, findingsFor, findingById, statusByElement, countByStatus, currentRun, approvalFor, signedRevision, agentsInvolved, revisionRef(b, rev, source), findWhatIf(b, ref, param, value), exportsOf, relatedElementIds(f, knownIds)`, `type LaneStatus`, `interface Lane {agent, status, events, counters}`, `buildLanes(b): Lane[]`.
  - `@/lib/diff`: `type ChangeKind`, `interface ElementChange {id, collection, kind, fields}`, `interface ModelDiff {changes, byId}`, `diffModels(before, after)`.
  - `@/lib/impact`: `classifyChain(chain, model): Discipline`, `chainHighlights(whatIf, model): Map<id, Discipline[]>`.

```ts
import type { IntegrationsSnapshot, ProjectBundle, World } from '@/lib/types'
import { normalizeBundle, type Bundle } from '@/lib/types'
import hallMini from './hall-mini.json'
import integrations from './integrations.json'
import world from './world.json'

/** Fresh deep copy each call so tests can mutate freely. */
export function fixtureBundle(): Bundle {
  return normalizeBundle(structuredClone(hallMini) as unknown as ProjectBundle)
}

export function fixtureWorld(): World {
  return structuredClone(world) as unknown as World
}

export function fixtureIntegrations(): IntegrationsSnapshot {
  return structuredClone(integrations) as unknown as IntegrationsSnapshot
}
```

```ts
import { fixtureBundle } from '@/test/fixtures'
import { visibleEvents } from './events'
import {
  agentsInvolved,
  approvalFor,
  buildLanes,
  countByStatus,
  findWhatIf,
  findingsFor,
  latestRevision,
  relatedElementIds,
  revisionRef,
  statusByElement,
} from './select'

test('normalised fixture bundle exposes revisions in order and findings per revision', () => {
  const b = fixtureBundle()
  expect(latestRevision(b)?.id).toBe('r2')
  expect(countByStatus(findingsFor(b, 'r1'))).toEqual({ pass: 3, fail: 2, warn: 1, 'n/a': 0 })
  expect(approvalFor(b, 'r2')?.approver_name).toBe('Priya Raman')
})

test('worst status per element', () => {
  const s = statusByElement(findingsFor(fixtureBundle(), 'r1'))
  expect(s.get('FA-01')).toBe('fail')
  expect(s.get('T-A1')).toBe('pass')
  expect(s.get('BR-R1')).toBe('warn')
})

test('completed steps show only the committing attempt (spec §6.2)', () => {
  const b = fixtureBundle()
  const seqs = visibleEvents(b.events, b.steps).map((e) => e.seq)
  expect(seqs).not.toContain(14) // p3.check attempt 1 is stale
  expect(seqs).toContain(15)
  expect(seqs).toContain(19) // step without a record keeps its latest attempt
})

test('lanes carry per-agent status and counted record summaries', () => {
  const lanes = buildLanes(fixtureBundle())
  const intake = lanes.find((l) => l.agent === 'intake')!
  expect(intake.status).toBe('done')
  expect(intake.counters).toEqual(['2/3 quotes verified', 'Drawing read · DXF agrees'])
  const checker = lanes.find((l) => l.agent === 'checker')!
  expect(checker.counters).toEqual(['6 checks'])
  expect(checker.events.every((e) => e.attempt === 2)).toBe(true)
  expect(lanes.find((l) => l.agent === 'procurement')!.counters).toEqual(['TX-2500 → TX-3150 (R15)'])
})

test('agents involved are counted from events', () => {
  expect(agentsInvolved(fixtureBundle())).toEqual([
    'intake',
    'orchestrator',
    'layout',
    'power',
    'cooling',
    'procurement',
    'checker',
    'docs',
  ])
})

test('revision refs and precomputed what-ifs', () => {
  const b = fixtureBundle()
  expect(revisionRef(b, 'r2', 'static')).toBe('seed:hall-a@r2')
  expect(revisionRef(b, 'r2', 'live')).toBe('ws:r2')
  expect(findWhatIf(b, 'seed:hall-a@r2', 'rack_kw', 200)?.impact_chain).toHaveLength(2)
  expect(findWhatIf(b, 'seed:hall-a@r2', 'rack_kw', 150)).toBeNull()
})

test('related elements include string inputs that name model elements', () => {
  const b = fixtureBundle()

  expect(relatedElementIds(f, new Set(['H-Z2', 'FB-03']))).toEqual(['H-Z2', 'FB-03'])
})
```

```ts
import { fixtureBundle } from '@/test/fixtures'
import { diffModels } from './diff'
import { chainHighlights, classifyChain } from './impact'
import { elementIndex } from './model'

test('diff between r1 and r2 finds the two fixed elements and nothing else', () => {
  const [r1, r2] = fixtureBundle().revisions
  const d = diffModels(r1.model, r2.model)
  expect([...d.byId.entries()].sort()).toEqual([
    ['FA-01', 'changed'],
    ['H-Z2', 'changed'],
  ])
  const fa = d.changes.find((c) => c.id === 'FA-01')!
  expect(fa.fields.map((f) => f.field)).toEqual(['kind', 'catalogue_id'])
  expect(fa.fields[1]).toEqual({ field: 'catalogue_id', before: 'BW-1600', after: 'CBL-8x240' })
})

test('diff reports added and removed elements', () => {
  const [r1] = fixtureBundle().revisions
  const after = structuredClone(r1.model!)
  after.equipment = after.equipment.filter((e) => e.id !== 'B-01')
  after.cooling_nodes.push({ id: 'CDU-2', kind: 'cdu', zone: 'Z2', capacity_kw: 1200, x: 10, y: 30 })
  const d = diffModels(r1.model, after)
  expect(d.byId.get('B-01')).toBe('removed')
  expect(d.byId.get('CDU-2')).toBe('added')
})

test('element index covers equipment, feeders, racks, cooling and zones', () => {
  const idx = elementIndex(fixtureBundle().revisions[0].model!)
  expect(idx.get('T-A1')).toEqual({ id: 'T-A1', kind: 'transformer', discipline: 'electrical' })
  expect(idx.get('FA-01')?.kind).toBe('feeder')
  expect(idx.get('L-MV-TA1')?.kind).toBe('link')
  expect(idx.get('H-Z2')?.discipline).toBe('cooling')
  expect(idx.get('Z2')).toEqual({ id: 'Z2', kind: 'zone', discipline: 'cooling' })
})

test('impact chains are split into electrical and cooling', () => {
  const b = fixtureBundle()
  const m = b.revisions[1].model!
  const w = b.what_ifs[0]
  expect(classifyChain(w.impact_chain[0], m)).toBe('electrical')
  expect(classifyChain(w.impact_chain[1], m)).toBe('cooling')
  const h = chainHighlights(w, m)
  expect(h.get('T-A1')).toEqual(['electrical'])
  expect(h.get('CDU-1')).toEqual(['cooling'])
  expect(h.get('R1-01')).toEqual(['electrical', 'cooling'])
})
```

`apps/web/src/lib/model.ts`:
```ts
// Element index over a DesignModel: every addressable id (equipment, connections, racks,
// cooling nodes, pipes, zones) with its discipline, for impact chains and click handling.
import type { DesignModel, EquipmentKind } from './types'

export type Discipline = 'electrical' | 'cooling'
export type ElementKind = EquipmentKind | 'feeder' | 'link' | 'pipe' | 'cdu' | 'manifold' | 'rack' | 'zone'

export interface ElementInfo {
  id: string
  kind: ElementKind
  discipline: Discipline
}

export function elementIndex(m: DesignModel | null): Map<string, ElementInfo> {
  const idx = new Map<string, ElementInfo>()
  if (!m) return idx
  for (const e of m.equipment) idx.set(e.id, { id: e.id, kind: e.kind, discipline: 'electrical' })
  for (const c of m.connections)
    idx.set(c.id, { id: c.id, kind: c.kind === 'internal' ? 'link' : 'feeder', discipline: 'electrical' })
  for (const r of m.racks) idx.set(r.id, { id: r.id, kind: 'rack', discipline: 'electrical' })
  for (const n of m.cooling_nodes) {
    idx.set(n.id, { id: n.id, kind: n.kind, discipline: 'cooling' })
    if (!idx.has(n.zone)) idx.set(n.zone, { id: n.zone, kind: 'zone', discipline: 'cooling' })
  }
  for (const p of m.pipes) {
    idx.set(p.id, { id: p.id, kind: 'pipe', discipline: 'cooling' })
    if (!idx.has(p.zone)) idx.set(p.zone, { id: p.zone, kind: 'zone', discipline: 'cooling' })
  }
  return idx
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/events.ts`:
```ts
// Typed readers for RunEvent payloads (event vocabulary in plans/2026-10-13-interfaces.md).
import type { FindingStatus, LedgerEntry, OpenQuestion, PlanStep, RunEvent, StepRecord } from './types'

const obj = (v: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | null =>
  v && typeof v === 'object' && !Array.isArray(v) ? (v as Record<string, unknown>) : null

export interface FindingPayload {
  finding_id: string
  rule_id: string
  element_id: string
  status: FindingStatus
  rev?: string
}
export function findingPayload(ev: RunEvent): FindingPayload | null {
  const p = obj(ev.payload)
  if (ev.kind !== 'finding' || !p || typeof p.finding_id !== 'string') return null
  return {
    finding_id: p.finding_id,
    rule_id: String(p.rule_id ?? ''),
    element_id: String(p.element_id ?? ''),
    status: (p.status as FindingStatus) ?? 'n/a',
    rev: typeof p.rev === 'string' ? p.rev : undefined,
  }
}

export type ArtifactType = 'brief' | 'options' | 'revision' | 'export'
export function artifactPayload(ev: RunEvent): { type: ArtifactType; ref: string } | null {
  const p = obj(ev.payload)
  if (ev.kind !== 'artifact' || !p || typeof p.ref !== 'string') return null
  return { type: p.type as ArtifactType, ref: p.ref }
}

export type GateName = 'plan' | 'option' | 'review'
export function gatePayload(ev: RunEvent): GateName | null {
  const p = obj(ev.payload)
  return ev.kind === 'gate' && p && typeof p.gate === 'string' ? (p.gate as GateName) : null
}

export function ledgerPayload(ev: RunEvent): LedgerEntry | null {
  const e = obj(obj(ev.payload)?.entry)
  return ev.kind === 'ledger' && e ? (e as unknown as LedgerEntry) : null
}

export function toolPayload(
  ev: RunEvent,
): { tool: string; args: Record<string, unknown>; summary: Record<string, unknown> } | null {
  const p = obj(ev.payload)
  if ((ev.kind !== 'tool_call' && ev.kind !== 'tool_result') || !p) return null
  return { tool: String(p.tool ?? ev.title), args: obj(p.args) ?? {}, summary: obj(p.summary) ?? {} }
}

export function planPayload(ev: RunEvent): { plan: PlanStep[]; open_questions: OpenQuestion[] } | null {
  const p = obj(ev.payload)
  if (ev.kind !== 'plan' || !p) return null
  return {
    plan: Array.isArray(p.plan) ? (p.plan as PlanStep[]) : [],
    open_questions: Array.isArray(p.open_questions) ? (p.open_questions as OpenQuestion[]) : [],
  }
}

/** Artifact refs may be bare ids ("r1") or Firestore paths ("workspaces/u/revisions/r1"). */
export function refTail(ref: string): string {
  const parts = ref.split('/')
  return parts[parts.length - 1]
}

/**
 * Spec §6.2: for a completed step show only events from the attempt that committed it; for the
 * running step, events from its current attempt. Steps without a record show their latest attempt.
 */
export function visibleEvents(events: RunEvent[], steps: StepRecord[]): RunEvent[] {
  const recorded = new Map(steps.map((s) => [s.id, s.attempt]))
  const latest = new Map<string, number>()
  for (const e of events) latest.set(e.step, Math.max(latest.get(e.step) ?? 0, e.attempt ?? 1))
  return events
    .filter((e) => (e.attempt ?? 1) === (recorded.get(e.step) ?? latest.get(e.step)))
    .sort((a, b) => a.seq - b.seq)
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/select.ts`:
```ts
// Pure selectors over a Bundle. Counting records is allowed (spec §3 "counted live", §6.3
// "N/M quotes verified", §13.5 "n counted from the bundle"); arithmetic on engineering values is not.
import { LANE_ORDER } from './agents'
import { artifactPayload, visibleEvents } from './events'
import { worstStatus } from './status'
import type {
  AgentName,
  Approval,
  Bundle,
  Finding,
  FindingStatus,
  Revision,
  RunEvent,
  RunRecord,
  WhatIfParam,
  WhatIfResult,
} from './types'

export type DataSource = 'static' | 'live' | 'replay'

export function latestRevision(b: Bundle): Revision | null {
  return b.revisions.length ? b.revisions[b.revisions.length - 1] : null
}

export function revisionById(b: Bundle, id: string | null | undefined): Revision | null {
  return (id && b.revisions.find((r) => r.id === id)) || null
}

export function parentRevision(b: Bundle, rev: Revision | null): Revision | null {
  return rev?.parent ? revisionById(b, rev.parent) : null
}

export function findingsFor(b: Bundle, revId: string | null | undefined): Finding[] {
  return (revId && b.findings[revId]) || []
}

export function findingById(b: Bundle, revId: string | null | undefined, id: string | null): Finding | null {
  return (id && findingsFor(b, revId).find((f) => f.id === id)) || null
}

export function statusByElement(findings: Finding[]): Map<string, FindingStatus> {
  const groups = new Map<string, FindingStatus[]>()
  for (const f of findings) groups.set(f.element_id, [...(groups.get(f.element_id) ?? []), f.status])
  const out = new Map<string, FindingStatus>()
  for (const [id, ss] of groups) out.set(id, worstStatus(ss)!)
  return out
}

export function countByStatus(findings: Finding[]): Record<FindingStatus, number> {
  const c: Record<FindingStatus, number> = { pass: 0, fail: 0, warn: 0, 'n/a': 0 }
  for (const f of findings) c[f.status] += 1
  return c
}

export function currentRun(b: Bundle): RunRecord | null {
  return b.runs.length ? [...b.runs].sort((x, y) => x.started_at - y.started_at)[b.runs.length - 1] : null
}

export function approvalFor(b: Bundle, revId: string | null | undefined): Approval | null {
  return b.approvals.find((a) => a.revision === revId && a.decision === 'approved') ?? null
}

export function signedRevision(b: Bundle): Revision | null {
  return [...b.revisions].reverse().find((r) => approvalFor(b, r.id)) ?? null
}

/** Distinct agents that emitted events (system excluded) — tour step 4 "{n} agents designed this". */
export function agentsInvolved(b: Bundle): AgentName[] {
  const seen = new Set<AgentName>()
  for (const e of b.events) if (e.agent !== 'system') seen.add(e.agent)
  return LANE_ORDER.filter((a) => seen.has(a))
}

/** The engine's revision reference for /api/what-if and /api/explain (spec §6.4). */
export function revisionRef(b: Bundle, revId: string, source: DataSource): string {
  return source === 'live' ? `ws:${revId}` : `seed:${b.project.id}@${revId}`
}

export function findWhatIf(b: Bundle, ref: string, param: WhatIfParam, value: number): WhatIfResult | null {
  return b.what_ifs.find((w) => w.revision_ref === ref && w.param === param && w.value === value) ?? null
}

export function exportsOf(b: Bundle): { title: string; ref: string }[] {
  return b.events
    .map((e) => ({ e, a: artifactPayload(e) }))
    .filter((x) => x.a?.type === 'export')
    .map((x) => ({ title: x.e.title, ref: x.a!.ref }))
}

/** Element ids a finding touches: its element plus any string input naming another element (e.g. R11 `over`). */
export function relatedElementIds(f: Finding, known: Set<string>): string[] {
  const ids = [f.element_id]
  for (const v of Object.values(f.inputs ?? {})) if (typeof v === 'string' && known.has(v) && !ids.includes(v)) ids.push(v)
  return ids
}

export type LaneStatus = 'idle' | 'running' | 'waiting' | 'done' | 'failed'
export interface Lane {
  agent: AgentName
  status: LaneStatus
  events: RunEvent[]
  counters: string[]
}

export function buildLanes(b: Bundle): Lane[] {
  const events = visibleEvents(b.events, b.steps)
  const run = currentRun(b)
  const lastSeq = events.length ? events[events.length - 1].seq : -1
  const stepStatus = new Map(b.steps.map((s) => [s.id, s.status]))
  const rev = latestRevision(b)
  const findings = findingsFor(b, rev?.id)
  const selected = run?.options?.find((o) => o.id === run.selected_option)

  return LANE_ORDER.filter((a) => a !== 'asset').map((agent) => {
    const evs = events.filter((e) => e.agent === agent)
    let status: LaneStatus = 'idle'
    if (evs.length) {
      const states = [...new Set(evs.map((e) => e.step))].map((s) => stepStatus.get(s))
      const last = evs[evs.length - 1]
      if (states.includes('failed')) status = 'failed'
      else if (states.includes('running')) status = 'running'
      else if (run?.status === 'running' && last.seq === lastSeq && !stepStatus.has(last.step)) status = 'running'
      else if (run?.status === 'at_gate' && last.kind === 'gate' && last.seq === lastSeq) status = 'waiting'
      else status = 'done'
    }
    const counters: string[] = []
    if (agent === 'intake' && b.brief) {
      const reqs = b.brief.requirements
      counters.push(`${reqs.filter((r) => r.verified).length}/${reqs.length} quotes verified`)
      const dc = b.brief.drawing_check ?? {}
      if (dc.agrees === true) counters.push('Drawing read · DXF agrees')
      else if (Array.isArray(dc.differences)) counters.push(`Drawing read · ${dc.differences.length} differences`)
    }
    if (agent === 'layout' && rev?.model) counters.push(`${rev.model.racks.length} racks placed`)
    if (agent === 'power' && run?.selected_option) counters.push(`Topology ${run.selected_option}`)
    if (agent === 'cooling' && rev?.model)
      counters.push(`${rev.model.cooling_nodes.filter((n) => n.kind === 'cdu').length} CDUs`)
    if (agent === 'procurement')
      for (const s of selected?.swaps ?? []) counters.push(`${String(s.from)} → ${String(s.to)} (${String(s.reason)})`)
    if (agent === 'checker' && findings.length) {
      const c = countByStatus(findings)
      counters.push(`${findings.length} checks`)
      if (c.fail) counters.push(`${c.fail} fail`)
    }
    if (agent === 'docs') {
      const n = exportsOf(b).length
      if (n) counters.push(`${n} exports`)
    }
    return { agent, status, events: evs, counters }
  })
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/diff.ts`:
```ts
// Graph diff between two revisions' models (P11 "diff the graph, not the image").
import type { DesignModel } from './types'

export type ChangeKind = 'added' | 'removed' | 'changed'
export interface FieldChange {
  field: string
  before: unknown
  after: unknown
}
export interface ElementChange {
  id: string
  collection: 'racks' | 'equipment' | 'connections' | 'cooling_nodes' | 'pipes'
  kind: ChangeKind
  fields: FieldChange[]
}
export interface ModelDiff {
  changes: ElementChange[]
  byId: Map<string, ChangeKind>
}

const FIELDS = {
  racks: ['kw', 'liquid_fraction', 'feeds', 'x', 'y'],
  equipment: ['kind', 'rating', 'unit', 'catalogue_id', 'system'],
  connections: ['kind', 'src', 'dst', 'length_m', 'catalogue_id', 'route'],
  cooling_nodes: ['kind', 'zone', 'capacity_kw', 'x', 'y'],
  pipes: ['dn', 'tier', 'route', 'flow_lps'],
} as const

type Collection = keyof typeof FIELDS
const same = (a: unknown, b: unknown) => JSON.stringify(a ?? null) === JSON.stringify(b ?? null)

export function diffModels(before: DesignModel | null | undefined, after: DesignModel | null | undefined): ModelDiff {
  const changes: ElementChange[] = []
  if (before && after) {
    for (const collection of Object.keys(FIELDS) as Collection[]) {
      const a = new Map((before[collection] as { id: string }[]).map((x) => [x.id, x as unknown as Record<string, unknown>]))
      const b = new Map((after[collection] as { id: string }[]).map((x) => [x.id, x as unknown as Record<string, unknown>]))
      for (const [id, nb] of b) {
        const na = a.get(id)
        if (!na) {
          changes.push({ id, collection, kind: 'added', fields: [] })
          continue
        }
        const fields = FIELDS[collection]
          .filter((f) => !same(na[f], nb[f]))
          .map((f) => ({ field: f, before: na[f], after: nb[f] }))
        if (fields.length) changes.push({ id, collection, kind: 'changed', fields })
      }
      for (const id of a.keys()) if (!b.has(id)) changes.push({ id, collection, kind: 'removed', fields: [] })
    }
  }
  return { changes, byId: new Map(changes.map((c) => [c.id, c.kind])) }
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/impact.ts`:
```ts
// What-if impact chains (spec §6.4): ordered element paths rack → busway → board → transformer
// and rack → zone → CDU, shown in two colours (electrical vs cooling).
import { elementIndex, type Discipline } from './model'
import type { DesignModel, WhatIfResult } from './types'

export function classifyChain(chain: string[], model: DesignModel | null): Discipline {
  const idx = elementIndex(model)
  return chain.some((id) => idx.get(id)?.discipline === 'cooling' || /^(Z\d+|CDU-|H-Z|BR-)/.test(id))
    ? 'cooling'
    : 'electrical'
}

/** element id → disciplines of every chain it sits on (a rack is on both). */
export function chainHighlights(result: WhatIfResult | null, model: DesignModel | null): Map<string, Discipline[]> {
  const out = new Map<string, Discipline[]>()
  if (!result) return out
  for (const chain of result.impact_chain) {
    const d = classifyChain(chain, model)
    for (const id of chain) {
      const cur = out.get(id) ?? []
      if (!cur.includes(d)) out.set(id, [...cur, d])
    }
  }
  return out
}
```

```ts
import { ApiError, httpApiClient, type Fetcher } from './apiClient'

function recorder(response: Response) {
  const calls: { path: string; init?: RequestInit }[] = []
  const fetcher: Fetcher = async (path, init) => {
    calls.push({ path, init })
    return response.clone()
  }
  return { calls, fetcher }
}

const json = (body: unknown, status = 200) =>
  new Response(JSON.stringify(body), { status, headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' } })

test('startRun posts the interface body and returns the run id', async () => {
  const { calls, fetcher } = recorder(json({ run_id: 'r-1' }, 202))
  const api = httpApiClient(fetcher)
  await expect(api.startRun('hall-b', 'ask')).resolves.toEqual({ run_id: 'r-1' })
  expect(calls[0].path).toBe('/api/runs')
  expect(calls[0].init?.method).toBe('POST')
  expect(JSON.parse(String(calls[0].init?.body))).toEqual({ project_id: 'hall-b', autonomy: 'ask' })
})

test('gate calls hit the gate routes with snake_case bodies', async () => {
  const { calls, fetcher } = recorder(new Response(null, { status: 202 }))
  const api = httpApiClient(fetcher)
  await api.planGate('r-1', { 'Q-redundancy': '2N' }, { 'p2.place_racks': 'keep row R1 short' })
  await api.optionGate('r-1', '2N')
  await api.reviewGate('r-1', 'approved', 'ok')

    '/api/runs/r-1/gates/plan',

  ])

    answers: { 'Q-redundancy': '2N' },

  })
  expect(JSON.parse(String(calls[1].init?.body))).toEqual({ option_id: '2N' })
})

test('non-2xx responses raise ApiError with the server detail', async () => {
  const { fetcher } = recorder(json({ detail: 'live-run cap reached' }, 429))
  const err = await httpApiClient(fetcher).startRun('hall-b', 'auto').catch((e: unknown) => e)
  expect(err).toBeInstanceOf(ApiError)

  expect((err as ApiError).detail).toBe('live-run cap reached')
})

})
```

`apps/web/src/services/apiClient.ts`:
```ts
// Typed client for the HTTP API table in plans/2026-10-13-interfaces.md. Every mutation goes
// through /api (clients never write Firestore). Every call is aborted after 12 s (API budget < 10 s).
import type {
  AssetResult,
  Autonomy,
  Material,
  Opportunity,
  PurchaseRequisition,
  TopologyId,
  WhatIfParam,
  WhatIfResult,
} from '@/lib/types'

export type Fetcher = (path: string, init?: RequestInit) => Promise<Response>

export class ApiError extends Error {
  readonly status: number
  readonly detail: string
  constructor(status: number, detail: string) {
    super(`API ${status}: ${detail}`)
    this.status = status
    this.detail = detail
  }
}

export interface VoiceToolResult {
  name: 'what_if' | 'explain_finding' | 'focus_element'
  args: Record<string, unknown>
  result: Record<string, unknown>
}
export interface VoiceResponse {
  transcript: string
  answer: string
  tool_results: VoiceToolResult[]
}

export interface ApiClient {
  health(): Promise<{ ok: boolean; version: string }>
  ensureWorkspace(): Promise<{ uid: string; created: boolean }>
  resetWorkspace(): Promise<void>
  startRun(projectId: string, autonomy: Autonomy): Promise<{ run_id: string }>
  planGate(runId: string, answers: Record<string, string>, planNotes: Record<string, string>): Promise<void>
  optionGate(runId: string, optionId: TopologyId): Promise<void>
  reviewGate(runId: string, decision: 'approved' | 'rejected', comment: string): Promise<void>
  resume(runId: string): Promise<{ resumed: boolean }>
  whatIf(revisionRef: string, param: WhatIfParam, value: number): Promise<WhatIfResult>
  explain(revisionRef: string, findingId: string): Promise<{ text: string }>
  voice(audio: Blob, revisionRef: string): Promise<VoiceResponse>
  asset(pack: 'grid' | 'solar'): Promise<AssetResult>
  erpMaterials(): Promise<Material[]>
  erpPrs(): Promise<PurchaseRequisition[]>
  crmOpportunities(): Promise<Opportunity[]>
  fileBlob(path: string): Promise<Blob>
}

const TIMEOUT_MS = 12_000

export function httpApiClient(fetcher: Fetcher): ApiClient {
  async function call<T>(path: string, init: RequestInit = {}): Promise<T> {
    const ctrl = new AbortController()
    const timer = setTimeout(() => ctrl.abort(), TIMEOUT_MS)
    try {
      const r = await fetcher(path, { ...init, signal: ctrl.signal })
      if (!r.ok) {
        let detail = r.statusText
        try {
          const body = (await r.json()) as { detail?: unknown }
          if (body.detail !== undefined) detail = typeof body.detail === 'string' ? body.detail : JSON.stringify(body.detail)
        } catch {
          // non-JSON error body
        }
        throw new ApiError(r.status, detail)
      }
      if (r.status === 204) return undefined as T
      const text = await r.text()
      return (text ? JSON.parse(text) : undefined) as T
    } finally {
      clearTimeout(timer)
    }
  }
  const post = <T>(path: string, body?: unknown) =>
    call<T>(path, { method: 'POST', body: body === undefined ? undefined : JSON.stringify(body) })

  return {
    health: () => call('/api/health'),
    ensureWorkspace: () => post('/api/workspace'),
    resetWorkspace: async () => {
      await post('/api/workspace/reset')
    },
    startRun: (projectId, autonomy) => post('/api/runs', { project_id: projectId, autonomy }),
    planGate: async (runId, answers, planNotes) => {
      await post(`/api/runs/${runId}/gates/plan`, { answers, plan_notes: planNotes })
    },
    optionGate: async (runId, optionId) => {
      await post(`/api/runs/${runId}/gates/option`, { option_id: optionId })
    },
    reviewGate: async (runId, decision, comment) => {
      await post(`/api/runs/${runId}/gates/review`, { decision, comment })
    },
    resume: (runId) => post(`/api/runs/${runId}/resume`),
    whatIf: (revisionRef, param, value) => post('/api/what-if', { revision_ref: revisionRef, param, value }),
    explain: (revisionRef, findingId) => post('/api/explain', { revision_ref: revisionRef, finding_id: findingId }),
    voice: (audio, revisionRef) => {
      const form = new FormData()
      form.append('audio', audio, audio.type.includes('ogg') ? 'clip.ogg' : 'clip.webm')
      form.append('revision_ref', revisionRef)
      return call('/api/voice', { method: 'POST', body: form })
    },
    asset: (pack) => post(`/api/asset/${pack}`),
    erpMaterials: () => call('/api/enterprise/erp/materials'),
    erpPrs: () => call('/api/enterprise/erp/prs'),
    crmOpportunities: () => call('/api/enterprise/crm/opportunities'),
    fileBlob: async (path) => {
      const r = await fetcher(`/api/files/${path.replace(/^\/+/, '')}`)
      if (!r.ok) throw new ApiError(r.status, r.statusText)
      return r.blob()
    },
  }
}
```

`apps/web/src/data/liveSource.ts`:
```ts
// Minimal read-only Firestore surface used by the live adapter. Clients only read their own
// workspace (`workspaces/{uid}/**`); they never write (spec §14).
import {
  collection,
  doc,
  getDoc,
  onSnapshot,
  orderBy,
  query,
  where,
  type Firestore,
  type QueryConstraint,
} from 'firebase/firestore'

export interface DocSnap {
  id: string
  data: Record<string, unknown>
}
export type Unsubscribe = () => void
export interface CollectionQuery {
  where?: [field: string, op: '==', value: string]
  orderBy?: string
}

export interface LiveSource {
  watchDoc(path: string, cb: (d: DocSnap | null) => void, onError?: (e: Error) => void): Unsubscribe
  watchCollection(
    path: string,
    cb: (docs: DocSnap[]) => void,
    q?: CollectionQuery,
    onError?: (e: Error) => void,
  ): Unsubscribe
  getDoc(path: string): Promise<DocSnap | null>
}

export function firestoreSource(db: Firestore): LiveSource {
  return {
    watchDoc(path, cb, onError) {
      return onSnapshot(
        doc(db, path),
        (s) => cb(s.exists() ? { id: s.id, data: s.data() } : null),
        (e) => onError?.(e),
      )
    },
    watchCollection(path, cb, q, onError) {
      const constraints: QueryConstraint[] = []
      if (q?.where) constraints.push(where(q.where[0], q.where[1], q.where[2]))
      if (q?.orderBy) constraints.push(orderBy(q.orderBy))
      return onSnapshot(
        query(collection(db, path), ...constraints),
        (s) => cb(s.docs.map((d) => ({ id: d.id, data: d.data() }))),
        (e) => onError?.(e),
      )
    },
    async getDoc(path) {
      const s = await getDoc(doc(db, path))
      return s.exists() ? { id: s.id, data: s.data() } : null
    },
  }
}
```

`apps/web/src/data/staticData.ts`:
```ts
// Static-first browsing (spec §11): seed projects, golden runs and the integrations snapshot load
// from Hosting and never depend on Cloud Run, Gemini, Firestore or App Check.
import { normalizeBundle, type Bundle, type IntegrationsSnapshot, type ProjectBundle, type World } from '@/lib/types'

export type JsonFetch = (url: string) => Promise<unknown>

export const fetchJson: JsonFetch = async (url) => {
  const r = await fetch(url)
  if (!r.ok) throw new Error(`${url}: ${r.status}`)
  return r.json()
}

export async function loadWorld(get: JsonFetch = fetchJson): Promise<World> {
  return (await get('/demo/world.json')) as World
}

export async function staticBundle(pid: string, get: JsonFetch = fetchJson): Promise<Bundle> {
  return normalizeBundle((await get(`/demo/projects/${encodeURIComponent(pid)}.json`)) as ProjectBundle)
}

export async function loadIntegrationsSnapshot(get: JsonFetch = fetchJson): Promise<IntegrationsSnapshot> {
  return (await get('/demo/integrations.json')) as IntegrationsSnapshot
}

/** Tender/drawing PDFs are served from Hosting so citations work with the backend down. */
export function docUrl(docId: string): string {
  return `/demo/docs/${encodeURIComponent(docId)}.pdf`
}
```

`apps/web/src/services/services.tsx`:

```tsx
// Dependency injection for everything that talks to the network, so components and tests never
// import firebase.ts directly. Production wiring lives in services/production.ts.
import { createContext, useContext, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import type { LiveSource } from '@/data/liveSource'
import type { JsonFetch } from '@/data/staticData'
import type { ApiClient } from './apiClient'

/** A function call requested by the Gemini Live model (spec §6.5 tools). */
export interface LiveVoiceCall {
  id?: string
  name: string
  args: Record<string, unknown>
}
export interface LiveVoiceHandle {
  stop(): Promise<void>
}
export type StartLiveVoice = (opts: {
  onToolCall: (call: LiveVoiceCall) => Promise<Record<string, unknown>>
}) => Promise<LiveVoiceHandle>

export interface Services {
  api: ApiClient
  getJson: JsonFetch
  /** Firestore reader for the visitor workspace; null when Firebase is unavailable. */
  live: LiveSource | null
  /** Anonymous Firebase uid (signs in on first use). */
  uid: () => Promise<string>
  /** Gemini Live voice session starter; null disables Live and forces push-to-talk. */
  startLiveVoice: StartLiveVoice | null
}

const Ctx = createContext<Services | null>(null)

export function ServicesProvider({ services, children }: { services: Services; children: ReactNode }) {
  return <Ctx.Provider value={services}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>
}

export function useServices(): Services {
  const s = useContext(Ctx)
  if (!s) throw new Error('useServices outside ServicesProvider')
  return s
}
```

`apps/web/src/store/bundles.ts`:
```ts
// One in-browser store holding a ProjectBundle per project view, fed by the static, live and
// replay adapters (spec §11 "one bundle format").
import { create } from 'zustand'
import type { DataSource } from '@/lib/select'
import type { Bundle } from '@/lib/types'

export interface ReplayState {
  cursor: number
  total: number
  playing: boolean
  speed: number
  elapsedS: number
}

export interface BundleEntry {
  status: 'loading' | 'ready' | 'error'
  source: DataSource
  bundle: Bundle | null
  error?: string
  replay?: ReplayState
}

interface BundleStore {
  entries: Record<string, BundleEntry>
  setLoading(key: string, source: DataSource): void
  setBundle(key: string, bundle: Bundle, source: DataSource): void
  setError(key: string, error: string, source: DataSource): void
  setReplay(key: string, replay: ReplayState): void
  clear(key: string): void
}

export const useBundleStore = create<BundleStore>()((set) => ({
  entries: {},
  setLoading: (key, source) =>
    set((s) => ({ entries: { ...s.entries, [key]: { status: 'loading', source, bundle: s.entries[key]?.bundle ?? null } } })),
  setBundle: (key, bundle, source) =>
    set((s) => ({ entries: { ...s.entries, [key]: { ...s.entries[key], status: 'ready', source, bundle, error: undefined } } })),
  setError: (key, error, source) =>
    set((s) => ({ entries: { ...s.entries, [key]: { status: 'error', source, bundle: s.entries[key]?.bundle ?? null, error } } })),
  setReplay: (key, replay) =>
    set((s) => (s.entries[key] ? { entries: { ...s.entries, [key]: { ...s.entries[key], replay } } } : s)),
  clear: (key) =>
    set((s) => {
      const entries = { ...s.entries }
      delete entries[key]
      return { entries }
    }),
}))

export function entryKey(pid: string, opts: { runId?: string | null; replay?: boolean } = {}): string {
  if (opts.runId) return `${pid}:live:${opts.runId}`
  return opts.replay ? `${pid}:replay` : `${pid}:static`
}
```

```ts
// Workspace UI state shared by canvas tabs, drawer, co-pilot and tour.
import { create } from 'zustand'
import type { Autonomy, Citation, WhatIfResult } from '@/lib/types'

export type CanvasTab = 'hall' | 'sld' | 'matrix' | 'options' | 'docs'
export const CANVAS_TABS: { id: CanvasTab; label: string }[] = [
  { id: 'hall', label: '3D Hall' },
  { id: 'sld', label: 'SLD' },
  { id: 'matrix', label: 'Matrix' },
  { id: 'options', label: 'Options' },
  { id: 'docs', label: 'Docs' },
]

export interface WhatIfView {
  result: WhatIfResult
  /** "precomputed" when served from the static bundle (spec §6.4), "engine" when from /api/what-if. */
  origin: 'precomputed' | 'engine'
}

interface UiState {
  tab: CanvasTab
  viewRev: string | null // null = latest revision
  compare: boolean
  findingId: string | null
  focusId: string | null
  citation: Citation | null
  whatIf: WhatIfView | null
  autonomy: Autonomy
  setTab(tab: CanvasTab): void
  setViewRev(rev: string | null): void
  setCompare(on: boolean): void
  openFinding(id: string): void
  closeFinding(): void
  focus(id: string | null): void
  openCitation(c: Citation): void
  closeCitation(): void
  setWhatIf(w: WhatIfView | null): void
  setAutonomy(a: Autonomy): void
  resetWorkspaceUi(): void
}

const initial = {

  viewRev: null,
  compare: false,
  findingId: null,
  focusId: null,
  citation: null,
  whatIf: null,
}

export const useUi = create<UiState>()((set) => ({

  setTab: (tab) => set({ tab }),
  setViewRev: (viewRev) => set({ viewRev }),
  setCompare: (compare) => set({ compare }),
  openFinding: (findingId) => set({ findingId }),
  closeFinding: () => set({ findingId: null }),
  focus: (focusId) => set({ focusId }),
  openCitation: (citation) => set({ citation }),
  closeCitation: () => set({ citation: null }),
  setWhatIf: (whatIf) => set({ whatIf }),
  setAutonomy: (autonomy) => set({ autonomy }),
  resetWorkspaceUi: () => set(initial),
}))
```

`apps/web/src/data/useWorld.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { create } from 'zustand'
import type { Project, World } from '@/lib/types'
import { useServices } from '@/services/services'
import { loadWorld } from './staticData'

interface WorldStore {
  world: World | null
  error: string | null
  loading: boolean
}
export const useWorldStore = create<WorldStore>()(() => ({ world: null, error: null, loading: false }))

/** Loads /demo/world.json once per session (static, works with the backend down). */
export function useWorld(): WorldStore {
  const { getJson } = useServices()
  const state = useWorldStore()
  useEffect(() => {
    const s = useWorldStore.getState()
    if (s.world || s.loading) return
    useWorldStore.setState({ loading: true })
    loadWorld(getJson)
      .then((world) => useWorldStore.setState({ world, loading: false, error: null }))
      .catch((e: Error) => useWorldStore.setState({ loading: false, error: e.message }))
  }, [getJson])
  return state
}

export function useWorldProject(pid: string | undefined): Project | null {
  const { world } = useWorld()
  return world?.projects.find((p) => p.id === pid) ?? null
}
```

```tsx
import { render, type RenderResult } from '@testing-library/react'
import type { ReactElement } from 'react'
import { MemoryRouter, Route, Routes } from 'react-router'
import { vi } from 'vitest'
import { TooltipProvider } from '@/components/ui/tooltip'
import { ApiError, type ApiClient } from '@/services/apiClient'
import { ServicesProvider, type Services } from '@/services/services'
import { useBundleStore } from '@/store/bundles'
import { useUi } from '@/store/ui'
import hallMini from './fixtures/hall-mini.json'
import integrations from './fixtures/integrations.json'
import world from './fixtures/world.json'

const offline = () => Promise.reject(new ApiError(503, 'offline'))

export function fakeApi(overrides: Partial<ApiClient> = {}): ApiClient {
  const base: ApiClient = {
    health: vi.fn(offline),
    ensureWorkspace: vi.fn(offline),
    resetWorkspace: vi.fn(offline),
    startRun: vi.fn(offline),
    planGate: vi.fn(offline),
    optionGate: vi.fn(offline),
    reviewGate: vi.fn(offline),
    resume: vi.fn(offline),
    whatIf: vi.fn(offline),
    explain: vi.fn(offline),
    voice: vi.fn(offline),
    asset: vi.fn(offline),
    erpMaterials: vi.fn(offline),
    erpPrs: vi.fn(offline),
    crmOpportunities: vi.fn(offline),
    fileBlob: vi.fn(offline),
  }
  return { ...base, ...overrides }
}

export const STATIC_FILES: Record<string, unknown> = {
  '/demo/world.json': world,
  '/demo/projects/hall-a.json': hallMini,
  '/demo/integrations.json': integrations,
}

export function fakeServices(overrides: Partial<Services> = {}): Services {
  return {
    api: fakeApi(),
    getJson: vi.fn(async (url: string) => {
      if (url in STATIC_FILES) return structuredClone(STATIC_FILES[url])
      throw new Error(`${url}: 404`)
    }),
    live: null,
    uid: vi.fn(async () => 'visitor-1'),
    startLiveVoice: null,
    ...overrides,
  }
}

export function resetStores(): void {
  useBundleStore.setState({ entries: {} })
  useUi.getState().resetWorkspaceUi()
}

/** Renders `ui` at `route` (matched by `path`) inside router, services and tooltip providers. */
export function renderWithProviders(
  ui: ReactElement,
  { route = '/', path = '*', services = fakeServices() }: { route?: string; path?: string; services?: Services } = {},
): RenderResult & { services: Services } {
  resetStores()
  const result = render(
    <ServicesProvider services={services}>
      <TooltipProvider delayDuration={0}>
        <MemoryRouter initialEntries={[route]}>
          <Routes>
            <Route path={path} element={ui} />
          </Routes>
        </MemoryRouter>
      </TooltipProvider>
    </ServicesProvider>,
  )
  return { ...result, services }
}
```

### Task 4: Live and replay adapters, `useProjectData` (Tue)

The live adapter assembles a `ProjectBundle` from Firestore snapshots of the visitor workspace (with the §6.2 attempt filter, findings per revision, brief/model refs); replay plays a static bundle's events on a compressed timer; one hook loads any of the three sources into the store. Lease watchdog calls resume when the heartbeat goes stale.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/data/liveBundle.ts`, `apps/web/src/data/replay.ts`, `apps/web/src/data/useLeaseWatchdog.ts`, `apps/web/src/data/useProjectData.ts`
- Test: `apps/web/src/data/liveBundle.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/data/replay.test.ts`, `apps/web/src/data/useLeaseWatchdog.test.tsx`, `apps/web/src/data/useProjectData.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 `visibleEvents, artifactPayload, findingPayload, planPayload, refTail`; Task 3 `LiveSource`, `Services`, `useBundleStore`, `entryKey`, `staticBundle`.
- Produces:
  - `@/data/liveBundle`: `LiveParts`, `deriveState(run, approvals, fallback)`, `assembleLive(parts): Bundle`, `LiveOptions {uid, runId, project, loadJson}`, `subscribeLiveBundle(src, opts, onChange, onError?) → Unsubscribe`. Subscribes to `workspaces/{uid}/runs/{rid}` (doc), `…/steps`, `…/events` (orderBy `seq`), `workspaces/{uid}/revisions` (where `project_id == pid`), each `…/revisions/{rev}/findings`, `workspaces/{uid}/approvals`, `workspaces/{uid}/prs` (where `project_id == pid`), `workspaces/{uid}/crm`.
  - `@/data/replay`: `frameAt(full, cursor): Bundle`, `ReplayControls {play, pause, seek, setSpeed, dispose, state}`, `createReplay(full, {speed?, autoplay?, onFrame})`.
  - `@/data/useLeaseWatchdog`: `leaseIsStale(run, nowS, maxAgeS=60)`, `useLeaseWatchdog(run, resume, {intervalMs?, now?})`.
  - `@/data/useProjectData`: `ProjectDataOptions {runId?, replay?, project?, speed?}`, `ProjectData {key, status, source, bundle, error?, replay?, controls}`, `useProjectData(pid, opts)`.

`apps/web/src/data/liveBundle.test.ts`:
```ts
import { fixtureBundle } from '@/test/fixtures'
import type { Bundle, Project } from '@/lib/types'
import type { CollectionQuery, DocSnap, LiveSource } from './liveSource'
import { subscribeLiveBundle } from './liveBundle'
import { leaseIsStale } from './useLeaseWatchdog'

/** In-memory LiveSource: tests push Firestore-like snapshots into watched paths. */
function fakeSource() {
  const docs = new Map<string, (d: DocSnap | null) => void>()
  const cols = new Map<string, { cb: (d: DocSnap[]) => void; q?: CollectionQuery }>()
  const stored = new Map<string, Record<string, unknown>>()
  const src: LiveSource = {
    watchDoc: (path, cb) => {
      docs.set(path, cb)
      return () => docs.delete(path)
    },
    watchCollection: (path, cb, q) => {
      cols.set(path, { cb, q })
      return () => cols.delete(path)
    },
    getDoc: async (path) => (stored.has(path) ? { id: path.split('/').pop()!, data: stored.get(path)! } : null),
  }
  return {
    src,
    docs,
    cols,
    stored,
    doc: (path: string, data: Record<string, unknown>) => docs.get(path)!({ id: path.split('/').pop()!, data }),
    col: (path: string, items: Record<string, unknown>[]) =>
      cols.get(path)!.cb(items.map((data, i) => ({ id: String(data.id ?? data.seq ?? i), data }))),
  }
}

const project: Project = {
  id: 'hall-b',
  name: 'Deccan AI Campus · Hall B',
  client: 'Deccan Compute',
  asset_pack: 'datahall',
  state: 'not_started',
  sample: true,
  updated_at: 0,
}

test('assembles a live bundle from workspace snapshots', async () => {
  const fx = fixtureBundle()
  const f = fakeSource()
  const seen: Bundle[] = []
  const base = 'workspaces/u1'
  const run = `${base}/runs/run-1`
  f.stored.set(`${run}/artifacts/brief`, fx.brief as unknown as Record<string, unknown>)
  const off = subscribeLiveBundle(f.src, { uid: 'u1', runId: 'run-1', project, loadJson: async () => null }, (b) => seen.push(b))

  expect(f.cols.get(`${run}/events`)?.q).toEqual({ orderBy: 'seq' })
  expect(f.cols.get(`${base}/revisions`)?.q).toEqual({ where: ['project_id', '==', 'hall-b'] })

  f.doc(run, { ...fx.runs[0], id: 'run-1', project_id: 'hall-b', status: 'running', gate: null })
  f.col(`${run}/steps`, fx.steps as unknown as Record<string, unknown>[])
  const events = fx.events.map((e) =>
    e.kind === 'artifact' && (e.payload as { type?: string }).type === 'brief'
      ? { ...e, payload: { type: 'brief', ref: `${run}/artifacts/brief` } }
      : e,
  )
  f.col(`${run}/events`, events as unknown as Record<string, unknown>[])
  f.col(`${base}/revisions`, fx.revisions.map((r) => ({ ...r, project_id: 'hall-b' })) as unknown as Record<string, unknown>[])
  expect(f.cols.has(`${base}/revisions/r1/findings`)).toBe(true)
  f.col(`${base}/revisions/r1/findings`, fx.findings.r1 as unknown as Record<string, unknown>[])
  await Promise.resolve()
  await Promise.resolve()

  const b = seen[seen.length - 1]
  expect(b.project.state).toBe('running')
  expect(b.runs[0].id).toBe('run-1')
  expect(b.events.map((e) => e.seq)).not.toContain(14) // stale attempt filtered
  expect(b.revisions.map((r) => r.id)).toEqual(['r1', 'r2'])
  expect(b.findings.r1).toHaveLength(6)
  expect(b.findings.r2).toEqual([])
  expect(b.brief?.requirements).toHaveLength(3)

  f.col(`${base}/approvals`, [{ ...fx.approvals[0] }])
  expect(seen[seen.length - 1].project.state).toBe('signed')

  off()
  expect(f.cols.size).toBe(0)
  expect(f.docs.size).toBe(0)
})

test('lease is stale only for running runs with an old heartbeat', () => {
  const run = { ...fixtureBundle().runs[0], status: 'running' as const, lease: { owner: 'w', attempt: 1, heartbeat_at: 1000 } }
  expect(leaseIsStale(run, 1059)).toBe(false)
  expect(leaseIsStale(run, 1061)).toBe(true)
  expect(leaseIsStale({ ...run, status: 'at_gate' }, 5000)).toBe(false)
})
```

`apps/web/src/data/replay.test.ts`:
```ts
import { fixtureBundle } from '@/test/fixtures'
import { createReplay, frameAt } from './replay'

test('frame 0 shows nothing recorded yet', () => {
  const f = frameAt(fixtureBundle(), 0)
  expect(f.events).toEqual([])
  expect(f.revisions).toEqual([])
  expect(f.brief).toBeNull()
  expect(f.runs[0].status).toBe('running')
  expect(f.approvals).toEqual([])
})

test('revisions and findings appear as their events play', () => {
  const full = fixtureBundle()
  const f = frameAt(full, 16) // through seq 16: revision r1 artifact + first finding
  expect(f.brief).not.toBeNull()
  expect((f.runs[0].options ?? []).map((o) => o.id)).toEqual(['2N', 'DR'])
  expect(f.revisions.map((r) => r.id)).toEqual(['r1'])
  expect(f.findings.r1.map((x) => x.id)).toEqual(['R05:FA-01'])
  expect(f.steps.find((s) => s.id === 'p3.check')?.status).toBe('running')
})

test('the final frame is the recorded bundle', () => {
  const full = fixtureBundle()
  expect(frameAt(full, full.events.length)).toBe(full)
})

test('createReplay plays events on a compressed timer and reports elapsed recorded time', () => {
  vi.useFakeTimers()
  const full = fixtureBundle()
  const frames: number[] = []
  const c = createReplay(full, { speed: 1000, onFrame: (_b, s) => frames.push(s.cursor) })
  expect(c.state().playing).toBe(true)
  vi.advanceTimersByTime(300)
  expect(c.state().cursor).toBe(1)
  vi.advanceTimersByTime(60_000)
  expect(c.state().cursor).toBe(full.events.length)
  expect(c.state().playing).toBe(false)
  expect(c.state().elapsedS).toBe(520)
  expect(frames[0]).toBe(0)
  c.seek(5)
  expect(c.state().cursor).toBe(5)
  c.dispose()
})
```

```tsx
import { renderHook } from '@testing-library/react'
import { fixtureBundle } from '@/test/fixtures'
import { useLeaseWatchdog } from './useLeaseWatchdog'

test('calls resume when the heartbeat is older than 60 s, at most once a minute', () => {
  vi.useFakeTimers()
  let now = 2000
  const resume = vi.fn(async () => ({ resumed: true }))
  const run = { ...fixtureBundle().runs[0], id: 'run-1', status: 'running' as const, lease: { owner: 'w', attempt: 1, heartbeat_at: 1950 } }
  renderHook(() => useLeaseWatchdog(run, resume, { intervalMs: 1000, now: () => now }))
  vi.advanceTimersByTime(1000)
  expect(resume).not.toHaveBeenCalled() // 50 s old
  now = 2015
  vi.advanceTimersByTime(1000)
  expect(resume).toHaveBeenCalledWith('run-1')
  now = 2030
  vi.advanceTimersByTime(1000)
  expect(resume).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
  now = 2080
  vi.advanceTimersByTime(1000)
  expect(resume).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(2)
})
```
