```tsx
import { act, renderHook, waitFor } from '@testing-library/react'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { ServicesProvider, type Services } from '@/services/services'
import { useBundleStore } from '@/store/bundles'
import { fakeServices, resetStores } from '@/test/render'
import { useProjectData, type ProjectDataOptions } from './useProjectData'

function hook(services: Services, pid: string, opts: ProjectDataOptions = {}) {
  resetStores()
  const wrapper = ({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) => <ServicesProvider services={services}>{children}</ServicesProvider>
  return renderHook(() => useProjectData(pid, opts), { wrapper })
}

test('static: puts /demo/projects/{pid}.json in the store once', async () => {
  const services = fakeServices()
  const { result, rerender } = hook(services, 'hall-a')
  expect(result.current.status).toBe('loading')
  await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.status).toBe('ready'))
  expect(result.current.source).toBe('static')
  expect(result.current.bundle?.project.id).toBe('hall-a')
  expect(useBundleStore.getState().entries['hall-a:static'].status).toBe('ready')
  rerender()
  expect(services.getJson).toHaveBeenCalledTimes(1)
})

test('static: a missing bundle surfaces an error', async () => {
  const { result } = hook(fakeServices(), 'nope')
  await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.status).toBe('error'))
  expect(result.current.error).toContain('404')
})

test('replay: starts at frame 0 and exposes controls', async () => {
  const { result } = hook(fakeServices(), 'hall-a', { replay: true, speed: 1000 })
  await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.controls).not.toBeNull())
  expect(result.current.source).toBe('replay')
  act(() => result.current.controls!.pause())
  act(() => result.current.controls!.seek(0))
  expect(result.current.bundle?.events).toEqual([])
  expect(result.current.replay?.total).toBe(25)
  act(() => result.current.controls!.seek(25))
  expect(result.current.bundle?.revisions).toHaveLength(2)
})

test('live: without a Firestore source the view reports an error instead of hanging', async () => {
  const { result } = hook(fakeServices({ live: null }), 'hall-b', { runId: 'run-1' })
  await waitFor(() => expect(result.current.status).toBe('error'))
  expect(result.current.source).toBe('live')
})
```

`apps/web/src/data/liveBundle.ts`:
```ts
// Live adapter: Firestore onSnapshot over the visitor workspace, assembled into a ProjectBundle
// (spec §11, §14). Firestore documents are `model_dump()`s of the contracts (snake_case).
import { artifactPayload } from '@/lib/events'
import { visibleEvents } from '@/lib/events'
import type {
  Approval,
  Bundle,
  CrmOverlay,
  DesignBrief,
  DesignModel,
  Finding,
  Project,
  ProjectState,
  PurchaseRequisition,
  Revision,
  RunEvent,
  RunRecord,
  StepRecord,
} from '@/lib/types'
import type { DocSnap, LiveSource, Unsubscribe } from './liveSource'

export interface LiveParts {
  project: Project
  run: RunRecord | null
  steps: StepRecord[]
  events: RunEvent[]
  revisions: Revision[]
  findings: Record<string, Finding[]>
  approvals: Approval[]
  prs: PurchaseRequisition[]
  crm: CrmOverlay[]
  brief: DesignBrief | null
  models: Record<string, DesignModel>
}

export function deriveState(run: RunRecord | null, approvals: Approval[], fallback: ProjectState): ProjectState {
  if (!run) return fallback
  if (approvals.some((a) => a.decision === 'approved')) return 'signed'
  if (run.status === 'at_gate' && run.gate === 'review') return 'in_review'
  if (run.status === 'running' || run.status === 'at_gate') return 'running'
  if (run.status === 'done') return 'checked'
  return fallback
}

export function assembleLive(p: LiveParts): Bundle {
  const revisions = p.revisions
    .filter((r) => r.project_id === p.project.id)
    .map((r) => ({ ...r, model: r.model ?? p.models[r.id] ?? null }))
    .sort((a, b) => a.created_at - b.created_at)
  const revIds = new Set(revisions.map((r) => r.id))
  const approvals = p.approvals.filter((a) => revIds.has(a.revision))
  const findings: Record<string, Finding[]> = {}
  for (const id of revIds) findings[id] = p.findings[id] ?? []
  return {
    project: { ...p.project, state: deriveState(p.run, approvals, p.project.state) },
    brief: p.brief,
    runs: p.run ? [p.run] : [],
    steps: p.steps,
    events: visibleEvents(p.events, p.steps),
    revisions,
    findings,
    approvals,
    prs: p.prs.filter((x) => x.project_id === p.project.id),
    crm: [...p.crm].sort((a, b) => b.updated_at - a.updated_at)[0] ?? null,
    what_ifs: [],
  }
}

const withId = <T,>(d: DocSnap): T => ({ ...d.data, id: (d.data.id as string | undefined) ?? d.id }) as unknown as T

export interface LiveOptions {
  uid: string
  runId: string
  project: Project
  /** Resolves a brief/model reference that is a URL or storage path (not a Firestore path). */
  loadJson: (ref: string) => Promise<unknown>
}

export function subscribeLiveBundle(
  src: LiveSource,
  opts: LiveOptions,
  onChange: (b: Bundle) => void,
  onError: (e: Error) => void = () => {},
): Unsubscribe {
  const base = `workspaces/${opts.uid}`
  const runPath = `${base}/runs/${opts.runId}`
  const parts: LiveParts = {
    project: opts.project,
    run: null,
    steps: [],
    events: [],
    revisions: [],
    findings: {},
    approvals: [],
    prs: [],
    crm: [],
    brief: null,
    models: {},
  }
  const unsubs: Unsubscribe[] = []
  const findingSubs = new Map<string, Unsubscribe>()
  const pending = new Set<string>()
  let closed = false
  const emit = () => {
    if (!closed) onChange(assembleLive(parts))
  }

  const resolveRef = async (ref: string): Promise<unknown> => {
    if (ref.startsWith('workspaces/')) return (await src.getDoc(ref))?.data ?? null
    return opts.loadJson(ref)
  }
  const loadBrief = () => {
    const ev = parts.events.find((e) => artifactPayload(e)?.type === 'brief')
    const ref = ev && artifactPayload(ev)!.ref
    if (!ref || parts.brief || pending.has(ref)) return
    pending.add(ref)
    resolveRef(ref)
      .then((d) => {
        if (d) parts.brief = d as DesignBrief
        emit()
      })
      .catch(onError)
  }
  const loadModels = () => {
    for (const r of parts.revisions) {
      if (r.model || !r.model_url || parts.models[r.id] || pending.has(r.model_url)) continue
      pending.add(r.model_url)
      resolveRef(r.model_url)
        .then((m) => {
          if (m) parts.models[r.id] = m as DesignModel
          emit()
        })
        .catch(onError)
    }
  }
  const syncFindings = () => {
    for (const r of parts.revisions) {
      if (findingSubs.has(r.id)) continue
      findingSubs.set(
        r.id,
        src.watchCollection(
          `${base}/revisions/${r.id}/findings`,
          (docs) => {
            parts.findings[r.id] = docs.map((d) => withId<Finding>(d))
            emit()
          },
          undefined,
          onError,
        ),
      )
    }
  }

  unsubs.push(
    src.watchDoc(runPath, (d) => {
      parts.run = d ? withId<RunRecord>(d) : null
      emit()
    }, onError),
    src.watchCollection(`${runPath}/steps`, (docs) => {
      parts.steps = docs.map((d) => withId<StepRecord>(d))
      emit()
    }, undefined, onError),
    src.watchCollection(`${runPath}/events`, (docs) => {
      parts.events = docs.map((d) => d.data as unknown as RunEvent)
      loadBrief()
      emit()
    }, { orderBy: 'seq' }, onError),
    src.watchCollection(`${base}/revisions`, (docs) => {
      parts.revisions = docs.map((d) => withId<Revision>(d))
      syncFindings()
      loadModels()
      emit()
    }, { where: ['project_id', '==', opts.project.id] }, onError),
    src.watchCollection(`${base}/approvals`, (docs) => {
      parts.approvals = docs.map((d) => withId<Approval>(d))
      emit()
    }, undefined, onError),
    src.watchCollection(`${base}/prs`, (docs) => {
      parts.prs = docs.map((d) => withId<PurchaseRequisition>(d))
      emit()
    }, { where: ['project_id', '==', opts.project.id] }, onError),
    src.watchCollection(`${base}/crm`, (docs) => {
      parts.crm = docs.map((d) => ({ opportunity_id: d.id, ...d.data }) as unknown as CrmOverlay)
      emit()
    }, undefined, onError),
  )

  return () => {
    closed = true
    unsubs.forEach((u) => u())
    findingSubs.forEach((u) => u())
  }
}
```

`apps/web/src/data/replay.ts`:
```ts
// Replay = a static bundle's events played back on a timer (spec §11). frameAt() derives the
// bundle as it looked after `cursor` events; nothing is computed beyond revealing recorded data.
import { artifactPayload, findingPayload, planPayload, refTail } from '@/lib/events'
import type { Bundle, Finding } from '@/lib/types'
import type { ReplayState } from '@/store/bundles'

/** seq of each finding event → revision it belongs to (payload.rev, else the latest revision artifact). */
function attribution(full: Bundle): Map<number, string> {
  const out = new Map<number, string>()
  let cur: string | null = null
  for (const e of full.events) {
    const a = artifactPayload(e)
    if (a?.type === 'revision') cur = refTail(a.ref)
    const f = findingPayload(e)
    if (f) {
      const rev = f.rev ?? cur ?? full.revisions[0]?.id
      if (rev) out.set(e.seq, rev)
    }
  }
  return out
}

export function frameAt(full: Bundle, cursor: number): Bundle {
  const total = full.events.length
  if (cursor >= total) return full
  const played = full.events.slice(0, Math.max(0, cursor))
  const attr = attribution(full)
  const has = (pred: (e: Bundle['events'][number]) => boolean) => played.some(pred)
  const hasAny = (pred: (e: Bundle['events'][number]) => boolean) => full.events.some(pred)

  const revealed = new Set<string>()
  const shown = new Map<string, Set<string>>()
  for (const e of played) {
    const a = artifactPayload(e)
    if (a?.type === 'revision') revealed.add(refTail(a.ref))
    const f = findingPayload(e)
    const rev = attr.get(e.seq)
    if (f && rev) {
      revealed.add(rev)
      shown.set(rev, (shown.get(rev) ?? new Set()).add(f.finding_id))
    }
  }
  const revsWithEvents = new Set(attr.values())
  const findings: Record<string, Finding[]> = {}
  for (const rev of revealed) {
    const all = full.findings[rev] ?? []
    findings[rev] = revsWithEvents.has(rev) ? all.filter((f) => shown.get(rev)?.has(f.id)) : all
  }

  const isBrief = (e: Bundle['events'][number]) => artifactPayload(e)?.type === 'brief'
  const isOptions = (e: Bundle['events'][number]) => artifactPayload(e)?.type === 'options'
  const isPlan = (e: Bundle['events'][number]) => planPayload(e) !== null
  const inP3 = has((e) => e.phase === 'p3' || e.phase === 'p4')
  const inP4 = has((e) => e.phase === 'p4')
  const last = played[played.length - 1]
  const playedSteps = new Set(played.map((e) => e.step))

  return {
    ...full,
    project: { ...full.project, state: 'running' },
    brief: has(isBrief) || (!hasAny(isBrief) && played.length > 0) ? full.brief : null,
    runs: full.runs.map((r) => ({
      ...r,
      status: 'running' as const,
      phase: last?.phase ?? 'p1',
      gate: null,
      plan: has(isPlan) || !hasAny(isPlan) ? r.plan : [],
      open_questions: has(isPlan) || !hasAny(isPlan) ? r.open_questions : [],
      options: has(isOptions) || (!hasAny(isOptions) && inP3) ? r.options : [],
      selected_option: inP3 ? r.selected_option : null,
      finished_at: null,
    })),
    steps: full.steps
      .filter((s) => playedSteps.has(s.id))
      .map((s) => ({ ...s, status: s.id === last?.step ? ('running' as const) : s.status, finished_at: s.id === last?.step ? null : s.finished_at })),
    events: played,
    revisions: full.revisions.filter((r) => revealed.has(r.id)),
    findings,
    approvals: inP4 ? full.approvals : [],
    prs: inP4 ? full.prs : [],
    crm: inP4 ? full.crm : null,
  }
}

export interface ReplayControls {
  play(): void
  pause(): void
  seek(cursor: number): void
  setSpeed(speed: number): void
  dispose(): void
  state(): ReplayState
}

/** Recorded gaps are compressed by `speed` and clamped so dead air never exceeds 1.5 s. */
export function createReplay(
  full: Bundle,
  opts: { speed?: number; autoplay?: boolean; onFrame: (b: Bundle, s: ReplayState) => void },
): ReplayControls {
  const events = full.events
  let cursor = 0
  let speed = opts.speed ?? 8
  let playing = false
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | null = null

  const state = (): ReplayState => ({
    cursor,
    total: events.length,
    playing,
    speed,
    elapsedS: cursor > 0 ? events[cursor - 1].ts - events[0].ts : 0,
  })
  const emit = () => opts.onFrame(frameAt(full, cursor), state())
  const delayMs = () => {
    if (cursor === 0) return 300
    const gapS = events[cursor].ts - events[cursor - 1].ts
    return Math.min(1500, Math.max(40, (gapS * 1000) / speed))
  }
  const stop = () => {
    if (timer) clearTimeout(timer)
    timer = null
  }
  const tick = () => {
    if (cursor >= events.length) {
      playing = false
      emit()
      return
    }
    timer = setTimeout(() => {
      cursor += 1
      emit()
      tick()
    }, delayMs())
  }

  const controls: ReplayControls = {
    play() {
      if (playing) return
      if (cursor >= events.length) cursor = 0
      playing = true
      emit()
      tick()
    },
    pause() {
      playing = false
      stop()
      emit()
    },
    seek(n) {
      cursor = Math.max(0, Math.min(events.length, n))
      emit()
      if (playing) {
        stop()
        tick()
      }
    },
    setSpeed(s) {
      speed = s
      emit()
    },
    dispose() {
      playing = false
      stop()
    },
    state,
  }
  emit()
  if (opts.autoplay ?? true) controls.play()
  return controls
}
```

`apps/web/src/data/useLeaseWatchdog.ts`:
```ts
// Spec §6.2: if the running phase's lease heartbeat is older than 60 s, the client calls
// POST /api/runs/{rid}/resume (at most once per minute).
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import type { RunRecord } from '@/lib/types'

export function leaseIsStale(run: RunRecord | null, nowS: number, maxAgeS = 60): boolean {
  if (!run || run.status !== 'running' || !run.lease) return false
  const hb = (run.lease.heartbeat_at ?? run.lease.heartbeatAt) as number | undefined
  return typeof hb === 'number' && nowS - hb > maxAgeS
}

export function useLeaseWatchdog(
  run: RunRecord | null,
  resume: (runId: string) => Promise<unknown>,
  opts: { intervalMs?: number; now?: () => number } = {},
): void {
  const runRef = useRef(run)
  const lastCall = useRef(0)
  runRef.current = run
  const intervalMs = opts.intervalMs ?? 15_000
  const now = opts.now ?? (() => Date.now() / 1000)

  useEffect(() => {
    const id = setInterval(() => {
      const r = runRef.current
      const t = now()
      if (r && leaseIsStale(r, t) && t - lastCall.current >= 60) {
        lastCall.current = t
        resume(r.id).catch(() => {
          // resume is best-effort; the next tick retries after 60 s
        })
      }
    }, intervalMs)
    return () => clearInterval(id)
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [intervalMs, resume])
}
```

`apps/web/src/data/useProjectData.ts`:
```ts
// Loads a project view into the bundle store from one of three adapters:
// static (/demo/projects/{pid}.json), live (Firestore, ?run=rid) or replay (?replay=1).
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import type { DataSource } from '@/lib/select'
import type { Bundle, Project } from '@/lib/types'
import { useServices } from '@/services/services'
import { entryKey, useBundleStore, type ReplayState } from '@/store/bundles'
import { subscribeLiveBundle } from './liveBundle'
import { createReplay, type ReplayControls } from './replay'
import { staticBundle } from './staticData'

export interface ProjectDataOptions {
  runId?: string | null
  replay?: boolean
  project?: Project | null
  speed?: number
}

export interface ProjectData {
  key: string
  status: 'loading' | 'ready' | 'error'
  source: DataSource
  bundle: Bundle | null
  error?: string
  replay?: ReplayState
  controls: ReplayControls | null
}

const placeholderProject = (pid: string): Project => ({
  id: pid,
  name: pid,
  client: '',
  asset_pack: 'datahall',
  state: 'running',
  sample: false,
  updated_at: 0,
})

export function useProjectData(pid: string, opts: ProjectDataOptions = {}): ProjectData {
  const services = useServices()
  const source: DataSource = opts.runId ? 'live' : opts.replay ? 'replay' : 'static'
  const key = entryKey(pid, { runId: opts.runId, replay: opts.replay })
  const entry = useBundleStore((s) => s.entries[key])
  const [controls, setControls] = useState<ReplayControls | null>(null)
  const projectRef = useRef(opts.project)
  projectRef.current = opts.project

  useEffect(() => {
    const store = useBundleStore.getState()
    let cancelled = false
    let cleanup: () => void = () => {}

    if (source === 'static') {
      if (store.entries[key]?.status === 'ready') return
      store.setLoading(key, source)
      staticBundle(pid, services.getJson)
        .then((b) => !cancelled && useBundleStore.getState().setBundle(key, b, source))
        .catch((e: Error) => !cancelled && useBundleStore.getState().setError(key, e.message, source))
    }

    if (source === 'replay') {
      store.setLoading(key, source)
      staticBundle(pid, services.getJson)
        .then((full) => {
          if (cancelled) return
          const c = createReplay(full, {
            speed: opts.speed,
            onFrame: (b, s) => {
              const st = useBundleStore.getState()
              st.setBundle(key, b, source)
              st.setReplay(key, s)
            },
          })
          setControls(c)
          cleanup = () => c.dispose()
        })
        .catch((e: Error) => !cancelled && useBundleStore.getState().setError(key, e.message, source))
    }

    if (source === 'live') {
      const live = services.live
      if (!live) {
        store.setError(key, 'Live data is unavailable in this environment', source)
        return
      }
      store.setLoading(key, source)
      services
        .uid()
        .then((uid) => {
          if (cancelled) return
          cleanup = subscribeLiveBundle(
            live,
            {
              uid,
              runId: opts.runId!,
              project: projectRef.current ?? placeholderProject(pid),
              loadJson: async (ref) =>
                ref.startsWith('http') || ref.startsWith('/')
                  ? services.getJson(ref)
                  : JSON.parse(await (await services.api.fileBlob(ref)).text()),
            },
            (b) => useBundleStore.getState().setBundle(key, b, source),
            (e) => useBundleStore.getState().setError(key, e.message, source),
          )
        })
        .catch((e: Error) => !cancelled && useBundleStore.getState().setError(key, e.message, source))
    }

    return () => {
      cancelled = true
      cleanup()
      setControls(null)
    }
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [key])

  return {
    key,
    status: entry?.status ?? 'loading',
    source,
    bundle: entry?.bundle ?? null,
    error: entry?.error,
    replay: entry?.replay,
    controls,
  }
}
```

```ts
import { currentShot, initPresentationMode, isPresentationMode } from './flags'

test('?present=1 turns presentation mode on for the session; ?present=0 turns it off', () => {
  expect(initPresentationMode('')).toBe(false)
  expect(initPresentationMode('?present=1')).toBe(true)
  expect(document.documentElement.dataset.present).toBe('1')
  expect(initPresentationMode('')).toBe(true) // persisted in sessionStorage
  expect(isPresentationMode()).toBe(true)
```

`apps/web/src/pages/Dashboard.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react'
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event'
import { Route, Routes } from 'react-router'
import { useWorldStore } from '@/data/useWorld'
import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render'
import { AppShell } from '@/shell/AppShell'
import { Dashboard } from './Dashboard'
import { Landing } from './Landing'

beforeEach(() => useWorldStore.setState({ world: null, error: null, loading: false }))

function shellAt(route: string) {
  return renderWithProviders(
    <Routes>
      <Route path="/app" element={<AppShell />}>
        <Route index element={<Dashboard />} />
        <Route path="p/:pid" element={<p>workspace</p>} />
      </Route>
    </Routes>,
    { route },
  )
}
```

`apps/web/src/lib/flags.ts`:
```ts
import { readJson, writeJson } from './storage'

/**
 * Presentation mode (`?present=1`, persists for the browser session; `?present=0` turns it off):
 * hides dev affordances ([data-dev]) and enlarges type for screen recording.
 */
export function initPresentationMode(search: string = window.location.search): boolean {
  const q = new URLSearchParams(search).get('present')
  if (q === '1') writeJson('present', true, sessionStorage)
  if (q === '0') writeJson('present', false, sessionStorage)
  const on = readJson<boolean>('present', false, sessionStorage)
  document.documentElement.dataset.present = on ? '1' : '0'
  return on
}

export function isPresentationMode(): boolean {
  return document.documentElement.dataset.present === '1'
}

/** Optional film shot (`?shot=cold-open`) used by the 3D view for the video's first beat. */
export function currentShot(search: string = window.location.search): string | null {
  return new URLSearchParams(search).get('shot')
}
```

```tsx
import { Archive, Circle, CircleCheck, Eye, FileText, LoaderCircle, Stamp, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import { cn } from 'cn'
import type { ProjectState } from '@/lib/types'

const META: Record<ProjectState, { label: string; icon: LucideIcon; cls: string }> = {
  not_started: { label: 'Not started', icon: Circle, cls: 'text-zinc-400' },
  running: { label: 'Running', icon: LoaderCircle, cls: 'text-sky-300' },
  in_review: { label: 'In review', icon: Eye, cls: 'text-amber-300' },
  checked: { label: 'Checked', icon: CircleCheck, cls: 'text-emerald-400' },
  draft: { label: 'Draft', icon: FileText, cls: 'text-zinc-300' },
  signed: { label: 'Signed', icon: Stamp, cls: 'text-emerald-300' },
  archived: { label: 'Archived', icon: Archive, cls: 'text-zinc-500' },
}

export function ProjectStateBadge({ state, className }: { state: ProjectState; className?: string }) {
  const m = META[state]
  const Icon = m.icon
  return (
    <span className={cn('inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs font-medium', m.cls, className)}>
      <Icon aria-hidden className={cn('size-3.5', state === 'running' && 'animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none')} />
      {m.label}
    </span>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { motion } from 'motion/react'
import { ArrowRight, CirclePlay, Zap } from 'lucide-react'
import { Link } from 'react-router'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { FICTIONAL_TEXT } from '@/components/Labels'

// Effects live only here (spec §12). The background traces are decorative and static under reduced motion.
export function Landing() {
  return (
    <div className="relative flex min-h-dvh flex-col overflow-hidden">
      <div
        aria-hidden
        className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-0 bg-[radial-gradient(ellipse_at_top,rgba(56,189,248,0.14),transparent_55%),radial-gradient(ellipse_at_bottom_right,rgba(251,191,36,0.10),transparent_50%)]"
      />
      <svg aria-hidden className="pointer-events-none absolute inset-x-0 top-24 mx-auto h-64 w-[56rem] max-w-full opacity-40">
        {[0, 1, 2, 3].map((i) => (
          <motion.path
            key={i}
            d={`M ${40 + i * 220} 10 V 90 H ${150 + i * 200} V 240`}
            fill="none"
            stroke={i % 2 ? '#60a5fa' : '#fbbf24'}
            strokeWidth={1.5}
            initial={{ pathLength: 0 }}
            animate={{ pathLength: 1 }}
            transition={{ duration: 2.2, delay: i * 0.25, ease: 'easeInOut' }}
          />
        ))}
      </svg>
      <main className="relative z-10 mx-auto flex max-w-3xl flex-1 flex-col items-center justify-center gap-6 px-6 text-center">
        <p className="rounded-full border border-zinc-700 px-3 py-1 text-xs text-zinc-400">
          AI designs. Physics checks. An engineer signs.
        </p>
        <h1 className="text-4xl font-semibold tracking-tight text-zinc-50 sm:text-5xl">
          Weeks of senior-engineer design work, in one reviewed, signed session.
        </h1>
        <p className="max-w-xl text-base text-zinc-400">
          Gemini agents read the tender and design power and cooling for an AI data hall. A deterministic checker proves
          every number. The engineer decides and signs, and procurement and sales pick it up from there.
        </p>
        <Button asChild size="lg" className="mt-2">
          <Link to="/app">
            Enter the demo workspace (no login) <ArrowRight aria-hidden />
          </Link>
        </Button>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center justify-center gap-3 text-sm">
          <Button asChild variant="outline" size="sm">
            <Link to="/app/p/hall-b">
              <Zap aria-hidden /> Run it live
            </Link>
          </Button>
          <Button asChild variant="ghost" size="sm">
            <Link to="/app/p/hall-a?replay=1">
              <CirclePlay aria-hidden /> Replay a recorded run
            </Link>
          </Button>
        </div>
        <ol className="mt-6 grid w-full grid-cols-1 gap-3 text-left text-sm sm:grid-cols-3" aria-label="How it works">
          {[
            ['Agents design', 'Gemini agents read the tender with verified citations and design power and cooling together.'],
            ['Physics checks', 'A deterministic rule pack checks every number; the AI cannot mark its own homework.'],
            ['An engineer signs', 'You decide at each gate, sign the revision, and PRs and the CRM update follow.'],
          ].map(([t, d], i) => (
            <li key={t} className="rounded-lg border border-zinc-800 bg-zinc-950/60 p-3">
              <span className="font-mono text-xs text-zinc-500">0{i + 1}</span>
              <p className="font-medium text-zinc-100">{t}</p>
              <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400">{d}</p>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ol>
      </main>
      <footer className="relative z-10 p-4 text-center text-xs text-zinc-500">{FICTIONAL_TEXT}</footer>
    </div>
  )
}
```

`apps/web/src/pages/Welcome.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogFooter, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog'
import { readJson, writeJson } from '@/lib/storage'
import { useState } from 'react'

export function hasBeenWelcomed(): boolean {
  return readJson<boolean>('welcomed', false)
}

/** First-visit welcome (spec §13.5): one sentence, "Take the 2-minute tour" (primary) or "Explore on my own". */
export function Welcome({ onStartTour }: { onStartTour: () => void }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(() => !hasBeenWelcomed())
  const close = () => {
    writeJson('welcomed', true)
    setOpen(false)
  }
  return (
    <Dialog open={open} onOpenChange={(o) => !o && close()}>
      <DialogContent showCloseButton={false} className="sm:max-w-md">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>Welcome to Meridian Power Engineering's Kirchhoff workspace</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>
            Kirchhoff's agents design power and cooling for AI data halls; a deterministic checker proves every number,
            and an engineer reviews and signs.
          </DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        <DialogFooter className="gap-2 sm:justify-start">
          <Button
            onClick={() => {
              close()
              onStartTour()
            }}
          >
            Take the 2-minute tour
          </Button>
          <Button variant="ghost" onClick={close}>
            Explore on my own
          </Button>
        </DialogFooter>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}
```

`apps/web/src/pages/Dashboard.tsx` (the welcome's tour button goes to Hall A's matrix until Task 14 wires the real tour):
```tsx
import { CirclePlay, Factory, SunMedium, UtilityPole, Zap, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router'
import { EmptyState } from '@/components/Labels'
import { ProjectStateBadge } from '@/components/ProjectState'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { useWorld } from '@/data/useWorld'
import { fmtRelative } from '@/lib/format'
import { readJson } from '@/lib/storage'
import type { AssetPack, Project } from '@/lib/types'
import { Welcome } from './Welcome'

const PACKS: { id: AssetPack; label: string; icon: LucideIcon }[] = [
  { id: 'datahall', label: 'AI data halls', icon: Factory },
  { id: 'grid', label: 'Grid', icon: UtilityPole },
  { id: 'solar', label: 'Solar', icon: SunMedium },
]

export function lastLiveRun(pid: string): string | null {
  return readJson<string | null>(`lastRun.${pid}`, null)
}

function ProjectCard({ p }: { p: Project }) {
  const liveRun = p.id === 'hall-b' ? lastLiveRun('hall-b') : null
  const href = liveRun ? `/app/p/${p.id}?run=${liveRun}` : `/app/p/${p.id}`
  return (
    <article
      data-tour={p.id === 'hall-b' ? 'hall-b' : undefined}
      className="group relative flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-lg border bg-card p-4 transition-colors hover:border-zinc-600"
    >
      <div className="flex items-start justify-between gap-2">
        <h3 className="text-sm font-medium text-zinc-100">
          <Link to={href} className="after:absolute after:inset-0 focus-visible:outline-none">
            {p.name}
          </Link>
        </h3>
        {p.sample && <span className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-zinc-400">sample</span>}
      </div>
      <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400">{p.client}</p>
      <div className="mt-auto flex items-center justify-between pt-2">
        <ProjectStateBadge state={liveRun ? 'running' : p.state} />
        <span className="text-xs text-zinc-500">{fmtRelative(p.updated_at)}</span>
      </div>
      {p.id === 'hall-b' && (
        <div className="relative z-10 flex gap-2 pt-1">
          <Button asChild size="xs">
            <Link to={href}>
              <Zap aria-hidden /> {liveRun ? 'Open your live run' : 'Run it live'}
            </Link>
          </Button>
          <Button asChild size="xs" variant="ghost">
            <Link to="/app/p/hall-a?replay=1">
              <CirclePlay aria-hidden /> Replay
            </Link>
          </Button>
        </div>
      )}
    </article>
  )
}

export function Dashboard() {
  const { world, error } = useWorld()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  if (error) return <EmptyState title="Could not load the demo world">{error}</EmptyState>
  if (!world) return <EmptyState title="Loading projects…" />
  const activity = [...world.activity].sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts)
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto grid h-full max-w-7xl grid-cols-1 gap-6 overflow-auto p-6 lg:grid-cols-[1fr_18rem]">
      <Welcome onStartTour={() => navigate('/app/p/hall-a?tab=matrix')} />
      <section aria-labelledby="projects-h" data-tour="projects" className="flex flex-col gap-6">
        <div>
          <h1 id="projects-h" className="text-lg font-semibold">
            Projects
          </h1>
          <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">{world.org.name}</p>
        </div>
        {PACKS.map((pack) => {
          const projects = world.projects.filter((p) => p.asset_pack === pack.id)
          if (!projects.length) return null
          const Icon = pack.icon
          return (
            <div key={pack.id} role="group" aria-label={pack.label} className="flex flex-col gap-3">
              <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">
                <Icon aria-hidden className="size-4" /> {pack.label}
              </h2>
              <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 sm:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-3">
                {projects.map((p) => (
                  <ProjectCard key={p.id} p={p} />
                ))}
              </div>
            </div>
          )
        })}
      </section>
      <aside className="flex flex-col gap-6">
        <section aria-labelledby="activity-h">
          <h2 id="activity-h" className="mb-2 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">
            Activity
          </h2>
          <ol className="flex flex-col gap-2 text-sm">
            {activity.map((a) => (
              <li key={a.id} className="border-l border-zinc-700 pl-3">
                <span className="text-zinc-200">{a.actor}</span> <span className="text-zinc-400">{a.text}</span>
                <div className="text-xs text-zinc-500">{fmtRelative(a.ts)}</div>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ol>
        </section>
        <section aria-labelledby="team-h">
          <h2 id="team-h" className="mb-2 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">
            Team
          </h2>
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-2 text-sm">
            {world.people.map((p) => (
              <li key={p.id} className="flex items-center gap-2">
                <span aria-hidden className="grid size-6 place-items-center rounded-full bg-zinc-800 text-[10px] text-zinc-300">
                  {p.initials ?? p.name.slice(0, 2).toUpperCase()}
                </span>
                <span className="text-zinc-200">{p.name}</span>
                <span className="truncate text-xs text-zinc-500">{p.role}</span>
              </li>
            ))}
            <li className="flex items-center gap-2 text-zinc-400">
              <span aria-hidden className="grid size-6 place-items-center rounded-full bg-sky-900 text-[10px] text-sky-200">
                YOU
              </span>
              You (Demo Reviewer)
            </li>
          </ul>
        </section>
      </aside>
    </div>
  )
}
```

### Task 6: Workspace layout, agent lanes, revision timeline, requirement cards (Tue)

The four-pane workspace (spec §12 screen 3): header with breadcrumb, asset-pack switcher, data-source tag and live "agents working" indicator; agent lanes on the left (one accent per agent, status icon + text, counters such as "2/3 quotes verified" and "Drawing read · DXF agrees", event lines per kind); canvas tabs in the middle; requirement cards with verified quotes and tender chips on the right; revision timeline at the bottom ("r1 2 fail ── r2 all pass ── signed by …") with compare toggle. Canvas panes are explanatory empty states until Tasks 7–12 plug in.

**Files:**
- Create: `apps/web/src/workspace/WorkspaceContext.tsx`, `apps/web/src/components/TenderChip.tsx`, `apps/web/src/workspace/AgentLanes.tsx`, `apps/web/src/workspace/RevisionTimeline.tsx`, `apps/web/src/workspace/CanvasTabs.tsx`, `apps/web/src/workspace/RequirementsPanel.tsx`, `apps/web/src/workspace/WorkspaceHeader.tsx`, `apps/web/src/workspace/WorkspacePage.tsx`
- Modify: `apps/web/src/App.tsx` (add the workspace route)
- Test: `apps/web/src/workspace/WorkspacePage.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Task 2 selectors/diff/impact; Task 3 `useUi`, `CANVAS_TABS`, `useWorld`, `useWorldProject`; Task 4 `useProjectData`.
- Produces:
  - `WorkspaceView` (`@/workspace/WorkspaceContext`): `{bundle, source, rev, parent, model, findings, status: Map<id, FindingStatus>, diff: ModelDiff|null, resolved: Set<id>, whatIf: WhatIfResult|null, highlights: Map<id, Discipline[]>, whatIfStatus: Map<id, FindingStatus>, revRef: string|null, run: RunRecord|null}`; `buildView(bundle, source, {viewRev, compare, whatIf})`; `<WorkspaceProvider bundle source>`; `useWorkspace()`.

  - `<WorkspacePage/>` for `/app/p/:pid`; honours `?tab=`, `?rev=`, `?finding=`, `?run=`, `?replay=1`.

```tsx
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react'
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event'
import { Route, Routes, useNavigate } from 'react-router'
import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render'
import { useUi } from '@/store/ui'
import { WorkspacePage } from './WorkspacePage'

const open = (route = '/app/p/hall-a') => renderWithProviders(<WorkspacePage />, { route, path: '/app/p/:pid' })

test('workspace renders lanes with status text and counters from the static bundle', async () => {
  open()
  const lanes = await screen.findByRole('navigation', { name: 'Agent lanes' })
  expect(within(lanes).getByText('Intake')).toBeInTheDocument()
  expect(within(lanes).getAllByText('Done').length).toBeGreaterThan(5)
  expect(within(lanes).getByText('2/3 quotes verified')).toBeInTheDocument()
  expect(within(lanes).getByText('Drawing read · DXF agrees')).toBeInTheDocument()
  expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: /Deccan AI Campus · Hall A/ })).toBeInTheDocument()
  expect(screen.getByText('Recorded design')).toBeInTheDocument()
})

test('revision timeline shows fail counts, the signer and switches revisions', async () => {
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  open()
  const footer = await screen.findByRole('contentinfo', { name: 'Revisions' })
  expect(within(footer).getByRole('button', { name: /r1 2 fail/ })).toBeInTheDocument()
  expect(within(footer).getByText(/signed by Priya Raman/)).toBeInTheDocument()
  await user.click(within(footer).getByRole('button', { name: /r1/ }))
  expect(useUi.getState().viewRev).toBe('r1')
  expect(within(footer).getByRole('link', { name: 'Open review' })).toHaveAttribute('href', '/app/p/hall-a/review/r1')
})

test('requirement cards show verified quotes with tender chips', async () => {
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  open()
  const reqs = await screen.findByRole('region', { name: 'Requirements' })
  expect(within(reqs).getAllByText('quote verified')).toHaveLength(2)
  await user.click(within(reqs).getByRole('button', { name: 'Open tender §4.6 · p.4' }))
  expect(useUi.getState().citation?.page).toBe(4)
})

test('?tab= deep link selects the canvas tab', async () => {
  open('/app/p/hall-a?tab=matrix')
  await screen.findByRole('navigation', { name: 'Agent lanes' })
  expect(screen.getByRole('tab', { name: 'Matrix' })).toHaveAttribute('aria-selected', 'true')
})

test('?rev= and ?finding= are applied again when only the query changes (tour step 3)', async () => {
  const user = userEvent.setup()
  function Jump() {
    const navigate = useNavigate()
    return <button onClick={() => navigate('/app/p/hall-a?tab=matrix&rev=r1&finding=R05:FA-01')}>jump</button>
  }
  renderWithProviders(
    <Routes>
      <Route
        path="/app/p/:pid"
        element={
          <>
            <Jump />
            <WorkspacePage />
          </>
        }
      />
    </Routes>,
    { route: '/app/p/hall-a?tab=matrix' },
  )
  await screen.findByRole('navigation', { name: 'Agent lanes' })
  await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'jump' }))
  expect(useUi.getState().findingId).toBe('R05:FA-01')
  expect(useUi.getState().viewRev).toBe('r1')
})
```

`apps/web/src/workspace/WorkspaceContext.tsx`:
```tsx
// Derived, memoised view of the bundle for the current workspace (selected revision, statuses,
// diff and what-if overlays). Every canvas reads it through useWorkspace().
import { createContext, useContext, useMemo, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { diffModels, type ModelDiff } from '@/lib/diff'
import { chainHighlights } from '@/lib/impact'
import type { Discipline } from '@/lib/model'
import {
  currentRun,
  findingsFor,
  latestRevision,
  parentRevision,
  revisionById,
  revisionRef,
  statusByElement,
  type DataSource,
} from '@/lib/select'
import type { Bundle, DesignModel, Finding, FindingStatus, Revision, RunRecord, WhatIfResult } from '@/lib/types'
import { useUi } from '@/store/ui'

export interface WorkspaceView {
  bundle: Bundle
  source: DataSource
  rev: Revision | null
  parent: Revision | null
  model: DesignModel | null
  findings: Finding[]
  status: Map<string, FindingStatus>
  /** Present only while "Compare with parent" is on and the revision has a parent. */
  diff: ModelDiff | null
  /** Elements that failed in the parent revision and pass now (cold-open "turns green"). */
  resolved: Set<string>
  /** What-if overlay: the result, element → impact-chain disciplines, and what-if statuses. */
  whatIf: WhatIfResult | null
  highlights: Map<string, Discipline[]>
  whatIfStatus: Map<string, FindingStatus>
  revRef: string | null
  run: RunRecord | null
}

export function buildView(bundle: Bundle, source: DataSource, ui: Pick<ReturnType<typeof useUi.getState>, 'viewRev' | 'compare' | 'whatIf'>): WorkspaceView {
  const rev = revisionById(bundle, ui.viewRev) ?? latestRevision(bundle)
  const parent = parentRevision(bundle, rev)
  const findings = findingsFor(bundle, rev?.id)
  const status = statusByElement(findings)
  const parentStatus = statusByElement(findingsFor(bundle, parent?.id))
  const resolved = new Set([...parentStatus].filter(([id, s]) => s === 'fail' && status.get(id) === 'pass').map(([id]) => id))
  const model = rev?.model ?? null
  const whatIf = ui.whatIf?.result ?? null
  return {
    bundle,
    source,
    rev,
    parent,
    model,
    findings,
    status,
    diff: ui.compare && parent ? diffModels(parent.model, model) : null,
    resolved,
    whatIf,
    highlights: chainHighlights(whatIf, model),
    whatIfStatus: whatIf ? statusByElement(whatIf.findings) : new Map(),
    revRef: rev ? revisionRef(bundle, rev.id, source) : null,
    run: currentRun(bundle),
  }
}

const Ctx = createContext<WorkspaceView | null>(null)

export function WorkspaceProvider({ bundle, source, children }: { bundle: Bundle; source: DataSource; children: ReactNode }) {
  const viewRev = useUi((s) => s.viewRev)
  const compare = useUi((s) => s.compare)
  const whatIf = useUi((s) => s.whatIf)
  const view = useMemo(() => buildView(bundle, source, { viewRev, compare, whatIf }), [bundle, source, viewRev, compare, whatIf])
  return <Ctx.Provider value={view}>{children}</Ctx.Provider>
}

export function useWorkspace(): WorkspaceView {
  const v = useContext(Ctx)
  if (!v) throw new Error('useWorkspace outside WorkspaceProvider')
  return v
}
```

```tsx
import { FileText } from 'lucide-react'
import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tooltip'
import type { Citation } from '@/lib/types'
import { useUi } from '@/store/ui'

/** NotebookLM-style citation: hover previews the verified quote, click opens the PDF at the clause. */
export function TenderChip({ citation, onOpen }: { citation: Citation; onOpen?: () => void }) {
  const openCitation = useUi((s) => s.openCitation)
  const label = `${citation.clause ?? 'Tender'} · p.${citation.page}`
  return (
    <Tooltip>
      <TooltipTrigger asChild>
        <button
          type="button"
          data-tour="tender-chip"
          aria-label={`Open tender ${label}`}
          onClick={() => {
            openCitation(citation)
            onOpen?.()
          }}
          className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded border border-sky-500/40 bg-sky-500/10 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-sky-200 hover:bg-sky-500/20"
        >
          <FileText aria-hidden className="size-3" />
          {label}
        </button>
      </TooltipTrigger>
      <TooltipContent className="max-w-xs">“{citation.quote}”</TooltipContent>
    </Tooltip>
  )
}

export function ClauseChip({ standard, clause }: { standard: string; clause?: string | null }) {
  return (
    <span className="inline-flex items-center rounded border border-zinc-700 bg-zinc-800/60 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-300">
      {standard}
      {clause ? ` ${clause}` : ''}
    </span>
  )
}
```

`apps/web/src/workspace/AgentLanes.tsx`:
```tsx
import { cn } from 'cn'
import {
  BookOpen,
  ChevronDown,
  CircleCheck,
  CircleDot,
  CircleX,
  FileText,
  Hand,
  ListChecks,
  LoaderCircle,
  MessageSquare,
  Wrench,
  type LucideIcon,
} from 'lucide-react'
import { AnimatePresence, motion } from 'motion/react'
import { useState } from 'react'
import { AgentDot } from '@/components/Labels'
import { StatusBadge } from '@/components/StatusBadge'
import { AGENTS } from '@/lib/agents'
import { artifactPayload, findingPayload, gatePayload, ledgerPayload, toolPayload } from '@/lib/events'
import { fmtKeyValue } from '@/lib/format'
import { buildLanes, type Lane, type LaneStatus } from '@/lib/select'
import type { RunEvent } from '@/lib/types'
import { useUi } from '@/store/ui'
import { useWorkspace } from './WorkspaceContext'

const LANE_STATUS: Record<LaneStatus, { label: string; icon: LucideIcon; cls: string }> = {
  idle: { label: 'Waiting', icon: CircleDot, cls: 'text-zinc-500' },
  running: { label: 'Working', icon: LoaderCircle, cls: 'text-sky-300' },
  waiting: { label: 'At gate', icon: Hand, cls: 'text-amber-300' },
  done: { label: 'Done', icon: CircleCheck, cls: 'text-emerald-400' },
  failed: { label: 'Failed', icon: CircleX, cls: 'text-rose-400' },
}

const KIND_ICON: Record<RunEvent['kind'], LucideIcon> = {
  plan: ListChecks,
  tool_call: Wrench,
  tool_result: Wrench,
  message: MessageSquare,
  artifact: FileText,
  finding: CircleCheck,
  gate: Hand,
  ledger: BookOpen,
  status: CircleDot,
}

function EventLine({ ev }: { ev: RunEvent }) {
  const openFinding = useUi((s) => s.openFinding)
  const setViewRev = useUi((s) => s.setViewRev)
  const Icon = KIND_ICON[ev.kind]
  const f = findingPayload(ev)
  if (f) {
    return (
      <button
        type="button"
        className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 text-left hover:text-zinc-100"
        onClick={() => {
          if (f.rev) setViewRev(f.rev)
          openFinding(f.finding_id)
        }}
      >
        <StatusBadge status={f.status} compact />
        <span className="font-mono">{f.rule_id}</span>
        <span className="truncate font-mono text-zinc-400">{f.element_id}</span>
      </button>
    )
  }
  const tool = toolPayload(ev)
  const gate = gatePayload(ev)
  const ledger = ledgerPayload(ev)
  const artifact = artifactPayload(ev)
  let text: string = ev.title
  if (tool && ev.kind === 'tool_call') text = `${tool.tool}()`
  if (tool && ev.kind === 'tool_result') {
    const parts = Object.entries(tool.summary).slice(0, 3).map(([k, v]) => fmtKeyValue(k, v))
    text = parts.length ? `${tool.tool} → ${parts.join(' · ')}` : `${tool.tool} ✓`
  }
  if (gate) text = `Gate: ${gate}`
  if (ledger) text = `${ledger.kind.replace('_', ' ')}: ${ledger.text}`
  if (artifact) text = `${artifact.type}: ${ev.title}`
  return (
    <div className="flex items-start gap-2">
      <Icon aria-hidden className="mt-0.5 size-3.5 shrink-0 text-zinc-500" />
      <span className="min-w-0">
        <span className="break-words">{text}</span>
        {ev.detail && <span className="block truncate text-zinc-500">{ev.detail}</span>}
      </span>
    </div>
  )
}

function LaneView({ lane }: { lane: Lane }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(lane.status === 'running' || lane.status === 'waiting')
  const [all, setAll] = useState(false)
  const meta = AGENTS[lane.agent]
  const st = LANE_STATUS[lane.status]
  const StIcon = st.icon
  const shown = all ? lane.events : lane.events.slice(-5)
  return (
    <li className="border-b border-zinc-800/80 px-3 py-2" style={{ boxShadow: `inset 2px 0 0 0 ${lane.status === 'idle' ? 'transparent' : meta.color.startsWith('#') ? meta.color : '#a78bfa'}` }}>
      <button
        type="button"
        aria-expanded={open}
        onClick={() => setOpen(!open)}
        className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 text-left text-sm"
      >
        <AgentDot agent={lane.agent} />
        <span className="font-medium text-zinc-100">{meta.label}</span>
        <span className={cn('ml-auto inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs', st.cls)}>
          <StIcon aria-hidden className={cn('size-3.5', lane.status === 'running' && 'animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none')} />
          {st.label}
        </span>
        <ChevronDown aria-hidden className={cn('size-3.5 text-zinc-500 transition-transform', open && 'rotate-180')} />
      </button>
      {lane.counters.length > 0 && (
        <ul className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap gap-1" aria-label={`${meta.label} summary`}>
          {lane.counters.map((c) => (
            <li key={c} className="rounded bg-zinc-800/70 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-300">
              {c}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
      <AnimatePresence initial={false}>
        {open && lane.events.length > 0 && (
          <motion.ol
            initial={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
            animate={{ height: 'auto', opacity: 1 }}
            exit={{ height: 0, opacity: 0 }}
            className="mt-2 flex flex-col gap-1.5 overflow-hidden text-xs text-zinc-300"
          >
            {shown.map((ev) => (
              <li key={ev.seq}>
                <EventLine ev={ev} />
              </li>
            ))}
            {lane.events.length > 5 && (
              <li>
                <button type="button" className="text-zinc-500 underline-offset-2 hover:underline" onClick={() => setAll(!all)}>
                  {all ? 'Show recent' : `Show all ${lane.events.length}`}
                </button>
              </li>
            )}
          </motion.ol>
        )}
      </AnimatePresence>
    </li>
  )
}

export function AgentLanes() {
  const { bundle } = useWorkspace()
  const lanes = buildLanes(bundle)
  return (
    <nav aria-label="Agent lanes" data-tour="lanes" className="h-full overflow-y-auto">
      <h2 className="px-3 pt-3 pb-1 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">Agents</h2>
      <ul>
        {lanes.map((l) => (
          <LaneView key={l.agent} lane={l} />
        ))}
      </ul>
    </nav>
  )
}
```

`apps/web/src/workspace/RevisionTimeline.tsx`:
```tsx
import { cn } from 'cn'
import { CircleCheck, CircleX, GitCompareArrows, Stamp } from 'lucide-react'
import { Link, useLocation } from 'react-router'
import { Switch } from '@/components/ui/switch'
import { approvalFor, countByStatus, findingsFor } from '@/lib/select'
import { useUi } from '@/store/ui'
import { useWorkspace } from './WorkspaceContext'

export function RevisionTimeline() {
  const { bundle, rev, parent } = useWorkspace()
  const setViewRev = useUi((s) => s.setViewRev)
  const compare = useUi((s) => s.compare)
  const setCompare = useUi((s) => s.setCompare)
  const runId = new URLSearchParams(useLocation().search).get('run')
  if (!bundle.revisions.length) {
    return (
      <footer className="flex h-10 items-center border-t px-4 text-xs text-zinc-500">
        Revisions appear here after the Checker's first pass.
      </footer>
    )
  }
  return (
    <footer aria-label="Revisions" className="flex h-11 items-center gap-3 overflow-x-auto border-t px-4 text-xs">
      <span className="font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">Revisions</span>
      <ol className="flex items-center gap-2">
        {bundle.revisions.map((r, i) => {
          const c = countByStatus(findingsFor(bundle, r.id))
          const approval = approvalFor(bundle, r.id)
          const active = r.id === rev?.id
          return (
            <li key={r.id} className="flex items-center gap-2">
              {i > 0 && <span aria-hidden className="h-px w-6 bg-zinc-700" />}
              <button
                type="button"
                aria-pressed={active}
                onClick={() => setViewRev(r.id)}
                className={cn(
                  'inline-flex items-center gap-1 rounded border px-2 py-1 font-mono',
                  active ? 'border-zinc-400 bg-zinc-800 text-zinc-100' : 'border-zinc-700 text-zinc-300 hover:border-zinc-500',
                )}
              >
                {r.id}{' '}
                {c.fail > 0 ? (
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 text-rose-400">
                    <CircleX aria-hidden className="size-3.5" />
                    {c.fail} fail
                  </span>
                ) : (
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-0.5 text-emerald-400">
                    <CircleCheck aria-hidden className="size-3.5" />
                    all pass
                  </span>
                )}
              </button>
              {approval && (
                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-300">
                  <Stamp aria-hidden className="size-3.5" />
                  signed by {approval.approver_name} ({approval.role})
                </span>
              )}
            </li>
          )
        })}
      </ol>
      <label className="ml-auto inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-zinc-400">
        <GitCompareArrows aria-hidden className="size-3.5" />
        Compare with parent
        <Switch checked={compare && !!parent} disabled={!parent} onCheckedChange={setCompare} aria-label="Compare with parent" />
      </label>
      {rev && (
        <Link to={`/app/p/${bundle.project.id}/review/${rev.id}${runId ? `?run=${runId}` : ''}`} className="rounded border border-zinc-700 px-2 py-1 text-zinc-200 hover:border-zinc-500">
          Open review
        </Link>
      )}
    </footer>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { Tabs, TabsContent, TabsList, TabsTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tabs'
import { CANVAS_TABS, useUi, type CanvasTab } from '@/store/ui'

export function CanvasTabs({ panes }: { panes: Record<CanvasTab, ReactNode> }) {
  const tab = useUi((s) => s.tab)
  const setTab = useUi((s) => s.setTab)
  return (
    <Tabs value={tab} onValueChange={(v) => setTab(v as CanvasTab)} className="flex h-full min-h-0 flex-col gap-0">
      <TabsList className="m-2 self-start">
        {CANVAS_TABS.map((t) => (
          <TabsTrigger key={t.id} value={t.id} data-tour={`tab-${t.id}`}>
            {t.label}
          </TabsTrigger>
        ))}
      </TabsList>
      {CANVAS_TABS.map((t) => (
        <TabsContent key={t.id} value={t.id} className="relative min-h-0 flex-1 overflow-hidden">
          {panes[t.id]}
        </TabsContent>
      ))}
    </Tabs>
  )
}
```

`apps/web/src/workspace/RequirementsPanel.tsx`:
```tsx
import { CircleCheck, TriangleAlert } from 'lucide-react'
import { motion } from 'motion/react'
import { TenderChip } from '@/components/TenderChip'
import { fmtQty } from '@/lib/format'
import { useWorkspace } from './WorkspaceContext'

/** Intake's requirement cards (spec §3 0:10–0:30): each with its verified quote and a tender chip. */
export function RequirementsPanel() {
  const { bundle } = useWorkspace()
  const brief = bundle.brief
  if (!brief) return <p className="p-3 text-xs text-zinc-500">Requirement cards appear when Intake has read the tender.</p>
  return (
    <section aria-label="Requirements" className="flex flex-col gap-2 p-3">
      <h3 className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">Citations</h3>
      <ol className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        {brief.requirements.map((r, i) => (
          <motion.li
            key={r.id}
            initial={{ opacity: 0, y: 6 }}
            animate={{ opacity: 1, y: 0 }}
            transition={{ delay: Math.min(i, 12) * 0.05 }}
            className="rounded border bg-card p-2 text-xs"
          >
            <p className="text-zinc-200">{r.text}</p>
            <div className="mt-1 flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              {typeof r.value === 'number' && <span className="font-mono text-zinc-300">{fmtQty(r.value, r.unit)}</span>}
              {typeof r.value === 'string' && <span className="font-mono text-zinc-300">{r.value}</span>}
              <TenderChip citation={r.citation} />
              {r.verified ? (
                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-emerald-400">
                  <CircleCheck aria-hidden className="size-3.5" /> quote verified
                </span>
              ) : (
                <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-amber-300">
                  <TriangleAlert aria-hidden className="size-3.5" /> quote not verified
                </span>
              )}
            </div>
          </motion.li>
        ))}
      </ol>
    </section>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { ChevronDown, ChevronRight } from 'lucide-react'
import { Link } from 'react-router'
import { AgentDot } from '@/components/Labels'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import {
  DropdownMenu,
  DropdownMenuContent,
  DropdownMenuItem,
  DropdownMenuLabel,
  DropdownMenuTrigger,
} from '@/components/ui/dropdown-menu'
import { useWorld } from '@/data/useWorld'
import { buildLanes } from '@/lib/select'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { useWorkspace } from './WorkspaceContext'

const SOURCE_LABEL = { static: 'Recorded design', live: 'Live run', replay: 'Replay' } as const
const PACK_LABEL = { datahall: 'AI data hall', grid: 'Grid', solar: 'Solar' } as const

export function WorkspaceHeader({ actions }: { actions?: ReactNode }) {
  const { bundle, source } = useWorkspace()
  const { world } = useWorld()
  const p = bundle.project
  const working = buildLanes(bundle).filter((l) => l.status === 'running')
  return (
    <div className="flex h-11 items-center gap-3 border-b px-4 text-sm">
      <nav aria-label="Breadcrumb" className="flex min-w-0 items-center gap-1 text-zinc-400">
        <Link to="/app" className="hover:text-zinc-100">
          Kirchhoff
        </Link>
        <ChevronRight aria-hidden className="size-3.5" />
        <h1 className="truncate font-medium text-zinc-100">
          {p.name}
          {p.sample && <span className="text-zinc-500"> (sample)</span>}
        </h1>
      </nav>
      <DropdownMenu>
        <DropdownMenuTrigger asChild>
          <Button variant="outline" size="xs">
            {PACK_LABEL[p.asset_pack]} <ChevronDown aria-hidden />
          </Button>
        </DropdownMenuTrigger>
        <DropdownMenuContent align="start">
          <DropdownMenuLabel>Asset packs</DropdownMenuLabel>
          {(world?.projects ?? []).map((wp) => (
            <DropdownMenuItem key={wp.id} asChild>
              <Link to={`/app/p/${wp.id}`}>
                {PACK_LABEL[wp.asset_pack]} · {wp.name}
              </Link>
            </DropdownMenuItem>
          ))}
        </DropdownMenuContent>
      </DropdownMenu>
      <span className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-zinc-300">{SOURCE_LABEL[source]}</span>
      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-400" aria-live="polite">
        {working.length ? (
          <>
            {working.map((l) => (
              <AgentDot key={l.agent} agent={l.agent} className="animate-pulse motion-reduce:animate-none" />
            ))}
            {working.length} agents working
          </>
        ) : (
          'No agents working'
        )}
      </span>
      <div className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-2">{actions}</div>
    </div>
  )
}
```

`apps/web/src/workspace/WorkspacePage.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { useParams, useSearchParams } from 'react-router'
import { EmptyState } from '@/components/Labels'
import { useProjectData } from '@/data/useProjectData'
import { useWorldProject } from '@/data/useWorld'
import { CANVAS_TABS, useUi, type CanvasTab } from '@/store/ui'
import { AgentLanes } from './AgentLanes'
import { CanvasTabs } from './CanvasTabs'
import { RequirementsPanel } from './RequirementsPanel'
import { RevisionTimeline } from './RevisionTimeline'
import { WorkspaceHeader } from './WorkspaceHeader'
import { WorkspaceProvider } from './WorkspaceContext'

/** Resets UI state per project, then applies ?tab=, ?rev= and ?finding= whenever they change (tour, deep links). */
function useUrlState(pid: string) {
  const [params] = useSearchParams()
  const tab = params.get('tab')
  const rev = params.get('rev')
  const finding = params.get('finding')
  useEffect(() => {
    useUi.getState().resetWorkspaceUi()
  }, [pid])
  useEffect(() => {
    const ui = useUi.getState()
    if (tab && CANVAS_TABS.some((t) => t.id === tab)) ui.setTab(tab as CanvasTab)
    if (rev) ui.setViewRev(rev)
    if (finding) ui.openFinding(finding)
  }, [pid, tab, rev, finding])
}

export function WorkspacePage() {
  const { pid = '' } = useParams()
  const [params] = useSearchParams()
  const runId = params.get('run')
  const replay = params.get('replay') === '1'
  const project = useWorldProject(pid)
  const data = useProjectData(pid, { runId, replay, project })
  useUrlState(pid)

  if (data.status === 'error' && !data.bundle) {
    return (
      <EmptyState title="This project's data is not available">
        {data.error}. Seed projects load from static files; live runs need the backend.
      </EmptyState>
    )
  }
  if (!data.bundle) return <EmptyState title="Loading workspace…" />

  const panes: Record<CanvasTab, ReactNode> = {
    hall: <EmptyState title="3D hall">The 3D view of the selected revision appears here.</EmptyState>,
    sld: <EmptyState title="Single-line diagram">The SLD of the selected revision appears here.</EmptyState>,
    matrix: <EmptyState title="Compliance matrix">Checker results for the selected revision appear here.</EmptyState>,
    options: <EmptyState title="Topology options">Costed options appear here after Power and Procurement.</EmptyState>,
    docs: <EmptyState title="Documents">Tender, drawings and exports appear here.</EmptyState>,
  }

  return (
    <WorkspaceProvider bundle={data.bundle} source={data.source}>
      <div className="grid h-full grid-rows-[auto_minmax(0,1fr)_auto]">
        <WorkspaceHeader />
        <div className="grid min-h-0 grid-cols-[16rem_minmax(0,1fr)_20rem]">
          <aside className="min-h-0 border-r">
            <AgentLanes />
          </aside>
          <section aria-label="Canvas" className="min-h-0">
            <CanvasTabs panes={panes} />
          </section>
          <aside aria-label="Co-pilot" className="min-h-0 overflow-y-auto border-l">
            <RequirementsPanel />
          </aside>
        </div>
        <RevisionTimeline />
      </div>
    </WorkspaceProvider>
  )
}
```

### Task 7: Single-line diagram (Tue)

React Flow + ELK layered layout of the selected revision's equipment and connections, with IEC-style custom nodes (MV board, transformer, board, UPS, STS, busway, rack group), a staged reveal by level (MV → transformers → boards → UPS → critical boards/STS → busways → rack groups), status colouring with icon + text, diff recolour (dashed amber "changed", green "✓ resolved") and what-if impact rings.
