`apps/web/src/workspace/StartRunPanel.tsx`:
```tsx
import { CirclePlay, FileText, LoaderCircle, Upload, Zap } from 'lucide-react'
import { useState, type DragEvent } from 'react'
import { Link, useNavigate } from 'react-router'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { writeJson } from '@/lib/storage'
import type { Project } from '@/lib/types'
import { ApiError } from '@/services/apiClient'
import { useServices } from '@/services/services'
import { useUi } from '@/store/ui'
import { AutonomySwitch } from './gates/AutonomySwitch'

export const LIVE_TARGET = 'hall-b'

const INPUTS = [
  { name: 'Tender (PDF)', file: 'tender-hall-a.pdf' },
  { name: 'Hall drawing (DXF)', file: 'hall-a.dxf' },
  { name: 'Hall drawing plot (PDF)', file: 'hall-a-plan.pdf' },
  { name: 'Catalogue + ERP data', file: 'sandbox (simulated)' },
]

/** Hall B "Run it live" (spec §11): starts P1 in the visitor's own workspace. */
export function StartRunPanel({ project, onStarted }: { project: Project | null; onStarted?: () => void }) {
  const { api } = useServices()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const autonomy = useUi((s) => s.autonomy)
  const setAutonomy = useUi((s) => s.setAutonomy)
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [over, setOver] = useState(false)

  const start = async () => {
    setBusy(true)
    setError(null)
    try {
      await api.ensureWorkspace()
      const { run_id } = await api.startRun(LIVE_TARGET, autonomy)
      writeJson(`lastRun.${LIVE_TARGET}`, run_id)
      onStarted?.()
      navigate(`/app/p/${LIVE_TARGET}?run=${run_id}`)
    } catch (e) {
      setError(
        e instanceof ApiError
          ? e.status === 429 || e.status === 409
            ? `Live runs are busy right now (${e.detail}). Watch the recorded run instead.`
            : `The live backend answered ${e.status}: ${e.detail}.`
          : 'The live backend is unreachable. Seed projects and replays still work.',
      )
    } finally {
      setBusy(false)
    }
  }

  const onDrop = (e: DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault()
    setOver(false)
    void start()
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex h-full max-w-2xl flex-col justify-center gap-5 p-6">
      <div>
        <h1 className="text-lg font-semibold">{project?.name ?? 'Deccan AI Campus · Hall B'}</h1>
        <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">
          Hall B uses the same tender, drawing and catalogue as Hall A. Kirchhoff's agents design it in your own
          workspace; you decide at each gate and sign at the end.
        </p>
      </div>
      <ul className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-xs">
        {INPUTS.map((i) => (
          <li key={i.name} className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded border bg-card px-2 py-1.5">
            <FileText aria-hidden className="size-4 text-zinc-500" />
            <span className="text-zinc-200">{i.name}</span>
            <span className="ml-auto truncate font-mono text-zinc-500">{i.file}</span>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <div
        role="region"
        aria-label="Drop the tender PDF and drawing to start"
        onDragOver={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault()
          setOver(true)
        }}
        onDragLeave={() => setOver(false)}
        onDrop={onDrop}
        className={`flex flex-col items-center gap-1 rounded-lg border border-dashed p-6 text-center text-sm ${over ? 'border-sky-400 bg-sky-500/10' : 'border-zinc-700'}`}
      >
        <Upload aria-hidden className="size-5 text-zinc-400" />
        Drop the tender PDF and drawing here to start
        <span className="text-xs text-zinc-500">The demo always uses the Hall B package above; dropped files are not uploaded.</span>
      </div>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <AutonomySwitch value={autonomy} onChange={setAutonomy} />
        <Button onClick={() => void start()} disabled={busy}>
          {busy ? <LoaderCircle aria-hidden className="animate-spin" /> : <Zap aria-hidden />} Start live run
        </Button>
        <Button asChild variant="ghost">
          <Link to="/app/p/hall-a?replay=1">
            <CirclePlay aria-hidden /> Replay a recorded run
          </Link>
        </Button>
      </div>
      {error && (
        <p role="alert" className="rounded border border-amber-500/40 bg-amber-500/10 p-2 text-sm text-amber-200">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
```

`apps/web/src/workspace/RunBar.tsx`:
```tsx
import { CircleCheck, CircleX, Clock, Hand, LoaderCircle, Pause, Play, RotateCcw } from 'lucide-react'
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router'
import { Num } from '@/components/Labels'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import type { ReplayControls } from '@/data/replay'
import { useLeaseWatchdog } from '@/data/useLeaseWatchdog'
import { fmtElapsed } from '@/lib/format'
import { useServices } from '@/services/services'
import type { ReplayState } from '@/store/bundles'
import { useWorkspace } from './WorkspaceContext'

const SPEEDS = [1, 4, 8, 16]

function ReplayBar({ replay, controls }: { replay: ReplayState; controls: ReplayControls | null }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b bg-zinc-950/60 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs">
      <Button
        variant="ghost"
        size="icon-xs"
        aria-label={replay.playing ? 'Pause replay' : 'Play replay'}
        onClick={() => (replay.playing ? controls?.pause() : controls?.play())}
      >
        {replay.playing ? <Pause aria-hidden /> : <Play aria-hidden />}
      </Button>
      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-zinc-300">
        <Clock aria-hidden className="size-3.5" /> Elapsed (recorded) <Num>{fmtElapsed(replay.elapsedS)}</Num>
      </span>
      <span className="text-zinc-500">Time-compressed replay of a recorded run</span>
      <span className="text-zinc-500" data-dev>
        event <Num>{replay.cursor}</Num>/<Num>{replay.total}</Num>
      </span>
      <span className="ml-auto flex items-center gap-1" data-dev>
        {SPEEDS.map((s) => (
          <Button key={s} size="xs" variant={replay.speed === s ? 'secondary' : 'ghost'} onClick={() => controls?.setSpeed(s)}>
            {s}×
          </Button>
        ))}
        <Button size="xs" variant="ghost" onClick={() => controls?.seek(replay.total)}>
          Skip to end
        </Button>
      </span>
    </div>
  )
}

function LiveBar() {
  const { run } = useWorkspace()
  const { api } = useServices()
  const [now, setNow] = useState(() => Date.now() / 1000)
  useLeaseWatchdog(run, api.resume)
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = setInterval(() => setNow(Date.now() / 1000), 1000)
    return () => clearInterval(id)
  }, [])
  if (!run) return <div className="border-b px-3 py-1.5 text-xs text-zinc-400">Connecting to your run…</div>
  const end = run.finished_at ?? now
  const st =
    run.status === 'running'
      ? { icon: LoaderCircle, text: `Running · phase ${run.phase ?? ''}`, cls: 'text-sky-300', spin: true }
      : run.status === 'at_gate'
        ? { icon: Hand, text: `Waiting for you · ${run.gate} gate`, cls: 'text-amber-300', spin: false }
        : run.status === 'done'
          ? { icon: CircleCheck, text: 'Run complete', cls: 'text-emerald-400', spin: false }
          : { icon: CircleX, text: run.status === 'expired' ? 'Expired at a gate after 24 h' : 'Run failed', cls: 'text-rose-400', spin: false }
  const Icon = st.icon
  return (
    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 border-b bg-zinc-950/60 px-3 py-1.5 text-xs" aria-live="polite">
      <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-1 ${st.cls}`}>
        <Icon aria-hidden className={`size-3.5 ${st.spin ? 'animate-spin motion-reduce:animate-none' : ''}`} />
        {st.text}
      </span>
      <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-zinc-400">
        <Clock aria-hidden className="size-3.5" /> Elapsed <Num>{fmtElapsed(end - run.started_at)}</Num>
      </span>
      <span className="text-zinc-500">Autonomy: {run.autonomy === 'ask' ? 'Ask' : 'Auto'}</span>
      {run.status === 'failed' && (
        <Button size="xs" variant="outline" className="ml-auto" onClick={() => void api.resume(run.id).catch(() => undefined)}>
          <RotateCcw aria-hidden /> Resume
        </Button>
      )}
      {run.status === 'expired' && (
        <Button asChild size="xs" variant="outline" className="ml-auto">
          <Link to="/app/p/hall-b">Start a new run</Link>
        </Button>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}

export function RunBar({ replay, controls }: { replay?: ReplayState; controls: ReplayControls | null }) {
  const { source } = useWorkspace()
  if (source === 'replay' && replay) return <ReplayBar replay={replay} controls={controls} />
  if (source === 'live') return <LiveBar />
  return null
}
```

`apps/web/src/workspace/WorkspacePage.tsx` (replace the whole file):
```tsx
import { lazy, Suspense, useEffect, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { useParams, useSearchParams } from 'react-router'
import { EmptyState } from '@/components/Labels'
import { useProjectData } from '@/data/useProjectData'
import { useWorldProject } from '@/data/useWorld'
import type { Project } from '@/lib/types'
import { CANVAS_TABS, useUi, type CanvasTab } from '@/store/ui'
import { AgentLanes } from './AgentLanes'
import { CanvasTabs } from './CanvasTabs'
import { FindingDrawer } from './finding/FindingDrawer'
import { ComplianceMatrix } from './matrix/ComplianceMatrix'
import { RequirementsPanel } from './RequirementsPanel'
import { RevisionTimeline } from './RevisionTimeline'
import { RunBar } from './RunBar'
import { SldView } from './sld/SldView'
import { LIVE_TARGET, StartRunPanel } from './StartRunPanel'
import { WorkspaceHeader } from './WorkspaceHeader'
import { WorkspaceProvider } from './WorkspaceContext'

// three.js and pdf.js are large: load them only when the 3D tab or a citation opens.
const HallView = lazy(() => import('./hall/HallView'))
const PdfViewer = lazy(() => import('./finding/PdfViewer'))

/** Resets UI state per project, then applies ?tab=, ?rev= and ?finding= whenever they change (tour, deep links). */
function useUrlState(pid: string) {
  const [params] = useSearchParams()
  const tab = params.get('tab')
  const rev = params.get('rev')
  const finding = params.get('finding')
  useEffect(() => {
    useUi.getState().resetWorkspaceUi()
  }, [pid])
  useEffect(() => {
    const ui = useUi.getState()
    if (tab && CANVAS_TABS.some((t) => t.id === tab)) ui.setTab(tab as CanvasTab)
    if (rev) ui.setViewRev(rev)
    if (finding) ui.openFinding(finding)
  }, [pid, tab, rev, finding])
}

export function WorkspacePage() {
  const { pid = '' } = useParams()
  const [params] = useSearchParams()
  const runId = params.get('run')
  const replay = params.get('replay') === '1'
  const project = useWorldProject(pid)
  if (pid === LIVE_TARGET && !runId && !replay) return <StartRunPanel project={project} />
  return <LoadedWorkspace key={`${pid}:${runId}:${replay}`} pid={pid} runId={runId} replay={replay} project={project} />
}

function LoadedWorkspace({ pid, runId, replay, project }: { pid: string; runId: string | null; replay: boolean; project: Project | null }) {
  const data = useProjectData(pid, { runId, replay, project })
  useUrlState(pid)

  if (data.status === 'error' && !data.bundle) {
    return (
      <EmptyState title="This project's data is not available">
        {data.error}. Seed projects load from static files; live runs need the backend.
      </EmptyState>
    )
  }
  if (!data.bundle) return <EmptyState title="Loading workspace…" />

  const panes: Record<CanvasTab, ReactNode> = {
    hall: (
      <Suspense fallback={<EmptyState title="Loading 3D hall…" />}>
        <HallView />
      </Suspense>
    ),
    sld: <SldView />,
    matrix: <ComplianceMatrix />,
    options: <EmptyState title="Topology options">Costed options appear here after Power and Procurement.</EmptyState>,
    docs: <EmptyState title="Documents">Tender, drawings and exports appear here.</EmptyState>,
  }

  return (
    <WorkspaceProvider bundle={data.bundle} source={data.source}>
      <div className="grid h-full grid-rows-[auto_minmax(0,1fr)_auto]">
        <WorkspaceHeader />
        <div className="grid min-h-0 grid-cols-[16rem_minmax(0,1fr)_20rem]">
          <aside className="min-h-0 border-r">
            <AgentLanes />
          </aside>
          <section aria-label="Canvas" className="flex min-h-0 flex-col">
            <RunBar replay={data.replay} controls={data.controls} />
            <div className="min-h-0 flex-1">
              <CanvasTabs panes={panes} />
            </div>
          </section>
          <aside aria-label="Co-pilot" className="min-h-0 overflow-y-auto border-l">
            <RequirementsPanel />
          </aside>
        </div>
        <RevisionTimeline />
      </div>
      <FindingDrawer />
      <Suspense fallback={null}>
        <PdfViewer />
      </Suspense>
    </WorkspaceProvider>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { render, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react'
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event'
import type { ReactNode } from 'react'
import { MemoryRouter } from 'react-router'
import { TooltipProvider } from '@/components/ui/tooltip'
import type { DataSource } from '@/lib/select'

function wrap(ui: ReactNode, { bundle = fixtureBundle(), source = 'static' as DataSource, services = fakeServices() as Services } = {}) {

}
```

`apps/web/src/workspace/gates/PlanGate.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Hand, ListChecks } from 'lucide-react'
import { useState } from 'react'
import { AgentDot } from '@/components/Labels'
import { TenderChip } from '@/components/TenderChip'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { RadioGroup, RadioGroupItem } from '@/components/ui/radio-group'
import { AGENTS } from '@/lib/agents'
import type { RunRecord } from '@/lib/types'
import { useServices } from '@/services/services'

/**
 * Plan gate (spec §12 screen 4): steps with owners, Intake's open questions with cited sources,
 * Approve. Interactive only for a live run waiting at the plan gate; otherwise read-only history.
 */
export function PlanGate({ run, interactive }: { run: RunRecord; interactive: boolean }) {
  const { api } = useServices()
  const plan = run.plan ?? []
  const questions = run.open_questions ?? []
  const [answers, setAnswers] = useState<Record<string, string>>(() =>
    Object.fromEntries(questions.map((q) => [q.id, q.resolution ?? q.default_resolution])),
  )
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState<Record<string, string>>({})
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const approve = async () => {
    setBusy(true)
    setError(null)
    try {
      await api.planGate(run.id, answers, Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(notes).filter(([, v]) => v.trim())))
    } catch {
      setError('Could not reach the API; try again.')
    } finally {
      setBusy(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <section aria-label="Plan" className="flex flex-col gap-3 rounded-lg border bg-card p-3 text-sm" data-tour="plan-gate">
      <h3 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">
        {interactive ? <Hand aria-hidden className="size-4 text-amber-300" /> : <ListChecks aria-hidden className="size-4" />}
        {interactive ? 'Plan gate · waiting for you' : 'Plan'}
      </h3>
      <ol className="flex flex-col gap-1.5">
        {plan.map((s, i) => (
          <li key={s.id} className="flex flex-col gap-1">
            <span className="flex items-center gap-2">
              <span className="w-4 font-mono text-xs text-zinc-500">{i + 1}</span>
              <AgentDot agent={s.agent} />
              <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400">{AGENTS[s.agent].label}</span>
              <span className="text-zinc-200">{s.title}</span>
            </span>
            {interactive ? (
              <Input
                aria-label={`Note for ${s.title}`}
                placeholder="Optional note for this step"
                value={notes[s.id] ?? ''}
                onChange={(e) => setNotes({ ...notes, [s.id]: e.target.value })}
                className="ml-6 h-7 text-xs"
              />
            ) : (
              s.note && <span className="ml-6 text-xs text-zinc-400">Note: {s.note}</span>
            )}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ol>
      {questions.map((q) => (
        <fieldset key={q.id} className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded border border-amber-500/30 bg-amber-500/5 p-2">
          <legend className="px-1 text-xs font-medium text-amber-200">Open question</legend>
          <p className="text-zinc-200">{q.text}</p>
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
            {q.citations.map((c) => (
              <TenderChip key={`${c.page}${c.clause}`} citation={c} />
            ))}
          </div>
          {interactive ? (
            <>
              <RadioGroup
                value={q.options.includes(answers[q.id]) ? answers[q.id] : ''}
                onValueChange={(v) => setAnswers({ ...answers, [q.id]: v })}
                aria-label={q.text}
              >
                {q.options.map((o) => (
                  <label key={o} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                    <RadioGroupItem value={o} /> {o}
                  </label>
                ))}
              </RadioGroup>
              <Input
                aria-label="Answer in your own words"
                placeholder="…or answer in your own words"
                value={q.options.includes(answers[q.id]) ? '' : (answers[q.id] ?? '')}
                onChange={(e) => setAnswers({ ...answers, [q.id]: e.target.value })}
                className="h-7 text-xs"
              />
            </>
          ) : (
            <p className="text-xs text-zinc-300">
              Resolved: <span className="text-zinc-100">{q.resolution ?? q.default_resolution}</span>
            </p>
          )}
        </fieldset>
      ))}
      {interactive && (
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <Button size="sm" onClick={() => void approve()} disabled={busy}>
            Approve plan
          </Button>
          {error && (
            <span role="alert" className="text-xs text-rose-300">
              {error}
            </span>
          )}
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  )
}
```

`apps/web/src/workspace/gates/OptionCards.tsx`:
```tsx
import { cn } from 'cn'
import { ArrowRight, Check, Hand } from 'lucide-react'
import { useState } from 'react'
import { EmptyState, IllustrativeLabel, Num } from '@/components/Labels'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { fmtNum, fmtQty, fmtUsd } from '@/lib/format'
import type { TopologyOption } from '@/lib/types'
import { useServices } from '@/services/services'
import { useWorkspace } from '../WorkspaceContext'

const NAMES: Record<TopologyOption['id'], string> = {
  '2N': '2N',
  DR: 'Distributed redundant (4-to-make-3)',
  BLOCK: 'Block redundant (3 + 1)',
}

function Metric({ label, value }: { label: string; value: string }) {
  return (
    <div className="flex items-baseline justify-between gap-2 text-xs">
      <dt className="text-zinc-400">{label}</dt>
      <dd>
        <Num className="text-zinc-100">{value}</Num>
      </dd>
    </div>
  )
}

/** Option gate (spec §12 screen 5): costed topology cards; selectable at a live option gate. */
export function OptionCards() {
  const { run, source } = useWorkspace()
  const { api } = useServices()
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const options = run?.options ?? []
  if (!run || !options.length)
    return <EmptyState title="No options yet">Power enumerates topologies and Procurement checks lead times; options appear here.</EmptyState>
  const atGate = source === 'live' && run.status === 'at_gate' && run.gate === 'option'

  const select = async (id: TopologyOption['id']) => {
    setBusy(id)
    setError(null)
    try {
      await api.optionGate(run.id, id)
    } catch {
      setError('Could not reach the API; try again.')
    } finally {
      setBusy(null)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col gap-3 overflow-auto p-3" data-tour="options">
      {atGate && (
        <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm text-amber-200">
          <Hand aria-hidden className="size-4" /> Option gate · pick the topology to route and check
        </p>
      )}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 lg:grid-cols-3">
        {options.map((o) => {
          const chosen = run.selected_option === o.id
          return (
            <article
              key={o.id}
              aria-label={`Option ${NAMES[o.id]}`}
              className={cn('flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-lg border bg-card p-3', chosen && 'border-emerald-400/70')}
            >
              <header className="flex items-center justify-between">
                <h3 className="font-medium text-zinc-100">{NAMES[o.id]}</h3>
                {chosen && (
                  <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-emerald-300">
                    <Check aria-hidden className="size-3.5" /> Selected
                  </span>
                )}
              </header>
              <dl className="flex flex-col gap-1">
                <Metric label="Installed" value={fmtQty(o.installed_mva, 'MVA')} />
                <Metric label="Loading normal / failover" value={`${fmtQty(o.normal_loading_pct, '%')} / ${fmtQty(o.failover_loading_pct, '%')}`} />
                <Metric label="Cost" value={fmtUsd(o.cost)} />
                <Metric label="Losses" value={fmtQty(o.losses_kw, 'kW')} />
                <Metric label="Max lead time" value={fmtQty(o.max_lead_time_weeks, 'weeks')} />
                <Metric label="Single points of failure" value={fmtNum(o.spof_count)} />
              </dl>
              {(o.swaps ?? []).length > 0 && (
                <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1 border-t pt-2 text-xs" aria-label="Swaps applied">
                  {(o.swaps ?? []).map((s, i) => (
                    <li key={i} className="flex items-center gap-1 font-mono text-green-300">
                      {String(s.from)} <ArrowRight aria-hidden className="size-3" /> {String(s.to)}
                      <span className="text-zinc-500">({String(s.reason)}, line {String(s.line)})</span>
                    </li>
                  ))}
                </ul>
              )}
              {atGate && (
                <Button size="sm" className="mt-auto" disabled={!!busy} onClick={() => void select(o.id)}>
                  Select {o.id}
                </Button>
              )}
            </article>
          )
        })}
      </div>
      {error && (
        <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-rose-300">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
      <IllustrativeLabel />
    </div>
  )
}
```

`apps/web/src/workspace/gates/GateBanner.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect } from 'react'
import { useUi } from '@/store/ui'
import { useWorkspace } from '../WorkspaceContext'
import { PlanGate } from './PlanGate'

/** Shows the interactive plan gate above the canvas and jumps to Options at the option gate. */
export function GateBanner() {
  const { run, source } = useWorkspace()
  const setTab = useUi((s) => s.setTab)
  const gate = source === 'live' && run?.status === 'at_gate' ? run.gate : null
  useEffect(() => {
    if (gate === 'option') setTab('options')
  }, [gate, setTab])
  if (gate !== 'plan' || !run) return null
  return (
    <div className="max-h-[55%] overflow-auto border-b p-3">
      <PlanGate run={run} interactive />
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { Download, FileText } from 'lucide-react'
import { EmptyState, IllustrativeLabel } from '@/components/Labels'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { exportsOf } from '@/lib/select'
import type { LedgerEntry } from '@/lib/types'
import { useServices } from '@/services/services'
import { useUi } from '@/store/ui'
import { useWorkspace } from '../WorkspaceContext'

const LEDGER_TITLES: Record<LedgerEntry['kind'], string> = {
  did: 'Did',
  assumed: 'Assumed',
  not_checked: 'Not checked in v0.1',
}

export function LedgerList({ ledger }: { ledger: LedgerEntry[] }) {
  return (
    <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-3 md:grid-cols-3">
      {(Object.keys(LEDGER_TITLES) as LedgerEntry['kind'][]).map((k) => (
        <section key={k} aria-label={LEDGER_TITLES[k]} className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          <h4 className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">{LEDGER_TITLES[k]}</h4>
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-xs text-zinc-300">
            {ledger
              .filter((e) => e.kind === k)
              .map((e, i) => (
                <li key={i} className="rounded border bg-card px-2 py-1">
                  {e.text}
                </li>
              ))}
          </ul>
        </section>
      ))}
    </div>
  )
}

/** Exports are public URLs for seed projects or storage paths served via GET /api/files/{path}. */
export function useOpenExport() {
  const { api } = useServices()
  return async (ref: string) => {
    if (ref.startsWith('/') || ref.startsWith('http')) {
      window.open(ref, '_blank', 'noopener')
      return
    }
    const blob = await api.fileBlob(ref)
    window.open(URL.createObjectURL(blob), '_blank', 'noopener')
  }
}

export function DocsTab() {
  const { bundle, rev } = useWorkspace()
  const openCitation = useUi((s) => s.openCitation)
  const openExport = useOpenExport()
  const docs = [...new Set((bundle.brief?.requirements ?? []).map((r) => r.citation.doc_id))]
  const exports = exportsOf(bundle)
  if (!docs.length && !exports.length && !rev) return <EmptyState title="No documents yet">The tender, drawings and exports appear as the run progresses.</EmptyState>
  return (
    <div className="flex h-full flex-col gap-5 overflow-auto p-3 text-sm">
      <section aria-label="Inputs" className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <h3 className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">Inputs</h3>
        {docs.map((d) => (
          <Button key={d} variant="outline" size="sm" className="self-start" onClick={() => openCitation({ doc_id: d, page: 1, quote: '', bbox: null, clause: null })}>
            <FileText aria-hidden /> {d}.pdf
          </Button>
        ))}
      </section>
      <section aria-label="Exports" className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <h3 className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">Exports</h3>
        {exports.length ? (
          exports.map((x) => (
            <Button key={x.ref} variant="outline" size="sm" className="self-start" onClick={() => void openExport(x.ref).catch(() => undefined)}>
              <Download aria-hidden /> {x.title}
            </Button>
          ))
        ) : (
          <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">The Docs agent publishes the calc report and BOQ before the review gate.</p>
        )}
      </section>
      {rev && (
        <section aria-label="Assumptions ledger" className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <h3 className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">Assumptions ledger · {rev.id}</h3>
          <LedgerList ledger={rev.ledger ?? []} />
        </section>
      )}
      <IllustrativeLabel />
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { Stamp } from 'lucide-react'
import { Link, useSearchParams } from 'react-router'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { latestRevision } from '@/lib/select'
import { useUi } from '@/store/ui'
import { AutonomySwitch } from './gates/AutonomySwitch'
import { useWorkspace } from './WorkspaceContext'

/** Header right side: [Ask|Auto] and [Request sign-off] (spec §12 workspace header). */
export function HeaderActions() {
  const { bundle, run } = useWorkspace()
  const autonomy = useUi((s) => s.autonomy)
  const setAutonomy = useUi((s) => s.setAutonomy)
  const [params] = useSearchParams()
  const latest = latestRevision(bundle)
  const atReview = run?.status === 'at_gate' && run.gate === 'review'
  const runQ = params.get('run') ? `?run=${params.get('run')}` : ''
  return (
    <>
      <AutonomySwitch value={run?.autonomy ?? autonomy} onChange={setAutonomy} locked={!!run} />
      {latest ? (
        <Button asChild size="sm" variant={atReview ? 'default' : 'outline'} data-tour="sign-off">
          <Link to={`/app/p/${bundle.project.id}/review/${latest.id}${runQ}`}>
            <Stamp aria-hidden /> {atReview ? 'Review and sign' : 'Request sign-off'}
          </Link>
        </Button>
      ) : (
        <Button size="sm" variant="outline" disabled>
          <Stamp aria-hidden /> Request sign-off
        </Button>
      )}
    </>
  )
}
```

`apps/web/src/pages/Integrations.tsx`:
```tsx
// Integrations (spec §13.3): ERP/CRM sandbox via /api/enterprise/*, documents and notifications from
// the static snapshot; falls back to /demo/integrations.json with a "showing cached snapshot" note.
import { Bell, Boxes, Cloud, Handshake, Puzzle, type LucideIcon } from 'lucide-react'
import { useEffect, useState, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { EmptyState, Num, SandboxLabel } from '@/components/Labels'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Sheet, SheetContent, SheetDescription, SheetHeader, SheetTitle } from '@/components/ui/sheet'
import { loadIntegrationsSnapshot } from '@/data/staticData'
import { fmtQty, fmtRelative, fmtTs, fmtUsd } from '@/lib/format'
import type { IntegrationsSnapshot } from '@/lib/types'
import { useServices } from '@/services/services'

export interface IntegrationsState {
  data: IntegrationsSnapshot | null
  live: boolean
  syncedAt: number | null
  error: string | null
}

export function useIntegrations(): IntegrationsState {
  const { api, getJson } = useServices()
  const [state, setState] = useState<IntegrationsState>({ data: null, live: false, syncedAt: null, error: null })
  useEffect(() => {
    let alive = true
    const snapshot = loadIntegrationsSnapshot(getJson)
    Promise.all([api.erpMaterials(), api.erpPrs(), api.crmOpportunities(), snapshot])
      .then(([materials, prs, opportunities, snap]) => {
        if (alive)
          setState({
            data: { ...snap, erp: { materials, prs }, crm: { opportunities } },
            live: true,
            syncedAt: Date.now() / 1000,
            error: null,
          })
      })
      .catch(() =>
        snapshot
          .then((snap) => alive && setState({ data: snap, live: false, syncedAt: snap.synced_at ?? null, error: null }))
          .catch((e: Error) => alive && setState({ data: null, live: false, syncedAt: null, error: e.message })),
      )
    return () => {
      alive = false
    }
  }, [api, getJson])
  return state
}

function Card({
  icon: Icon,
  title,
  kind,
  sandbox,
  status,
  counts,
  children,
  tour,
}: {
  icon: LucideIcon
  title: string
  kind: string
  sandbox: boolean
  status: string
  counts: string[]
  children?: ReactNode
  tour?: string
}) {
  return (
    <article aria-label={title} data-tour={tour} className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-lg border bg-card p-4">
      <header className="flex items-center gap-2">
        <Icon aria-hidden className="size-4 text-zinc-400" />
        <h2 className="font-medium text-zinc-100">{title}</h2>
        {sandbox && <SandboxLabel className="ml-auto" />}
      </header>
      <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400">{kind}</p>
      <p className="text-xs text-zinc-300">{status}</p>
      <ul className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5 text-xs">
        {counts.map((c) => (
          <li key={c} className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 font-mono text-zinc-200">
            {c}
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
      <div className="mt-auto">{children}</div>
    </article>
  )
}

function DataSheet({ title, label, children }: { title: string; label: string; children: ReactNode }) {
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  return (
    <>
      <Button size="xs" variant="outline" onClick={() => setOpen(true)} aria-label={`View data: ${title}`}>
        View data
      </Button>
      <Sheet open={open} onOpenChange={setOpen}>
        <SheetContent side="right" className="w-[42rem] overflow-y-auto sm:max-w-[42rem]">
          <SheetHeader>
            <SheetTitle>{title}</SheetTitle>
            <SheetDescription>{label}</SheetDescription>
          </SheetHeader>
          <div className="px-4 pb-6">{children}</div>
        </SheetContent>
      </Sheet>
    </>
  )
}

const th = 'border-b px-2 py-1 text-left font-medium text-zinc-400'
const td = 'border-b border-zinc-800/70 px-2 py-1'

export function IntegrationsPage() {
  const { data, live, syncedAt, error } = useIntegrations()
  if (error) return <EmptyState title="Integrations snapshot unavailable">{error}</EmptyState>
  if (!data) return <EmptyState title="Loading integrations…" />
  const status = live ? `Connected · last sync ${syncedAt ? fmtRelative(syncedAt) : 'now'}` : `Cached snapshot · ${syncedAt ? fmtTs(syncedAt) : ''}`
  const handoffPrs = data.erp.prs.filter((p) => p.project_id === 'hall-a' || p.project_id === 'hall-b')
  const handoffOpps = data.crm.opportunities.filter((o) => /hall [ab]/i.test(o.name))
  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex h-full max-w-6xl flex-col gap-5 overflow-auto p-6">
      <div className="flex flex-wrap items-baseline gap-3">
        <h1 className="text-lg font-semibold">Integrations</h1>
        {!live && (
          <span role="status" className="rounded bg-amber-500/10 px-2 py-0.5 text-xs text-amber-200">
            API unreachable — showing cached snapshot
          </span>
        )}
      </div>
      <section aria-label="Latest handoff" data-tour="integrations-prs" className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded-lg border bg-card p-4">
        <h2 className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">Latest handoff from signed designs</h2>
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm">
          {handoffPrs.map((p) => (
```

`apps/web/src/onboarding/checklist/checklistStore.ts`:
```ts
// Getting-started checklist (spec §13.5). Progress lives in localStorage so it works with the backend down.
import { create } from 'zustand'
import { readJson, writeJson } from '@/lib/storage'

export type ChecklistId = 'open_signed' | 'inspect_source' | 'run_whatif' | 'ask_copilot' | 'run_live' | 'approve'

export const CHECKLIST: { id: ChecklistId; label: string }[] = [
  { id: 'open_signed', label: 'Open a signed design' },
  { id: 'inspect_source', label: 'Inspect a finding and its source' },
  { id: 'run_whatif', label: 'Run a what-if' },
  { id: 'ask_copilot', label: 'Ask the co-pilot (voice or text)' },
  { id: 'run_live', label: 'Run Hall B live' },
  { id: 'approve', label: 'Approve a revision' },
]

const KEY = 'checklist'
type Done = Partial<Record<ChecklistId, true>>

interface ChecklistStore {
  done: Done
  complete(id: ChecklistId): void
  reload(): void
}

export const useChecklist = create<ChecklistStore>()((set, get) => ({
  done: readJson<Done>(KEY, {}),
  complete: (id) => {
    if (get().done[id]) return
    const done = { ...get().done, [id]: true as const }
    writeJson(KEY, done)
    set({ done })
  },
  reload: () => set({ done: readJson<Done>(KEY, {}) }),
}))

export function completeItem(id: ChecklistId): void {
  useChecklist.getState().complete(id)
}
```

```tsx
import { ChevronDown, Circle, CircleCheck } from 'lucide-react'
import { useState } from 'react'
import { cn } from 'cn'
import { CHECKLIST, useChecklist } from './checklistStore'

/** Bottom-left getting-started card; hidden in presentation mode (data-dev). */
export function Checklist() {
  const done = useChecklist((s) => s.done)
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(true)
  const count = CHECKLIST.filter((c) => done[c.id]).length
  return (
    <aside data-dev aria-label="Getting started" className="fixed bottom-14 left-3 z-40 w-64 rounded-lg border bg-popover text-sm shadow-xl">
      <button type="button" aria-expanded={open} onClick={() => setOpen(!open)} className="flex w-full items-center gap-2 px-3 py-2">
        <span className="font-medium">Getting started</span>
        <span className="font-mono text-xs text-zinc-400">
          {count} of {CHECKLIST.length}
        </span>
        <ChevronDown aria-hidden className={cn('ml-auto size-4 text-zinc-500 transition-transform', open && 'rotate-180')} />
      </button>
      {open && (
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1 px-3 pb-3">
          {CHECKLIST.map((c) => (
            <li key={c.id} className={cn('flex items-center gap-2 text-xs', done[c.id] ? 'text-zinc-400 line-through' : 'text-zinc-200')}>
              {done[c.id] ? (
                <CircleCheck aria-label="done" className="size-3.5 text-emerald-400" />
              ) : (
                <Circle aria-label="to do" className="size-3.5 text-zinc-500" />
              )}
              {c.label}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      )}
    </aside>
  )
}
```

`apps/web/src/onboarding/tour/tourSteps.ts`:
```ts
// The 7-step guided tour (spec §13.5). Text never states a number that is not counted from data.
import { agentsInvolved } from '@/lib/select'
import type { Bundle } from '@/lib/types'

export interface TourContext {
  /** Hall A's static bundle if it has been loaded this session. */
  hallA: Bundle | null
}

export interface TourStep {
  id: string
  route: (ctx: TourContext) => string
  target: string
  title: (ctx: TourContext) => string
  body: string
}

/** The earliest revision with a failing finding, and that finding (tour step 3 opens it). */
export function firstFailure(b: Bundle | null): { rev: string; id: string } | null {
  for (const r of b?.revisions ?? []) {
    const f = (b!.findings[r.id] ?? []).find((x) => x.status === 'fail')
    if (f) return { rev: r.id, id: f.id }
  }
  return null
}

export const TOUR_STEPS: TourStep[] = [
  {
    id: 'projects',
    route: () => '/app',
    target: '[data-tour="projects"]',
    title: () => "Your firm's live projects",
    body: 'Meridian Power Engineering designs AI data halls, grid connections and solar fields here.',
  },
  {
    id: 'matrix',
    route: () => '/app/p/hall-a?tab=matrix',
    target: '[data-tour="matrix-row"]',
    title: () => 'Every number is checked and cited',
    body: 'Hall A is signed. Each cell is a deterministic rule check on one element; click any cell to see why.',
  },
  {
    id: 'finding',
    route: (ctx) => {
      const f = firstFailure(ctx.hallA)
      return f ? `/app/p/hall-a?tab=matrix&rev=${f.rev}&finding=${encodeURIComponent(f.id)}` : '/app/p/hall-a?tab=matrix'
    },
    target: '[data-tour="tender-chip"]',
    title: () => 'Open a finding and its source',
    body: 'This failure was caught on the first revision. Click the tender chip: the PDF opens at the clause, highlighted.',
  },
  {
    id: 'lanes',
    route: () => '/app/p/hall-a',
    target: '[data-tour="lanes"]',
    title: (ctx) => {
      const n = ctx.hallA ? agentsInvolved(ctx.hallA).length : null
      return n ? `${n} agents designed this; here's what each did` : "The agents that designed this, and what each did"
    },
    body: 'Each lane is one agent: its tool calls, results, findings and the assumptions it logged.',
  },
  {
    id: 'whatif',
    route: () => '/app/p/hall-a',
    target: '[data-tour="whatif-chip"]',
    title: () => 'Try it: racks to 200 kW',
    body: 'The engine re-checks a shadow copy of the signed design. Nothing is saved; every failure lights up across power and cooling.',
  },
  {
    id: 'integrations',
    route: () => '/app/integrations',
    target: '[data-tour="integrations-prs"]',
    title: () => 'Approval pushed these PRs to ERP and moved the CRM deal',
    body: 'Engineering, procurement and sales in one workflow (sandbox systems).',
  },
  {
    id: 'hall-b',
    route: () => '/app',
    target: '[data-tour="hall-b"]',
    title: () => 'Now run it live yourself',
    body: "Hall B is Hall A's twin. Start a live run in your own workspace, or watch a recorded one.",
  },
]
```

`apps/web/src/onboarding/tour/tour.ts`:
```ts
import { create } from 'zustand'
import { readJson, writeJson } from '@/lib/storage'
import { TOUR_STEPS } from './tourSteps'

interface Persisted {
  active: boolean
  step: number
  done: boolean
}
interface TourStore extends Persisted {
  start(): void
  next(): void
  prev(): void
  stop(completed: boolean): void
  reload(): void
}

const KEY = 'tour'
const initial = (): Persisted => readJson<Persisted>(KEY, { active: false, step: 0, done: false })

/** Tour progress persists in localStorage (spec §13.4), so a reload resumes the same step. */
export const useTour = create<TourStore>()((set, get) => {
  const save = (p: Persisted) => {
    writeJson(KEY, p)
    set(p)
  }
  return {
    ...initial(),
    start: () => save({ active: true, step: 0, done: get().done }),
    next: () => {
      const s = get()
      if (s.step >= TOUR_STEPS.length - 1) save({ active: false, step: 0, done: true })
      else save({ ...s, step: s.step + 1 })
    },
    prev: () => {
      const s = get()
      save({ ...s, step: Math.max(0, s.step - 1) })
    },
    stop: (completed) => save({ active: false, step: 0, done: completed || get().done }),
    reload: () => set(initial()),
  }
})
```

`apps/web/src/onboarding/tour/TourHost.tsx`:
```tsx
// In-house spotlight tour on shadcn Popover (spec §12 libraries, §13.5).
import { useEffect, useState } from 'react'
import { useLocation, useNavigate } from 'react-router'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Popover, PopoverAnchor, PopoverContent } from '@/components/ui/popover'
import { useBundleStore } from '@/store/bundles'
import { useUi } from '@/store/ui'
import { useTour } from './tour'
import { TOUR_STEPS } from './tourSteps'

export function TourHost() {
  const { active, step, next, prev, stop } = useTour()
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const location = useLocation()
  const hallA = useBundleStore((s) => s.entries['hall-a:static']?.bundle ?? null)
  const [rect, setRect] = useState<DOMRect | null>(null)
  const s = TOUR_STEPS[step]
  const ctx = { hallA }
  const route = s.route(ctx)

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!active) return
    // Close drawers from the previous step; step 3 re-opens its finding through ?finding=.
    const ui = useUi.getState()
    ui.closeCitation()
    if (s.id !== 'finding') ui.closeFinding()
    if (`${location.pathname}${location.search}` !== route) navigate(route)
    // eslint-disable-next-line react-hooks/exhaustive-deps
  }, [active, step])

  // Wait (up to ~4 s) for the step's anchor to appear, then spotlight it.
  useEffect(() => {
    if (!active) return
    let raf = 0
    let tries = 0
    setRect(null)
    const find = () => {
      const el = document.querySelector(s.target)
      if (el) {
        el.scrollIntoView({ block: 'center' })
        setRect(el.getBoundingClientRect())
      } else if (tries++ < 240) raf = requestAnimationFrame(find)
    }
    find()
    return () => cancelAnimationFrame(raf)
  }, [active, step, location.key, s.target])

  useEffect(() => {
    if (!active) return
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (e.key === 'ArrowRight') next()
      if (e.key === 'ArrowLeft') prev()
    }
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey)
  }, [active, next, prev])

  if (!active) return null
  const box = rect
    ? { left: rect.left - 6, top: rect.top - 6, width: rect.width + 12, height: rect.height + 12 }
    : { left: window.innerWidth / 2, top: window.innerHeight / 3, width: 1, height: 1 }
  const last = step === TOUR_STEPS.length - 1
  return (
    // Re-keyed when the anchor is found so the popover layer mounts above any drawer opened by the step.
    <Popover open key={`${step}:${rect ? 'found' : 'none'}`}>
      <PopoverAnchor asChild>
        <div data-testid="tour-anchor" className={rect ? 'tour-spotlight' : 'pointer-events-none fixed'} style={box} />
      </PopoverAnchor>
      <PopoverContent
        side="bottom"
        align="start"
        className="z-[70] w-80"
        aria-label="Guided tour"
        onEscapeKeyDown={() => stop(false)}
        onInteractOutside={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
        onOpenAutoFocus={(e) => e.preventDefault()}
      >
        <p className="font-mono text-[11px] text-zinc-500">
          Step {step + 1} of {TOUR_STEPS.length}
        </p>
        <h2 className="mt-1 text-sm font-semibold text-zinc-50">{s.title(ctx)}</h2>
        <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-300">{s.body}</p>
        {!rect && <p className="mt-1 text-xs text-zinc-500">Loading this part of the workspace…</p>}
        <div className="mt-3 flex items-center gap-2">
          <Button size="xs" variant="ghost" onClick={() => stop(false)}>
            Skip tour
          </Button>
          <Button size="xs" variant="outline" className="ml-auto" disabled={step === 0} onClick={prev}>
            Back
          </Button>
          <Button size="xs" onClick={next}>
            {last ? 'Finish' : 'Next'}
          </Button>
        </div>
      </PopoverContent>
    </Popover>
  )
}
```

`apps/web/src/components/Hint.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState, type ReactElement } from 'react'
import { Tooltip, TooltipContent, TooltipTrigger } from '@/components/ui/tooltip'
import { readJson, writeJson } from '@/lib/storage'

/** One-line first-hover tip (spec §13.5 contextual hints); never shown again once dismissed. */
export function Hint({ id, text, children }: { id: string; text: string; children: ReactElement }) {
  const key = `hints.${id}`
  const [seen, setSeen] = useState(() => readJson<boolean>(key, false))
  const [open, setOpen] = useState(false)
  if (seen) return children
  return (
    <Tooltip
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={(o) => {
        if (!o && open) {
          writeJson(key, true)
          setSeen(true)
        }
        setOpen(o)
      }}
    >
      <TooltipTrigger asChild>{children}</TooltipTrigger>
      <TooltipContent data-dev className="max-w-xs">
        {text}
      </TooltipContent>
    </Tooltip>
  )
}
```

`apps/web/src/shell/useShortcuts.ts`:
```ts
import { useEffect, useRef } from 'react'
import { useNavigate } from 'react-router'

const typing = (t: EventTarget | null) =>
  t instanceof HTMLElement && (t.isContentEditable || ['INPUT', 'TEXTAREA', 'SELECT'].includes(t.tagName))

/** Keyboard (spec §13.5): `?` help and tour, `g p` projects, `g i` integrations. */
export function useShortcuts(onHelp: () => void): void {
  const navigate = useNavigate()
  const pendingG = useRef(0)
  useEffect(() => {
    const onKey = (e: KeyboardEvent) => {
      if (typing(e.target) || e.metaKey || e.ctrlKey || e.altKey) return
      if (e.key === '?') {
        e.preventDefault()
        onHelp()
        return
      }
      if (e.key === 'g') {
        pendingG.current = Date.now()
        return
      }
      if (Date.now() - pendingG.current < 1000) {
        if (e.key === 'p') navigate('/app')
        if (e.key === 'i') navigate('/app/integrations')
        pendingG.current = 0
      }
    }
    window.addEventListener('keydown', onKey)
    return () => window.removeEventListener('keydown', onKey)
  }, [navigate, onHelp])
}
```

`apps/web/src/shell/ShortcutsDialog.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Dialog, DialogContent, DialogDescription, DialogHeader, DialogTitle } from '@/components/ui/dialog'
import { clearKirchhoffStorage } from '@/lib/storage'
import { useTour } from '@/onboarding/tour/tour'
import { useServices } from '@/services/services'

const KEYS: [string, string][] = [
  ['?', 'Shortcuts, tour and reset'],
  ['g then p', 'Projects'],
  ['g then i', 'Integrations'],
  ['← →', 'Previous / next tour step'],
  ['Esc', 'Close a drawer, dialog or the tour'],
]

export function ShortcutsDialog({ open, onOpenChange }: { open: boolean; onOpenChange: (o: boolean) => void }) {
  const { api } = useServices()
  const start = useTour((s) => s.start)
  const [confirm, setConfirm] = useState(false)

  const reset = async () => {
    // Deletes the visitor's Firestore overlay (live runs, approvals, PRs, CRM stage) and local progress.
    await api.resetWorkspace().catch(() => undefined)
    clearKirchhoffStorage()
    window.location.assign('/app')
  }

  return (
    <Dialog
      open={open}
      onOpenChange={(o) => {
        setConfirm(false)
        onOpenChange(o)
      }}
    >
      <DialogContent className="sm:max-w-md">
        <DialogHeader>
          <DialogTitle>Help and shortcuts</DialogTitle>
          <DialogDescription>Keyboard works everywhere except while typing.</DialogDescription>
        </DialogHeader>
        <dl className="grid grid-cols-[6rem_1fr] gap-y-1 text-sm">
          {KEYS.map(([k, v]) => (
            <div key={k} className="contents">
              <dt>
                <kbd className="rounded border border-zinc-600 bg-zinc-800 px-1.5 font-mono text-xs">{k}</kbd>
              </dt>
              <dd className="text-zinc-300">{v}</dd>
            </div>
          ))}
        </dl>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          <Button
            size="sm"
            onClick={() => {
              onOpenChange(false)
              start()
            }}
          >
            Restart the tour
          </Button>
          {confirm ? (
            <Button size="sm" variant="destructive" onClick={() => void reset()}>
              Confirm: reset my demo workspace
            </Button>
          ) : (
            <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => setConfirm(true)}>
              Reset demo workspace
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
      </DialogContent>
    </Dialog>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { Stamp } from 'lucide-react'
import { fmtTs, shortHash } from '@/lib/format'
import type { Approval, Revision } from '@/lib/types'

/** Reviewer stamp (spec §12 screen 7): name, role, time and the revision hash it signs. */
export function ApprovalStamp({ approval, revision }: { approval: Approval; revision: Revision }) {
  const approved = approval.decision === 'approved'
  return (
    <figure
      aria-label="Approval stamp"
      className={`flex max-w-md flex-col gap-1 rounded-lg border-2 p-4 ${approved ? 'border-emerald-400/70 bg-emerald-500/5' : 'border-rose-400/70 bg-rose-500/5'}`}
    >
      <span className={`inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-semibold uppercase tracking-widest ${approved ? 'text-emerald-300' : 'text-rose-300'}`}>
        <Stamp aria-hidden className="size-5" /> {approved ? 'Approved' : 'Rejected'}
      </span>
      <span className="text-base text-zinc-50">{approval.approver_name}</span>
      <span className="text-sm text-zinc-300">{approval.role}</span>
      <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400">{fmtTs(approval.ts)}</span>
      <span className="font-mono text-xs text-zinc-400" title={revision.hash}>
        Revision {revision.id} · sha256 {shortHash(revision.hash)}
      </span>
      {approval.comment && <figcaption className="mt-1 text-sm text-zinc-300">“{approval.comment}”</figcaption>}
    </figure>
  )
}
```

`apps/web/src/workspace/review/ReviewPage.tsx`:
```tsx
// Review and sign-off (spec §12 screen 7): diff vs parent, findings summary, assumptions ledger,
// Approve with stamp; after approval the PRs (ERP) and CRM stage handoff (spec §3 1:58–2:18).
import { ArrowLeft, Download } from 'lucide-react'
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Link, useParams, useSearchParams } from 'react-router'
import { EmptyState, IllustrativeLabel, Num, SandboxLabel } from '@/components/Labels'
import { StatusBadge } from '@/components/StatusBadge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Textarea } from '@/components/ui/textarea'
import { useProjectData } from '@/data/useProjectData'
import { useWorldProject } from '@/data/useWorld'
import { diffModels, type ElementChange } from '@/lib/diff'
import { fmtNum, fmtTs, shortHash } from '@/lib/format'
import { countByStatus, currentRun, exportsOf, findingsFor, parentRevision, revisionById, type DataSource } from '@/lib/select'
import { STATUS_META, statusRank } from '@/lib/status'
import type { Bundle } from '@/lib/types'
import { completeItem } from '@/onboarding/checklist/checklistStore'
import { useServices } from '@/services/services'
import { LedgerList, useOpenExport } from '../docs/DocsTab'
import { ApprovalStamp } from './ApprovalStamp'

function show(v: unknown): string {
  if (typeof v === 'number') return fmtNum(v)
  if (v === null || v === undefined) return '—'
  if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.length && typeof v[0] === 'object' ? 'route' : v.join(', ')
  return String(v)
}

function ChangeLine({ c }: { c: ElementChange }) {
  return (
    <li className="flex flex-col gap-0.5 rounded border bg-card px-2 py-1 text-xs">
      <span>
        <span className="font-mono text-zinc-100">{c.id}</span> <span className="text-zinc-400">{c.kind}</span>
      </span>
      {c.fields.map((f) => (
        <span key={f.field} className="font-mono text-zinc-300">
          {f.field}: {f.field === 'route' ? 'rerouted' : `${show(f.before)} → ${show(f.after)}`}
        </span>
      ))}
    </li>
  )
}

export function ReviewView({ bundle, source, revId, runQuery }: { bundle: Bundle; source: DataSource; revId: string; runQuery: string }) {
  const { api } = useServices()
  const openExport = useOpenExport()
  const [comment, setComment] = useState('')
  const [sent, setSent] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const rev = revisionById(bundle, revId)
  if (!rev) return <EmptyState title={`Revision ${revId} not found`}>It may not have been created yet in this run.</EmptyState>
  const parent = parentRevision(bundle, rev)
  const findings = findingsFor(bundle, rev.id)
  const counts = countByStatus(findings)
  const open = findings.filter((f) => f.status !== 'pass').sort((a, b) => statusRank(b.status) - statusRank(a.status))
  const diff = parent ? diffModels(parent.model, rev.model) : null
  const approval = bundle.approvals.filter((a) => a.revision === rev.id).sort((a, b) => b.ts - a.ts)[0] ?? null
  const run = currentRun(bundle)
  const atReview = source === 'live' && run?.status === 'at_gate' && run.gate === 'review'
  const prs = bundle.prs.filter((p) => p.revision === rev.id)
  const workspace = `/app/p/${bundle.project.id}${runQuery}`

  const decide = async (decision: 'approved' | 'rejected') => {
    if (!run) return
    setBusy(true)
    try {
      await api.reviewGate(run.id, decision, comment)
      if (decision === 'approved') completeItem('approve')
      setSent(decision === 'approved' ? 'Approval sent. Purchase requisitions and the CRM update follow.' : 'Rejection sent.')
    } catch {
      setSent('Could not reach the API; try again.')
    } finally {
      setBusy(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex h-full max-w-5xl flex-col gap-6 overflow-auto p-6">
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-3">
        <Button asChild variant="ghost" size="sm">
          <Link to={workspace}>
            <ArrowLeft aria-hidden /> Workspace
          </Link>
        </Button>
        <h1 className="text-lg font-semibold">
          Review · {bundle.project.name} · <span className="font-mono">{rev.id}</span>
        </h1>
        <span className="font-mono text-xs text-zinc-400" title={rev.hash}>
          sha256 {shortHash(rev.hash)}
        </span>
        <IllustrativeLabel className="ml-auto" />
      </header>

      <section aria-label="Findings summary" className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <h2 className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">Checker summary</h2>
        <p className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 text-sm">
          <span className="font-mono text-zinc-200">{findings.length} checks</span>
          {(['fail', 'warn', 'pass', 'n/a'] as const).map((s) => (
            <span key={s} className={`font-mono ${STATUS_META[s].text}`}>
              {counts[s]} {STATUS_META[s].label.toLowerCase()}
            </span>
          ))}
        </p>
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          {open.map((f) => (
            <li key={f.id} className="flex items-center gap-2 text-sm">
              <StatusBadge status={f.status} />
              <Link className="font-mono text-zinc-200 hover:underline" to={`${workspace}${runQuery ? '&' : '?'}tab=matrix&rev=${rev.id}&finding=${encodeURIComponent(f.id)}`}>
                {f.rule_id} · {f.element_id}
              </Link>
              <span className="text-zinc-400">{f.title}</span>
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </section>

      <section aria-label="Changes from parent" className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <h2 className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">
          {parent ? `Changes from ${parent.id}` : 'First revision (no parent)'}
        </h2>
        {diff && (diff.changes.length ? <ul className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-2 md:grid-cols-2">{diff.changes.map((c) => <ChangeLine key={c.id} c={c} />)}</ul> : <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">No model changes.</p>)}
      </section>

      <section aria-label="Assumptions ledger" className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <h2 className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">Assumptions ledger</h2>
        <LedgerList ledger={rev.ledger ?? []} />
      </section>

      <section aria-label="Sign-off" className="flex flex-col gap-3">
        <h2 className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">Sign-off</h2>
        {approval ? (
          <ApprovalStamp approval={approval} revision={rev} />
        ) : atReview ? (
          <div className="flex max-w-xl flex-col gap-2">
            <Textarea aria-label="Review comment" placeholder="Comment for the record (optional)" value={comment} onChange={(e) => setComment(e.target.value)} />
            {counts.fail > 0 && (
              <p role="alert" className="text-sm text-rose-300">
                Approve is disabled: {counts.fail} findings still fail after the fix loop. Reject, or open the findings to see why.
              </p>
            )}
            <div className="flex gap-2">
              <Button disabled={busy || counts.fail > 0 || !!sent} onClick={() => void decide('approved')}>
                Approve and sign as You (Demo Reviewer)
              </Button>
              <Button variant="outline" disabled={busy || !!sent} onClick={() => void decide('rejected')}>
                Reject
              </Button>
            </div>
            {sent && <p className="text-sm text-zinc-300">{sent}</p>}
          </div>
        ) : (
          <p className="text-sm text-zinc-400">
            Sign-off happens at the review gate of a live run. Start one on Hall B to sign as You (Demo Reviewer).
          </p>
        )}
      </section>

      {(prs.length > 0 || bundle.crm) && approval?.decision === 'approved' && (
        <section aria-label="Handoff" className="flex flex-col gap-2">
          <h2 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">
            Handoff <SandboxLabel />
          </h2>
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm">
            {prs.map((p) => (
              <li key={p.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <span className="font-mono text-zinc-100">{p.id}</span>
                <span className={p.status === 'blocked' ? 'text-rose-300' : 'text-zinc-300'}>
                  {p.status === 'blocked' ? 'blocked: SLD, BOQ and PR lines disagree (R12)' : p.status}
                </span>
                <span className="text-zinc-400">
                  {p.lines.map((l) => `${l.qty} × ${l.material_id}`).join(', ')} · {fmtTs(p.created_at)}
                </span>
              </li>
            ))}
            {bundle.crm && (
              <li className="text-zinc-300">
                CRM opportunity moved to <span className="text-zinc-50">{bundle.crm.stage}</span>
              </li>
            )}
          </ul>
        </section>
      )}

      {exportsOf(bundle).length > 0 && (
        <section aria-label="Exports" className="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
          {exportsOf(bundle).map((x) => (
            <Button key={x.ref} variant="outline" size="sm" onClick={() => void openExport(x.ref).catch(() => undefined)}>
              <Download aria-hidden /> {x.title}
            </Button>
          ))}
        </section>
      )}
      <p className="text-xs text-zinc-500">
        <Num>{findings.length}</Num> deterministic checks by the rule pack; the AI cannot mark its own homework.
      </p>
    </div>
  )
}

export function ReviewPage() {
  const { pid = '', rev = '' } = useParams()
  const [params] = useSearchParams()
  const runId = params.get('run')
  const project = useWorldProject(pid)
  const data = useProjectData(pid, { runId, project })
  if (!data.bundle) return <EmptyState title={data.status === 'error' ? 'This project is not available' : 'Loading review…'}>{data.error}</EmptyState>
  return <ReviewView bundle={data.bundle} source={data.source} revId={rev} runQuery={runId ? `?run=${runId}` : ''} />
}
```

### Task 16: What-if chips and impact chain (Thu)

Spec §6.4 and storyboard 1:40–1:58: chips for the engine's preset what-ifs (racks → 200 kW, racks → 150 kW, liquid fraction → 0.9, ΔV limit → 1.5 %, transformer loading cap → 35 %; WS1 precomputes these for Hall A) plus a custom parameter/value input. A chip first looks for a precomputed `WhatIfResult` in the bundle (so tour step 5 works with the backend down), otherwise calls `POST /api/what-if`. The result (failing findings only, per WS1) shows as a "not saved" banner with value vs limit, and the impact chain lights the SLD, 3D hall and matrix in two colours (electrical amber, cooling blue) through the `whatIf` overlay that Tasks 7–9 already read.

`apps/web/src/workspace/copilot/useWhatIf.ts`:
```ts
// What-if (spec §6.4): precomputed results from the static bundle first (works with the backend
// down), then POST /api/what-if. Results are shown, never saved.
import { useState } from 'react'
import { fmtQty } from '@/lib/format'
import { findWhatIf } from '@/lib/select'
import type { WhatIfParam, WhatIfResult } from '@/lib/types'
import { completeItem } from '@/onboarding/checklist/checklistStore'
import { useServices } from '@/services/services'
import { useUi } from '@/store/ui'
import { useWorkspace } from '../WorkspaceContext'

export interface WhatIfChip {
  param: WhatIfParam
  value: number
}

/** The engine's chip set (WS1 precomputes these for Hall A). Values are user inputs, not results. */
export const WHAT_IF_PRESETS: WhatIfChip[] = [
  { param: 'rack_kw', value: 200 },
  { param: 'rack_kw', value: 150 },
  { param: 'liquid_fraction', value: 0.9 },
  { param: 'dv_limit_pct', value: 1.5 },
  { param: 'tx_loading_cap_pct', value: 35 },
]

const PARAM_LABEL: Record<WhatIfParam, { label: string; unit: string | null }> = {
  rack_kw: { label: 'Racks', unit: 'kW' },
  liquid_fraction: { label: 'Liquid fraction', unit: null },
  dv_limit_pct: { label: 'ΔV limit', unit: '%' },
  tx_loading_cap_pct: { label: 'Transformer loading cap', unit: '%' },
}

export function chipLabel(c: WhatIfChip): string {
  const p = PARAM_LABEL[c.param]
  return `${p.label} → ${fmtQty(c.value, p.unit)}`
}

export function paramLabel(p: WhatIfParam): string {
  return PARAM_LABEL[p].label
}

export function chipsFor(precomputed: WhatIfResult[]): WhatIfChip[] {
  const all = [...WHAT_IF_PRESETS, ...precomputed.map((w) => ({ param: w.param, value: w.value }))]
  return all.filter((c, i) => all.findIndex((d) => d.param === c.param && d.value === c.value) === i)
}

export function useWhatIf() {
  const view = useWorkspace()
  const { api } = useServices()
  const setWhatIf = useUi((s) => s.setWhatIf)
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)

  const run = async (param: WhatIfParam, value: number): Promise<WhatIfResult | null> => {
    if (!view.revRef) return null
    setError(null)
    const ui = useUi.getState()
    if (ui.tab === 'options' || ui.tab === 'docs') ui.setTab('sld')
    const pre = findWhatIf(view.bundle, view.revRef, param, value)
    if (pre) {
      setWhatIf({ result: pre, origin: 'precomputed' })
      completeItem('run_whatif')
      return pre
    }
    setLoading(true)
    try {
      const result = await api.whatIf(view.revRef, param, value)
      setWhatIf({ result, origin: 'engine' })
      completeItem('run_whatif')
      return result
    } catch {
      setError('The engine is unreachable right now; the sample hall’s precomputed chips still work.')
      return null
    } finally {
      setLoading(false)
    }
  }
  return { run, loading, error, clear: () => setWhatIf(null) }
}
```

`apps/web/src/workspace/copilot/WhatIfPanel.tsx`:
```tsx
import { LoaderCircle, X } from 'lucide-react'
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Num } from '@/components/Labels'
import { StatusBadge } from '@/components/StatusBadge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import { CHAIN_COLORS } from '@/lib/agents'
import { fmtQty } from '@/lib/format'
import { classifyChain } from '@/lib/impact'
import type { WhatIfParam } from '@/lib/types'
import { useUi } from '@/store/ui'
import { useWorkspace } from '../WorkspaceContext'
import { chipLabel, chipsFor, paramLabel, useWhatIf, WHAT_IF_PRESETS } from './useWhatIf'

const PARAMS: WhatIfParam[] = ['rack_kw', 'liquid_fraction', 'dv_limit_pct', 'tx_loading_cap_pct']

/** What-if chips + custom input + result with the two-colour impact chain (spec §3 1:40–1:58). */
export function WhatIfPanel() {
  const view = useWorkspace()
  const active = useUi((s) => s.whatIf)
  const { run, loading, error, clear } = useWhatIf()
  const [param, setParam] = useState<WhatIfParam>('rack_kw')
  const [value, setValue] = useState('')
  const chips = chipsFor(view.bundle.what_ifs.filter((w) => w.revision_ref === view.revRef))
  if (!view.rev) return null

  return (
    <section aria-label="What-if" className="flex flex-col gap-2 border-b p-3">
      <h3 className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">What if…</h3>
      <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-1.5">
        {chips.map((c, i) => (
          <Button
            key={`${c.param}:${c.value}`}
            size="xs"
            variant="outline"
            data-tour={i === 0 ? 'whatif-chip' : undefined}
            disabled={loading}
            onClick={() => void run(c.param, c.value)}
          >
            {chipLabel(c)}
          </Button>
        ))}
      </div>
      <form
        className="flex items-center gap-1.5"
        onSubmit={(e) => {
          e.preventDefault()
          const v = Number(value)
          if (value.trim() && Number.isFinite(v)) void run(param, v)
        }}
      >
        <select
          aria-label="What-if parameter"
          value={param}
          onChange={(e) => setParam(e.target.value as WhatIfParam)}
          className="h-7 rounded border bg-transparent px-1 text-xs"
        >
          {PARAMS.map((p) => (
            <option key={p} value={p} className="bg-zinc-900">
              {paramLabel(p)}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
        <Input
          aria-label="What-if value"
          inputMode="decimal"
          placeholder={String(WHAT_IF_PRESETS.find((c) => c.param === param)?.value ?? '')}
          value={value}
          onChange={(e) => setValue(e.target.value)}
          className="h-7 w-20 text-xs"
        />
        <Button size="xs" type="submit" disabled={loading}>
          {loading ? <LoaderCircle aria-hidden className="animate-spin" /> : null} Run
        </Button>
      </form>
      {error && (
        <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-amber-200">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
      {active && (
        <div className="flex flex-col gap-2 rounded border border-amber-400/40 bg-amber-500/5 p-2 text-xs" aria-live="polite">
          <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
            <span className="font-medium text-amber-100">{chipLabel(active.result)}</span>
            <span className="text-zinc-400">not saved · {active.origin === 'precomputed' ? 'precomputed by the engine' : 'engine, just now'}</span>
            <Button size="icon-xs" variant="ghost" className="ml-auto" aria-label="Clear what-if" onClick={clear}>
              <X aria-hidden />
            </Button>
          </div>
          <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1" aria-label="What-if failures">
            {active.result.findings.map((f) => (
              <li key={f.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-1.5">
                <StatusBadge status={f.status} compact />
                <span className="font-mono text-zinc-100">
                  {f.rule_id} · {f.element_id}
                </span>
                {typeof f.value === 'number' && (
                  <span className="text-zinc-400">
                    <Num>{fmtQty(f.value, f.unit)}</Num> vs limit <Num>{fmtQty(f.limit ?? null, f.unit)}</Num>
                  </span>
                )}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
          <ol className="flex flex-col gap-1" aria-label="Impact chain">
            {active.result.impact_chain.map((chain) => {
              const d = classifyChain(chain, view.model)
              return (
                <li key={chain.join('>')} className="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <span aria-hidden className="size-2 shrink-0 rounded-full" style={{ background: CHAIN_COLORS[d] }} />
                  <span className="text-zinc-400">{d === 'electrical' ? 'Electrical' : 'Cooling'}:</span>
                  <span className="font-mono text-zinc-200">{chain.join(' → ')}</span>
                </li>
              )
            })}
          </ol>
        </div>
      )}
    </section>
  )
}
```

```tsx
  expect(parseIntent('hello')).toBeNull()
})
```
