- [ ] **Step 3: Implement intents and tools**

`apps/web/src/workspace/copilot/intent.ts`:
```ts
// Typed co-pilot input → one of the three co-pilot tools (spec §6.5). Deterministic, no LLM:
// there is no text-chat endpoint, and typed chips must work with the backend down.
import type { WhatIfParam } from '@/lib/types'

export type Intent =
  | { tool: 'what_if'; param: WhatIfParam; value: number }
  | { tool: 'explain_finding'; element: string; rule?: string }
  | { tool: 'focus_element'; element: string }

const NUM = '(\\d+(?:\\.\\d+)?)'
const ELEMENT = /\b([A-Z]{1,3}\d?-[A-Z]?\d{1,2}(?:-\d{2})?|[A-Z]\d)\b/i
const RULE = /\b(R\d{2}b?)\b/i

export function parseIntent(text: string): Intent | null {
  const t = text.trim()
  if (/^what\s*if|^if\b/i.test(t) || /\bwhat-if\b/i.test(t)) {
    const rack = t.match(new RegExp(`rack\\w*\\D*?${NUM}\\s*kw`, 'i'))
    if (rack) return { tool: 'what_if', param: 'rack_kw', value: Number(rack[1]) }
    const liquid = t.match(new RegExp(`liquid\\D*?${NUM}`, 'i'))
    if (liquid) return { tool: 'what_if', param: 'liquid_fraction', value: Number(liquid[1]) }
    const dv = t.match(new RegExp(`(?:ΔV|dv|voltage drop)\\D*?${NUM}`, 'i'))
    if (dv) return { tool: 'what_if', param: 'dv_limit_pct', value: Number(dv[1]) }
    const tx = t.match(new RegExp(`transformer\\D*?${NUM}`, 'i'))
    if (tx) return { tool: 'what_if', param: 'tx_loading_cap_pct', value: Number(tx[1]) }
    return null
  }
  const el = t.match(ELEMENT)?.[1]?.toUpperCase()
  if (/^(why|explain)\b/i.test(t) && el) {
    const rule = t.match(RULE)?.[1]
    return { tool: 'explain_finding', element: el, rule: rule ? rule.toUpperCase().replace('B', 'b') : undefined }
  }
  if (/^(show|focus|where|zoom|find)\b/i.test(t) && el) return { tool: 'focus_element', element: el }
  return null
}

export const INTENT_HELP = 'Try: “what if racks go to 200 kW”, “why FA-01”, “show H-Z2”.'
```

`apps/web/src/workspace/copilot/tools.ts`:
```ts
// The co-pilot's three tools (spec §6.5), executed in the browser against /api and the bundle.
// Typed input, Gemini Live function calls and push-to-talk results all go through here.
// Summaries are built from engine records only (spec §6.3).
import { fillPlaceholders, fmtQty } from '@/lib/format'
import { statusRank } from '@/lib/status'
import type { Finding, WhatIfParam, WhatIfResult } from '@/lib/types'
import type { ApiClient } from '@/services/apiClient'
import type { WorkspaceView } from '../WorkspaceContext'

export interface ToolContext {
  view: WorkspaceView
  api: ApiClient
  runWhatIf: (param: WhatIfParam, value: number) => Promise<WhatIfResult | null>
  focus: (id: string) => void
  openFinding: (id: string) => void
}

export interface ToolOutcome {
  summary: string
  /** Sent back to Gemini Live as the FunctionResponse.response. */
  data: Record<string, unknown>
}

const WHAT_IF_PARAMS: WhatIfParam[] = ['rack_kw', 'liquid_fraction', 'dv_limit_pct', 'tx_loading_cap_pct']

function worstFindingFor(findings: Finding[], element: string, rule?: string): Finding | null {
  return (
    findings
      .filter((f) => f.element_id.toUpperCase() === element.toUpperCase() && (!rule || f.rule_id === rule))
      .sort((a, b) => statusRank(b.status) - statusRank(a.status))[0] ?? null
  )
}

export async function executeTool(name: string, args: Record<string, unknown>, ctx: ToolContext): Promise<ToolOutcome> {
  if (name === 'what_if') {
    const param = String(args.param) as WhatIfParam
    const value = Number(args.value)
    if (!WHAT_IF_PARAMS.includes(param) || !Number.isFinite(value)) return { summary: 'That what-if is not supported.', data: { error: 'unsupported' } }
    const r = await ctx.runWhatIf(param, value)
    if (!r) return { summary: 'The engine is unreachable for that what-if.', data: { error: 'unavailable' } }
    const fails = r.findings.filter((f) => f.status === 'fail')
    const summary = fails.length
      ? `${fails.length} checks fail: ${fails
          .slice(0, 4)
          .map((f) => `${f.rule_id} ${f.element_id} ${fmtQty(f.value ?? null, f.unit)} vs ${fmtQty(f.limit ?? null, f.unit)}`)
          .join('; ')}`
      : 'No check fails.'
    return {
      summary,
      data: {
        failing: fails.map((f) => ({ rule: f.rule_id, element: f.element_id, value: f.value, limit: f.limit, unit: f.unit })),
        impact_chain: r.impact_chain,
      },
    }
  }
  if (name === 'explain_finding') {
    const id = String(args.finding_id ?? '')
    const finding =
      ctx.view.findings.find((f) => f.id === id) ??
      worstFindingFor(ctx.view.findings, String(args.element_id ?? id.split(':')[1] ?? ''), (args.rule_id as string | undefined) ?? id.split(':')[0])
    if (!finding) return { summary: `No finding for ${id || String(args.element_id)} in this revision.`, data: { error: 'not_found' } }
    ctx.openFinding(finding.id)
    let text = fillPlaceholders(finding.explanation || finding.title, finding)
    if (ctx.view.revRef) {
      try {
        text = fillPlaceholders((await ctx.api.explain(ctx.view.revRef, finding.id)).text, finding)
      } catch {
        // engine explanation stands
      }
    }
    return { summary: text, data: { finding_id: finding.id, status: finding.status, value: finding.value, limit: finding.limit, unit: finding.unit, text } }
  }
  if (name === 'focus_element') {
    const id = String(args.element_id ?? '').toUpperCase()
    ctx.focus(id)
    return { summary: `Showing ${id}.`, data: { element_id: id } }
  }
  return { summary: `Unknown tool ${name}.`, data: { error: 'unknown_tool' } }
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement voice (Live + push-to-talk)**

`apps/web/src/workspace/copilot/voice/liveVoice.ts` (Firebase JS SDK 13 exports `AgentPlatformBackend`; `VertexAIBackend` no longer exists — ruling R1):
```ts
// Gemini Live via Firebase AI Logic (spec §6.5). Firebase JS SDK 13 renamed VertexAIBackend to
// AgentPlatformBackend (same Vertex backend); the Live model is served in us-central1 only.
import {
  AgentPlatformBackend,
  getAI,
  getLiveGenerativeModel,
  ResponseModality,
  Schema,
  startAudioConversation,
  type FunctionDeclaration,
} from 'firebase/ai'
import { app } from '@/firebase'
import type { StartLiveVoice } from '@/services/services'

export const LIVE_MODEL = 'gemini-live-2.5-flash-native-audio'

export const TOOL_DECLARATIONS: FunctionDeclaration[] = [
  {
    name: 'what_if',
    description:
      'Re-check the current revision with a changed load or limit (engine only, not saved). Returns failing checks and the impact chain.',
    parameters: Schema.object({
      properties: {
        param: Schema.enumString({ enum: ['rack_kw', 'liquid_fraction', 'dv_limit_pct', 'tx_loading_cap_pct'] }),
        value: Schema.number(),
      },
    }),
  },
  {
    name: 'explain_finding',
    description: 'Explain one checker finding, e.g. finding_id "R05:FA-01", or by element_id.',
    parameters: Schema.object({
      properties: { finding_id: Schema.string(), element_id: Schema.string() },
      optionalProperties: ['finding_id', 'element_id'],
    }),
  },
  {
    name: 'focus_element',
    description: 'Show an element (e.g. "FA-01", "H-Z2", "CDU-4") on the canvas.',
    parameters: Schema.object({ properties: { element_id: Schema.string() } }),
  },
]

const SYSTEM = [
  'You are the Kirchhoff co-pilot for an AI data-hall electrical and cooling design.',
  'Answer by calling a tool. Speak only numbers that a tool returned; never estimate or compute numbers yourself.',
  'Keep answers to one or two sentences.',
].join(' ')

export const startLiveVoice: StartLiveVoice = async ({ onToolCall }) => {
  const ai = getAI(app, { backend: new AgentPlatformBackend('us-central1') })
  const model = getLiveGenerativeModel(ai, {
    model: LIVE_MODEL,
    generationConfig: { responseModalities: [ResponseModality.AUDIO] },
    tools: [{ functionDeclarations: TOOL_DECLARATIONS }],
    systemInstruction: SYSTEM,
  })
  const session = await model.connect()
  const controller = await startAudioConversation(session, {
    functionCallingHandler: async (calls) => {
      const call = calls[0]
      const response = await onToolCall({ id: call.id, name: call.name, args: (call.args ?? {}) as Record<string, unknown> })
      return { id: call.id, name: call.name, response }
    },
  })
  return {
    stop: async () => {
      await controller.stop()
      await session.close()
    },
  }
}
```

`apps/web/src/workspace/copilot/voice/pushToTalk.ts`:
```ts
// Push-to-talk fallback (spec §6.5): record a clip, POST /api/voice, speak the answer with the
// browser's speech synthesis.

export interface Recording {
  stop(): Promise<Blob>
}

export function canRecord(): boolean {
  return typeof window !== 'undefined' && 'MediaRecorder' in window && !!navigator.mediaDevices?.getUserMedia
}

export async function startRecording(): Promise<Recording> {
  const stream = await navigator.mediaDevices.getUserMedia({ audio: true })
  const mime = MediaRecorder.isTypeSupported('audio/webm') ? 'audio/webm' : 'audio/ogg'
  const rec = new MediaRecorder(stream, { mimeType: mime })
  const chunks: Blob[] = []
  rec.ondataavailable = (e) => chunks.push(e.data)
  rec.start()
  return {
    stop: () =>
      new Promise<Blob>((resolve) => {
        rec.onstop = () => {
          stream.getTracks().forEach((t) => t.stop())
          resolve(new Blob(chunks, { type: mime }))
        }
        rec.stop()
      }),
  }
}

export function speak(text: string): void {
  if (typeof window === 'undefined' || !('speechSynthesis' in window)) return
  window.speechSynthesis.cancel()
  window.speechSynthesis.speak(new SpeechSynthesisUtterance(text))
}
```

`apps/web/src/workspace/copilot/voice/useVoice.ts`:
```ts
// Voice state machine: Gemini Live first; on failure (or when Live is not configured) switch
// automatically to push-to-talk via POST /api/voice (spec §6.5).
import { useEffect, useRef, useState } from 'react'
import type { VoiceResponse } from '@/services/apiClient'
import { useServices, type LiveVoiceCall, type LiveVoiceHandle } from '@/services/services'
import { canRecord, startRecording, type Recording } from './pushToTalk'

export type VoiceMode = 'off' | 'connecting' | 'live' | 'ptt' | 'recording' | 'thinking'

const LIVE_TIMEOUT_MS = 8000

export function useVoice(opts: {
  revisionRef: string | null
  onToolCall: (call: LiveVoiceCall) => Promise<Record<string, unknown>>
  onPushToTalk: (res: VoiceResponse) => void
}) {
  const { api, startLiveVoice } = useServices()
  const [mode, setMode] = useState<VoiceMode>('off')
  const [note, setNote] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const live = useRef<LiveVoiceHandle | null>(null)
  const rec = useRef<Recording | null>(null)
  const optsRef = useRef(opts)
  optsRef.current = opts

  useEffect(
    () => () => {
      void live.current?.stop().catch(() => undefined)
    },
    [],
  )

  const startLive = async () => {
    setNote(null)
    if (!startLiveVoice) {
      setMode('ptt')
      setNote('Live voice is not available here; hold the button to talk.')
      return
    }
    setMode('connecting')
    try {
      live.current = await Promise.race([
        startLiveVoice({ onToolCall: (c) => optsRef.current.onToolCall(c) }),
        new Promise<never>((_, reject) => setTimeout(() => reject(new Error('timeout')), LIVE_TIMEOUT_MS)),
      ])
      setMode('live')
    } catch {
      setMode('ptt')
      setNote('Gemini Live could not connect; switched to push-to-talk.')
    }
  }

  const stopLive = async () => {
    await live.current?.stop().catch(() => undefined)
    live.current = null
    setMode('off')
  }

  const pttDown = async () => {
    if (!canRecord()) {
      setNote('This browser cannot record audio; use the typed box.')
      return
    }
    try {
      rec.current = await startRecording()
      setMode('recording')
    } catch {
      setNote('Microphone permission was denied; use the typed box.')
    }
  }

  const pttUp = async () => {
    const r = rec.current
    if (!r) return
    rec.current = null
    setMode('thinking')
    try {
      const blob = await r.stop()
      if (!optsRef.current.revisionRef) throw new Error('no revision')
      optsRef.current.onPushToTalk(await api.voice(blob, optsRef.current.revisionRef))
    } catch {
      setNote('The voice service is unreachable; use the typed box or the chips.')
    } finally {
      setMode('ptt')
    }
  }

  return { mode, note, startLive, stopLive, pttDown, pttUp }
}
```

`apps/web/src/workspace/copilot/CoPilotPanel.tsx`:
```tsx
import { LoaderCircle, Mic, MicOff, Send } from 'lucide-react'
import { useRef, useState } from 'react'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { Input } from '@/components/ui/input'
import type { WhatIfResult } from '@/lib/types'
import { completeItem } from '@/onboarding/checklist/checklistStore'
import type { VoiceResponse } from '@/services/apiClient'
import { useServices, type LiveVoiceCall } from '@/services/services'
import { useUi } from '@/store/ui'
import { PlanGate } from '../gates/PlanGate'
import { RequirementsPanel } from '../RequirementsPanel'
import { useWorkspace } from '../WorkspaceContext'
import { INTENT_HELP, parseIntent } from './intent'
import { executeTool, type ToolContext } from './tools'
import { useWhatIf } from './useWhatIf'
import { speak } from './voice/pushToTalk'
import { useVoice } from './voice/useVoice'
import { WhatIfPanel } from './WhatIfPanel'

interface LogEntry {
  id: number
  who: 'you' | 'co-pilot'
  via: 'typed' | 'voice'
  text: string
}

export function CoPilotPanel() {
  const view = useWorkspace()
  const { api } = useServices()
  const { run: runWhatIf } = useWhatIf()
  const focus = useUi((s) => s.focus)
  const openFinding = useUi((s) => s.openFinding)
  const setWhatIf = useUi((s) => s.setWhatIf)
  const [text, setText] = useState('')
  const [log, setLog] = useState<LogEntry[]>([])
  const seq = useRef(0)
  const ctx: ToolContext = { view, api, runWhatIf, focus, openFinding }
  const ctxRef = useRef(ctx)
  ctxRef.current = ctx

  const add = (who: LogEntry['who'], via: LogEntry['via'], t: string) =>
    setLog((l) => [...l.slice(-7), { id: ++seq.current, who, via, text: t }])

  const ask = async (q: string) => {
    add('you', 'typed', q)
    completeItem('ask_copilot')
    const intent = parseIntent(q)
    if (!intent) {
      add('co-pilot', 'typed', INTENT_HELP)
      return
    }
    const args =
      intent.tool === 'what_if'
        ? { param: intent.param, value: intent.value }
        : intent.tool === 'explain_finding'
          ? { element_id: intent.element, rule_id: intent.rule }
          : { element_id: intent.element }
    const out = await executeTool(intent.tool, args, ctxRef.current)
    add('co-pilot', 'typed', out.summary)
  }

  const onToolCall = async (call: LiveVoiceCall) => {
    completeItem('ask_copilot')
    add('you', 'voice', `(voice) ${call.name.replace('_', ' ')}`)
    const out = await executeTool(call.name, call.args, ctxRef.current)
    add('co-pilot', 'voice', out.summary)
    return out.data
  }

  const onPushToTalk = (res: VoiceResponse) => {
    completeItem('ask_copilot')
    add('you', 'voice', res.transcript)
    for (const t of res.tool_results) {
      if (t.name === 'what_if' && Array.isArray((t.result as Partial<WhatIfResult>).impact_chain))
        setWhatIf({ result: t.result as unknown as WhatIfResult, origin: 'engine' })
      if (t.name === 'focus_element') focus(String(t.args.element_id ?? t.result.element_id ?? ''))
      if (t.name === 'explain_finding' && t.args.finding_id) openFinding(String(t.args.finding_id))
    }
    add('co-pilot', 'voice', res.answer)
    speak(res.answer)
  }

  const voice = useVoice({ revisionRef: view.revRef, onToolCall, onPushToTalk })
  const pttMode = voice.mode === 'ptt' || voice.mode === 'recording' || voice.mode === 'thinking'

  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col">
      <section aria-label="Ask the co-pilot" className="flex flex-col gap-2 border-b p-3">
        <h3 className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-500">
          <span aria-hidden className="size-2 rounded-full bg-[linear-gradient(135deg,#e879f9,#38bdf8)]" /> Co-pilot
        </h3>
        <form
          className="flex gap-1.5"
          onSubmit={(e) => {
            e.preventDefault()
            if (text.trim()) void ask(text.trim())
            setText('')
          }}
        >
          <Input aria-label="Ask the co-pilot" placeholder="Ask, or “what if racks go to 200 kW”" value={text} onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)} className="h-8 text-xs" />
          <Button size="icon-sm" type="submit" aria-label="Send">
            <Send aria-hidden />
          </Button>
        </form>
        <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
          {voice.mode === 'off' && (
            <Button size="xs" variant="outline" onClick={() => void voice.startLive()}>
              <Mic aria-hidden /> Start voice
            </Button>
          )}
          {voice.mode === 'connecting' && (
            <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-zinc-300">
              <LoaderCircle aria-hidden className="size-3.5 animate-spin" /> Connecting to Gemini Live…
            </span>
          )}
          {voice.mode === 'live' && (
            <>
              <span className="inline-flex items-center gap-1 text-xs text-fuchsia-200">
                <Mic aria-hidden className="size-3.5 animate-pulse motion-reduce:animate-none" /> Listening (Gemini Live)
              </span>
              <Button size="xs" variant="ghost" onClick={() => void voice.stopLive()}>
                <MicOff aria-hidden /> Stop
              </Button>
            </>
          )}
          {pttMode && (
            <Button
              size="xs"
              variant={voice.mode === 'recording' ? 'default' : 'outline'}
              disabled={voice.mode === 'thinking'}
              onPointerDown={() => void voice.pttDown()}
              onPointerUp={() => void voice.pttUp()}
              onKeyDown={(e) => (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter') && !e.repeat && void voice.pttDown()}
              onKeyUp={(e) => (e.key === ' ' || e.key === 'Enter') && void voice.pttUp()}
            >
              {voice.mode === 'thinking' ? <LoaderCircle aria-hidden className="animate-spin" /> : <Mic aria-hidden />}
              {voice.mode === 'recording' ? 'Release to send' : 'Hold to talk'}
            </Button>
          )}
        </div>
        {voice.note && <p className="text-xs text-zinc-400">{voice.note}</p>}
        {log.length > 0 && (
          <ol aria-label="Conversation" className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-xs" aria-live="polite">
            {log.map((e) => (
              <li key={e.id} className={e.who === 'you' ? 'text-zinc-400' : 'text-zinc-100'}>
                <span className="font-medium">{e.who === 'you' ? 'You' : 'Co-pilot'}:</span> {e.text}
              </li>
            ))}
          </ol>
        )}
      </section>
      <WhatIfPanel />
      {view.run && (view.run.plan?.length ?? 0) > 0 && !(view.source === 'live' && view.run.status === 'at_gate' && view.run.gate === 'plan') && (
        <div className="border-b p-3">
          <PlanGate run={view.run} interactive={false} />
        </div>
      )}
      <RequirementsPanel />
    </div>
  )
}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement**

`apps/web/src/assetpack/assetResult.ts`:
```ts
// Asset-pack results (spec §10). Static bundles carry the engine's result dict in the last
// tool_result event whose tool is evaluate_grid / evaluate_solar (ruling R5).
import { toolPayload } from '@/lib/events'
import type { AssetResult, Bundle } from '@/lib/types'

export const ASSET_TOOLS = ['evaluate_grid', 'evaluate_solar']

export function assetResultFrom(b: Bundle): AssetResult | null {
  for (const e of [...b.events].reverse()) {
    const t = toolPayload(e)
    if (e.kind === 'tool_result' && t && ASSET_TOOLS.includes(t.tool) && Array.isArray(t.summary.options)) {
      return t.summary as unknown as AssetResult
    }
  }
  return null
}

const NAME_KEYS = ['id', 'name', 'label']

export function optionName(o: Record<string, unknown>): string {
  return String(o.name ?? o.label ?? o.id ?? '')
}

export function optionId(o: Record<string, unknown>): string {
  return String(o.id ?? o.name ?? '')
}

/** Union of the option dicts' keys (engine order), excluding identity keys. */
export function optionColumns(options: Record<string, unknown>[]): string[] {
  const cols: string[] = []
  for (const o of options) for (const k of Object.keys(o)) if (!NAME_KEYS.includes(k) && !cols.includes(k)) cols.push(k)
  return cols
}

type XY = [number, number]
export interface Shapes {
  lines: { id: string; points: XY[] }[]
  polygons: { id: string; points: XY[] }[]
  rects: { id: string; r: [number, number, number, number] }[]
  points: { id: string; p: XY }[]
}

const isNum = (v: unknown): v is number => typeof v === 'number' && Number.isFinite(v)
const isXY = (v: unknown): v is XY => Array.isArray(v) && v.length === 2 && v.every(isNum)
const isRect = (v: unknown): v is [number, number, number, number] => Array.isArray(v) && v.length === 4 && v.every(isNum)
const CLOSED = /polygon|field|boundary|site|area|parcel/i

/** Generic geometry reader: [x,y] → point; [[x,y],…] → line (closed if the key names an area); [[x0,y0,x1,y1],…] → rects. */
export function geometryShapes(geometry: Record<string, unknown>): Shapes {
  const s: Shapes = { lines: [], polygons: [], rects: [], points: [] }
  for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(geometry)) {
    if (isXY(v)) s.points.push({ id: k, p: v })
    else if (Array.isArray(v) && v.length && v.every(isXY)) (CLOSED.test(k) ? s.polygons : s.lines).push({ id: k, points: v as XY[] })
    else if (Array.isArray(v) && v.length && v.every(isRect)) v.forEach((r, i) => s.rects.push({ id: `${k}-${i}`, r: r as [number, number, number, number] }))
  }
  return s
}

export function shapeBounds(s: Shapes): [number, number, number, number] | null {
  const xs: number[] = []
  const ys: number[] = []
  const add = ([x, y]: XY) => {
    xs.push(x)
    ys.push(y)
  }
  s.lines.forEach((l) => l.points.forEach(add))
  s.polygons.forEach((l) => l.points.forEach(add))
  s.points.forEach((p) => add(p.p))
  s.rects.forEach(({ r }) => {
    add([r[0], r[1]])
    add([r[2], r[3]])
  })
  if (!xs.length) return null
  return [Math.min(...xs), Math.min(...ys), Math.max(...xs), Math.max(...ys)]
}
```

`apps/web/src/assetpack/AssetPackPage.tsx`:
```tsx
// Grid and Solar asset-pack screens (spec §10, storyboard 2:18–2:28). Thin: same shell, checker
// findings and Asset agent narration; results from the static bundle or POST /api/asset/{pack}.
import { cn } from 'cn'
import { RotateCcw, SunMedium, UtilityPole } from 'lucide-react'
import { useState } from 'react'
import { Link } from 'react-router'
import { AgentDot, EmptyState, IllustrativeLabel, Num } from '@/components/Labels'
import { StatusBadge } from '@/components/StatusBadge'
import { Button } from '@/components/ui/button'
import { useProjectData } from '@/data/useProjectData'
import { useWorldProject } from '@/data/useWorld'
import { describeKey, fillPlaceholders, fmtNum, fmtQty, fmtUsd } from '@/lib/format'
import type { AssetResult } from '@/lib/types'
import { useServices } from '@/services/services'
import { assetResultFrom, geometryShapes, optionColumns, optionId, optionName, shapeBounds } from './assetResult'

function cell(key: string, v: unknown): string {
  if (typeof v !== 'number') return v === null || v === undefined ? '—' : String(v)
  const d = describeKey(key)
  return d.usd ? fmtUsd(v) : fmtNum(v)
}

function header(key: string): string {
  const d = describeKey(key)
  return d.unit ? `${d.label} (${d.unit})` : d.usd ? `${d.label} (USD)` : d.label
}

function GeometryView({ geometry }: { geometry: Record<string, unknown> }) {
  const s = geometryShapes(geometry)
  const b = shapeBounds(s)
  if (!b) return null
  const pad = Math.max(b[2] - b[0], b[3] - b[1]) * 0.05 || 1
  const [x0, y0, w, h] = [b[0] - pad, b[1] - pad, b[2] - b[0] + 2 * pad, b[3] - b[1] + 2 * pad]
  // y is north-up in engine coordinates; flip for SVG.
  const pt = ([x, y]: [number, number]) => `${x},${y0 + h - (y - y0)}`
  const stroke = Math.max(w, h) / 300
  return (
    <svg role="img" aria-label="Site geometry" viewBox={`${x0} ${y0} ${w} ${h}`} className="h-64 w-full rounded border bg-zinc-950">
      {s.polygons.map((p) => (
        <polygon key={p.id} points={p.points.map(pt).join(' ')} fill="rgba(45,212,191,0.08)" stroke="#2dd4bf" strokeWidth={stroke} />
      ))}
      {s.rects.map(({ id, r }) => (
        <rect key={id} x={r[0]} y={y0 + h - (r[3] - y0)} width={r[2] - r[0]} height={r[3] - r[1]} fill="#1e3a5f" stroke="#60a5fa" strokeWidth={stroke / 2} />
      ))}
      {s.lines.map((l) => (
        <polyline key={l.id} points={l.points.map(pt).join(' ')} fill="none" stroke="#fbbf24" strokeWidth={stroke * 1.5} />
      ))}
      {s.points.map((p) => (
        <g key={p.id}>
          <circle cx={p.p[0]} cy={y0 + h - (p.p[1] - y0)} r={stroke * 4} fill="#e4e4e7" />
          <text x={p.p[0]} y={y0 + h - (p.p[1] - y0) - stroke * 6} fontSize={stroke * 10} fill="#a1a1aa" textAnchor="middle">
            {p.id}
          </text>
        </g>
      ))}
    </svg>
  )
}

export function AssetPackView({ pack, result, onRerun, rerunning, narration }: { pack: 'grid' | 'solar'; result: AssetResult; onRerun: () => void; rerunning: boolean; narration: string | null }) {
  const cols = optionColumns(result.options)
  return (
    <div className="flex flex-col gap-5">
      <section aria-label="Options" className="flex flex-col gap-2">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
          <h2 className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">{pack === 'grid' ? 'Conductor options' : 'String and layout options'}</h2>
          <Button size="xs" variant="outline" className="ml-auto" onClick={onRerun} disabled={rerunning}>
            <RotateCcw aria-hidden /> Re-run with the engine
          </Button>
        </div>
        <table className="w-full text-xs" aria-label="Asset options">
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th className="border-b px-2 py-1 text-left text-zinc-400">Option</th>
              {cols.map((c) => (
                <th key={c} className="border-b px-2 py-1 text-left font-medium text-zinc-400">
                  {header(c)}
                </th>
              ))}
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {result.options.map((o) => {
              const rec = optionId(o) === result.recommended || optionName(o) === result.recommended
              return (
                <tr key={optionId(o)} className={cn(rec && 'bg-teal-500/10')}>
                  <td className="border-b border-zinc-800 px-2 py-1 text-zinc-100">
                    {optionName(o)}
                    {rec && <span className="ml-2 rounded border border-teal-400/60 px-1 text-[10px] text-teal-200">Recommended</span>}
                  </td>
                  {cols.map((c) => (
                    <td key={c} className="border-b border-zinc-800 px-2 py-1 font-mono tabular-nums">
                      {cell(c, o[c])}
                    </td>
                  ))}
                </tr>
              )
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
        {narration && (
          <p className="flex items-center gap-2 text-xs text-zinc-300">
            <AgentDot agent="asset" /> {narration}
          </p>
        )}
      </section>
      <section aria-label="Checks" className="flex flex-col gap-1">
        <h2 className="text-xs font-medium uppercase tracking-wide text-zinc-400">Checker</h2>
        <ul className="flex flex-col gap-1 text-sm">
          {result.findings.map((f) => (
            <li key={f.id} className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
              <StatusBadge status={f.status} />
              <span className="font-mono text-zinc-200">
                {f.rule_id} · {f.element_id}
              </span>
              <span className="text-zinc-400">{f.title}</span>
              {typeof f.value === 'number' && (
                <span className="text-xs text-zinc-400">
                  <Num>{fmtQty(f.value, f.unit)}</Num> vs <Num>{fmtQty(f.limit ?? null, f.unit)}</Num>
                </span>
              )}
              {f.explanation && <span className="w-full pl-6 text-xs text-zinc-500">{fillPlaceholders(f.explanation, f)}</span>}
            </li>
          ))}
        </ul>
      </section>
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-4 lg:grid-cols-[2fr_1fr]">
        <section aria-label="Geometry">
          <GeometryView geometry={result.geometry} />
        </section>
        {result.sld && (
          <section aria-label="Mini SLD" className="flex flex-col items-start gap-1 text-xs">
            {result.sld.nodes.map((n, i) => {
              const edge = result.sld!.edges.find((e) => e.dst === n.id)
              return (
                <div key={n.id} className="flex flex-col items-start">
                  {i > 0 && (
                    <span className="ml-4 border-l border-zinc-600 py-1 pl-2 font-mono text-[10px] text-zinc-500">{edge?.label ?? ''}</span>
                  )}
                  <span className="rounded border bg-card px-2 py-1">
                    <span className="font-mono text-zinc-100">{n.id}</span> <span className="text-zinc-400">{n.label}</span>
                  </span>
                </div>
              )
            })}
          </section>
        )}
      </div>
      <IllustrativeLabel />
    </div>
  )
}

export function AssetPackPage({ pack, pid }: { pack: 'grid' | 'solar'; pid: string }) {
  const project = useWorldProject(pid)
  const data = useProjectData(pid, { project })
  const { api } = useServices()
  const [override, setOverride] = useState<AssetResult | null>(null)
  const [busy, setBusy] = useState(false)
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null)
  const Icon = pack === 'grid' ? UtilityPole : SunMedium
  const result = override ?? (data.bundle ? assetResultFrom(data.bundle) : null)
  const narration = data.bundle ? ([...data.bundle.events].reverse().find((e) => e.agent === 'asset' && e.kind === 'message')?.title ?? null) : null

  const rerun = async () => {
    setBusy(true)
    setError(null)
    try {
      setOverride(await api.asset(pack))
    } catch {
      setError('The engine is unreachable; showing the recorded result.')
    } finally {
      setBusy(false)
    }
  }

  return (
    <div className="mx-auto flex h-full max-w-6xl flex-col gap-4 overflow-auto p-6">
      <header className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
        <Link to="/app" className="text-sm text-zinc-400 hover:text-zinc-100">
          Projects
        </Link>
        <span className="text-zinc-600">/</span>
        <Icon aria-hidden className="size-4 text-teal-300" />
        <h1 className="text-lg font-semibold">{project?.name ?? data.bundle?.project.name ?? pid}</h1>
        <span className="rounded bg-zinc-800 px-1.5 py-0.5 text-[11px] text-zinc-300">{pack === 'grid' ? 'Grid asset pack' : 'Solar asset pack'}</span>
      </header>
      {error && (
        <p role="alert" className="text-xs text-amber-200">
          {error}
        </p>
      )}
      {result ? (
        <AssetPackView pack={pack} result={result} onRerun={() => void rerun()} rerunning={busy} narration={narration} />
      ) : data.status === 'loading' ? (
        <EmptyState title="Loading…" />
      ) : (
        <EmptyState
          title="No recorded result for this asset pack"
          action={
            <Button size="sm" onClick={() => void rerun()} disabled={busy}>
              Run the {pack} engine
            </Button>
          }
        >
          {data.error ?? 'The static bundle has no evaluate_grid/evaluate_solar result.'}
        </EmptyState>
      )}
    </div>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { Route, Routes } from 'react-router'
import { AssetPackPage } from './assetpack/AssetPackPage'
import { Dashboard } from './pages/Dashboard'
import { Landing } from './pages/Landing'
import { IntegrationsPage } from './pages/Integrations'
import { NotFound } from './pages/NotFound'
import { AppShell } from './shell/AppShell'
import { ReviewPage } from './workspace/review/ReviewPage'
import { WorkspacePage } from './workspace/WorkspacePage'

export default function App() {
  return (
    <Routes>
      <Route path="/" element={<Landing />} />
      <Route path="/app" element={<AppShell />}>
        <Route index element={<Dashboard />} />
        <Route path="p/:pid" element={<WorkspacePage />} />
        <Route path="p/:pid/review/:rev" element={<ReviewPage />} />
        <Route path="p/grid-132kv" element={<AssetPackPage pack="grid" pid="grid-132kv" />} />
        <Route path="p/solar-5mwp" element={<AssetPackPage pack="solar" pid="solar-5mwp" />} />
        <Route path="integrations" element={<IntegrationsPage />} />
      </Route>
      <Route path="*" element={<NotFound />} />
    </Routes>
  )
}
```

```tsx
import { screen } from '@testing-library/react'
import { useWorldStore } from '@/data/useWorld'
import { ErrorBoundary } from '@/components/ErrorBoundary'
import { renderWithProviders } from '@/test/render'
import App from './App'

beforeEach(() => {
  localStorage.setItem('kirchhoff.welcomed', 'true')
  useWorldStore.setState({ world: null, error: null, loading: false })
})

test('lazy routes resolve: review page and integrations', async () => {
  const { unmount } = renderWithProviders(<App />, { route: '/app/p/hall-a/review/r2' })
  expect(await screen.findByRole('figure', { name: 'Approval stamp' })).toBeInTheDocument()
  unmount()
  renderWithProviders(<App />, { route: '/app/integrations' })
  expect(await screen.findByRole('heading', { name: 'Integrations' })).toBeInTheDocument()
})

test('presentation mode hides dev affordances: checklist and help carry data-dev', async () => {
  renderWithProviders(<App />, { route: '/app' })
  await screen.findByText('Deccan AI Campus · Hall A')
  expect(screen.getByRole('complementary', { name: 'Getting started' })).toHaveAttribute('data-dev')
  expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Help and shortcuts' })).toHaveAttribute('data-dev')
  expect(screen.getByRole('note')).not.toHaveAttribute('data-dev') // the fictional-data banner always shows
})

test('a crashing view shows a recoverable error instead of a blank page', () => {
  const spy = vi.spyOn(console, 'error').mockImplementation(() => {})
  const Boom = () => {
    throw new Error('boom')
  }
  renderWithProviders(
    <ErrorBoundary resetKey="/x">
      <Boom />
    </ErrorBoundary>,
  )
  expect(screen.getByText('This view hit an error')).toBeInTheDocument()
  spy.mockRestore()
})
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implement**

`apps/web/src/components/ErrorBoundary.tsx`:
```tsx
import { Component, type ErrorInfo, type ReactNode } from 'react'
import { EmptyState } from './Labels'
import { Button } from './ui/button'

/** Keeps one broken view from blanking the demo; resets when `resetKey` (the route) changes. */
export class ErrorBoundary extends Component<{ resetKey: string; children: ReactNode }, { error: Error | null; key: string }> {
  state = { error: null as Error | null, key: this.props.resetKey }

  static getDerivedStateFromError(error: Error) {
    return { error }
  }

  static getDerivedStateFromProps(props: { resetKey: string }, state: { error: Error | null; key: string }) {
    return props.resetKey !== state.key ? { error: null, key: props.resetKey } : null
  }

  componentDidCatch(error: Error, info: ErrorInfo) {
    console.error('[kirchhoff] view crashed', error, info.componentStack)
  }

  render() {
    if (!this.state.error) return this.props.children
    return (
      <EmptyState
        title="This view hit an error"
        action={
          <Button size="sm" variant="outline" onClick={() => window.location.reload()}>
            Reload
          </Button>
        }
      >
        The rest of the demo still works. Try the projects page or reload.
      </EmptyState>
    )
  }
}
```

`apps/web/src/App.tsx` (replace):
```tsx
import { lazy, Suspense } from 'react'
import { Route, Routes, useLocation } from 'react-router'
import { ErrorBoundary } from './components/ErrorBoundary'
import { EmptyState } from './components/Labels'
import { Dashboard } from './pages/Dashboard'
import { Landing } from './pages/Landing'
import { NotFound } from './pages/NotFound'
import { AppShell } from './shell/AppShell'

// Heavy screens (React Flow + ELK, TanStack, react-pdf, three.js) load on demand.
const WorkspacePage = lazy(() => import('./workspace/WorkspacePage').then((m) => ({ default: m.WorkspacePage })))
const ReviewPage = lazy(() => import('./workspace/review/ReviewPage').then((m) => ({ default: m.ReviewPage })))
const IntegrationsPage = lazy(() => import('./pages/Integrations').then((m) => ({ default: m.IntegrationsPage })))
const AssetPackPage = lazy(() => import('./assetpack/AssetPackPage').then((m) => ({ default: m.AssetPackPage })))

export default function App() {
  const { pathname } = useLocation()
  return (
    <ErrorBoundary resetKey={pathname}>
      <Suspense fallback={<EmptyState title="Loading…" />}>
        <Routes>
          <Route path="/" element={<Landing />} />
          <Route path="/app" element={<AppShell />}>
            <Route index element={<Dashboard />} />
            <Route path="p/:pid" element={<WorkspacePage />} />
            <Route path="p/:pid/review/:rev" element={<ReviewPage />} />
            <Route path="p/grid-132kv" element={<AssetPackPage pack="grid" pid="grid-132kv" />} />
            <Route path="p/solar-5mwp" element={<AssetPackPage pack="solar" pid="solar-5mwp" />} />
            <Route path="integrations" element={<IntegrationsPage />} />
          </Route>
          <Route path="*" element={<NotFound />} />
        </Routes>
      </Suspense>
    </ErrorBoundary>
  )
}
```

```
])
```

## Cross-workstream requirements this plan depends on

**WS2 (agents + API) must:**
1. Write Firestore documents as `model_dump()` of the contracts (snake_case), timestamps as unix seconds; lease as `{owner, attempt, heartbeat_at}` (R2).
2. Make every `artifact` event's `payload.ref` either a Firestore path under `workspaces/{uid}/…` (brief, e.g. `workspaces/{uid}/runs/{rid}/artifacts/brief` holding a `DesignBrief`) or a URL/storage path; revision artifacts' ref must end in the revision id (R7). Emit the revision artifact before that revision's `finding` events and add `rev` to each `finding` payload (R8).
3. Keep `RunRecord.options`, `plan`, `open_questions`, `gate`, `selected_option` current on `workspaces/{uid}/runs/{rid}` (the UI reads gates from the run doc).
4. `POST /api/voice`: accept multipart `audio` + `revision_ref`; return `tool_results: [{name, args, result}]` (what_if `result` = a `WhatIfResult`) (R9). Use the same three tool schemas as `TOOL_DECLARATIONS`.
5. Review gate writes the Approval with `approver_name: "You (Demo Reviewer)"`, `role: "Reviewer"` (R10). Return HTTP 429 with a `detail` string when the live-run cap or per-visitor limit is hit.
6. Use `Citation.doc_id` values equal to the PDF file stems in `scenarios/hall_a/out/` (e.g. `tender-hall-a`) (R6); `revision_ref` formats `ws:{rev}` / `seed:{pid}@{rev}` (R17).
7. Asset agent: emit the engine result dict as a `tool_result` event with `payload.tool = "evaluate_grid" | "evaluate_solar"` and `payload.summary = {options, recommended, findings, geometry, sld?}`; `POST /api/asset/{pack}` returns the same dict (R5).

**WS4 (enterprise + demo world + infra) must:**
1. Generate `public/demo/world.json` and `public/demo/integrations.json` exactly in the `World` / `IntegrationsSnapshot` shapes of `src/lib/types.ts` (R13), timestamps in unix seconds; documents use public URLs (e.g. `/demo/docs/tender-hall-a.pdf`).
2. Generate `public/demo/projects/{pid}.json` for every world project (Hall B may be omitted: the UI shows the start panel); Hall A's bundle must include the precomputed `what_ifs` for the five chips (racks 200 and 150 kW, liquid fraction 0.9, ΔV limit 1.5 %, transformer loading cap 35 %) with `revision_ref = "seed:hall-a@{signed rev}"`; grid/solar bundles carry the asset `tool_result` event (R5).
3. Configure the Storage bucket CORS to allow `GET` from https://kirchhoff.web.app so `/api/files/{path}` redirects to signed URLs can be fetched as blobs (Docs exports, large `model_url`s).
4. Playwright selectors can rely on: `[data-tour=…]` anchors, `table[aria-label="Compliance matrix"]`, matrix cell buttons named `"{rule} {element}: {Status}"`, `figure[aria-label="Approval stamp"]`, `button "Start live run"`, `button "Approve and sign as You (Demo Reviewer)"`, `region "Latest handoff"`.

**WS1 (engine):** keep finding ids `"{rule}:{element}"`; for R11 put the crossed electrical element id in a string `inputs` value (e.g. `inputs.over = "FB-03"`) so the 3D label anchors at the crossing; `WhatIfResult.findings` holds failing findings only (the UI shows them all).
